Abstract: Partial shape matching is a crucial yet underexplored problem in 3D vision, with significant relevance to real-world scenarios where shapes are often only partially observed. Existing feature descriptors face difficulties in this setting, as traditional representations either struggle with the boundaries of partial shapes or heavily depend on the shape's spatial position. While existing approaches have employed DINO features for partial shape matching, these features are not inherently suited for handling partial observations. In this work, we propose a method to refine DINO features using LoRA-based self-supervised learning, enabling the generation of feature descriptors that are robust to partiality. Our features substantially improve performance on partial shape matching compared to traditional or vision foundation features. Additionally, when integrated into existing partial shape matching pipelines, we achieve state-of-the-art results on partial shape matching and left-right prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [74] [From Adults to Children: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Chest X-Ray Classification under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21809)
*Sara Inácio,Hugo Proença,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究调查了基础模型在从成人到儿科胸部X光分类的任务中的泛化能力，并表明所有模型在迁移至儿科数据时表现显著下降。增加适配深度并未改善跨人群泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基础模型在成人和儿科数据之间的泛化能力，特别是在缺乏统一基准的情况下填补此知识空白。

Method: 将两个数据集（VinDr-CXR 和 PediCXR）整合为五分类任务，使用六种基础模型和两种CNN基准模型进行评估。所有模型仅使用成人数据进行适配，并通过线性探针、部分微调和全微调方式进行测试。

Result: 所有模型在成人到儿科数据迁移时表现大幅下降，性能降低超过10个百分点。在成人测试集上宏观AUROC值接近92%，在儿科领域则降至约78%。增加适配深度对跨人群泛化的提升效果不明显。

Conclusion: 该基准研究展示了基础模型在儿科数据上的泛化能力不足，强调了进一步研究以改善其跨人群性能的重要性。

Abstract: Foundation models have shown significant potential across diverse fields and have been applied to medical imaging, but their robustness across patient populations remains unclear. In this work, we investigate foundation models' ability to generalize from adult to pediatric chest X-ray classification. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark to compare multiple foundation models under a common adult-to-pediatric evaluation setting while also analyzing the effect of adaptation depth. We harmonize VinDr-CXR and PediCXR into a five-label classification task and evaluate six foundation models and two CNN baselines. All models are adapted using adult data only and tested in both the in-domain and pediatric domains using linear probing, partial fine-tuning, and full fine-tuning. The benchmark reveals a clear and consistent trend: all models suffer a substantial performance degradation when transferred from adult to pediatric data, with drops exceeding 10 percentage points. While Macro-AUROC values reach approximately 92% on the adult test set, performance decreases to around 78% on the pediatric domain. Another important finding is that increasing the depth of adaptation does not significantly improve cross-population generalization. Moreover, strong in-domain performance does not translate into better performance on pediatric data.

</details>


### [75] [Temporal Periodic Image Registration with Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21845)
*Mathias Lowes,Kristine Aavild Sørensen,Maxime Sermesant,Rasmus R. Paulsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Temporal-IDIR的方法，用于实现时间图像注册，通过优化单一的隐神经表示来同时建模时间图像序列中的变形，引入了时间一致损失来正则化变形预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引入Temporal-IDIR方法的原因是现有方法在处理时间图像注册时存在一定的局限性，尤其是在建模连续时间帧之间的平滑变换和自正则化方面。

Method: Temporal-IDIR方法首先通过优化单一的隐神经表示来从单一网络中预测所有时间帧的变形，然后引入一种时间一致性损失函数来惩罚直接时空变性和通过中间帧变换之间的差异。

Result: 在DIR-LAB数据集上，该方法实现了1.03毫米的目标注册误差，优于其他基于隐神经表示的注册方法。此外，该框架还支持通过估计给定时间帧之间的变形来实现时间帧之间的平滑插值。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种创新的时间图像注册方法，展示了在临床和医学成像中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have recently gained popularity for their ability to model functions using simple and lightweight networks. These methods have also proven effective in deformable image registration. In this work, we extend INRs for deformable image registration to the domain of temporal image registration, focusing on periodic temporal image sequences. Our approach, Temporal-IDIR, optimizes a single INR to model deformations across all frames in a temporal image sequence simultaneously, allowing for self-regularization through its own deformation predictions. To achieve this, we introduce a temporal consistency loss that penalizes discrepancies between direct source-to-target transformations and those traversing intermediate frames. We evaluate our framework on the DIR-LAB dataset, using the target registration error (TRE) between annotated and moved landmarks as the metric. Here, we achieve a TRE of 1.03 mm, outperforming other INR-based registration methods. Additionally, our framework supports smooth interpolation between time frames by estimating deformations between the given input frames. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/MMLowes/Temporal_INR.

</details>


### [76] [Seeing Both the Forest and the Trees: Reusing Holistic 3D Priors for Part-Decomposed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21850)
*Yiqun Zhao,Binbin Huang,Haobin Duan,Zibo Zhao,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为STRUCTPARTS的新颖框架，通过双框架坐标交织机制，在保持部分详细度和整体结构一致性的同时，实现了零件分解生成。它可以以较低的训练成本达到或超越现有方法的效果，并支持零件网格细化和部件级编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，基于图像条件的3D生成模型能够同时捕捉几何细节和整体结构，但其输出为单一融合模型。本文旨在探讨部分分解生成模型如何继承整体先验模型的两种重要属性，以改进3D资产生成的细节和结构一致性。

Method: 本文提出了STRUCTPARTS框架，其核心技术创新在于双框架坐标交织机制。每个形状潜在变量被解释为在局部部件参考框架和全局对象参考框架下的两种视图，并通过一个全局局部交织转换器同时利用先验模型在部件尺度上的几何细节和在整体对象尺度上的结构一致性。此外，该框架使用了一种分段网格骨架作为结构输入，它包括一个粗略网格和一个保持在先验模型的原生3D表示中的面级分割掩码。

Result: 在PartObjaverse-Tiny数据集上，STRUCTPARTS在部分分解模型中达到了或超越了先前模型的效果，且仅需少量微调。此外，该方法自然支持零件网格细化和部件级编辑。

Conclusion: 本文提出的structured-parts框架是3D生成模型中的一个创新贡献，证明了通过考虑局部和整体参考框架，可以同时实现细节和结构一致性的生成。

Abstract: Generating 3D assets with explicit parts requires two properties at once: geometric detail inside each part and structural coherence across the whole object. Recent image-conditioned 3D generation models adopt a structured 3D representation that anchors tokens to explicit spatial positions, capturing both properties, but their output remains a single fused mesh. We ask how a part-decomposed generation model can inherit both properties of these holistic priors, and answer with STRUCTPARTS, a framework whose core technical contribution is a dual-frame coordinate interleaving mechanism: each shape latent is interpreted under both a part-local and an object-global reference frame, and processed through a global-local inter-leaved transformer that simultaneously reuses the prior's geometric detail at the part scale and its structural coherence at the whole-object scale. As its structural input, STRUCTPARTS uses a segmented mesh scaffold, a coarse mesh paired with a face-level segmentation mask kept entirely within the prior's native 3D representation. With only lightweight finetuning, both intra-part detail and inter-part coherence emerge from the same pretrained weights. On PartObjaverse-Tiny, STRUCTPARTS matches or surpasses prior part-decomposed models at a fraction of their training cost, while naturally supporting part-mesh refinement and part-level editing.

</details>


### [77] [Anvil: Scaling Embodied Spatial Intelligence through Knowledge Recirculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21882)
*Weilin Ruan,Bohan Zhou,Jinghao Li,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Peiran Dong,Yingjie Xu,Leyi Wu,Haojian Huang,Binghui Xie,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Anvil 提供了一个统一框架，包含数据生成、训练和基准测试，为实体间的关系推理和动作相关表达提供支持。该框架通过多阶段一致性验证、故障驱动的数据回收、场景对齐监督和差异化的评估方法，改进了模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联合训练方法可能会将不同阶段所需的知识混淆起来。因此，需要一个新的框架来统一数据生成、训练和评估，从而更好地支持实体间的关系推理和动作相关表达。

Method: Anvil 包含三个主要组件：Anvil-Gen、Anvil-Ben 和 Anvil-MOPD。Anvil-Gen 是一个混合数据生成器，结合了形式特定的生成、跨阶段一致性验证和故障驱动的数据回收。Anvil-Ben 提供场景对齐的监督和场景关联的评估。Anvil-MOPD 是一个学生，从专门的教师那里吸收异质空间知识并通过蒸馏进行知识整合。

Result: 采用 Anvil-MOPD 模型在七个公开基准测试上获得了显著提高，分别比 Qwen3.5-9B 和 RynnBrain-8B 高 9.29 和 11.03 个百分点，同时在 VABench-Point 上达到了 80.67% 的性能。

Conclusion: Anvil 作为一个统一框架，能够有效解决联合训练中不同阶段知识混淆的问题，实现更复杂的实体间关系推理和动作相关表达能力。

Abstract: Embodied spatial intelligence requires more than visual recognition: models must localize entities, reason about their relations, and express this knowledge in action-relevant forms. Yet joint training can conflate the knowledge needed at different stages because supervision is fragmented across incompatible geometric and semantic outputs. We introduce Anvil, a unified framework for data generation, training, and benchmarking for embodied spatial intelligence. (1) Anvil-Gen is a heterogeneous data flywheel that combines form-specific generation, cross-stage consistency verification, and failure-driven recollection across 11 Ground--Relation--Act forms. (2) Anvil-Ben provides scene-aligned supervision for learning diverse embodied spatial capabilities and scene-linked assessments that distinguish stage competence from error propagation. (3) Anvil-MOPD is a 9B student that consolidates heterogeneous spatial knowledge through distillation from specialist teachers. Semantic-first-fault routing targets teacher supervision to the student's earliest decisive failure, while stage normalization controls the loss scale across native outputs of different lengths. We construct 5.8M source-grounded records spanning 160K images, reserving 580K spatial records for held-out evaluation and using the remaining 5.22M records for training. On average across seven public benchmarks, Anvil-MOPD outperforms Qwen3.5-9B and RynnBrain-8B by 9.29 and 11.03 percentage points, respectively, while reaching 80.67% on VABench-Point. Controlled studies support targeted coverage, selective transfer, and feedback-guided repair as complementary framework components.

</details>


### [78] [SurgStreamer: proactive, request-driven monitoring of surgical video streams with structured state and a reusable reasoning layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21895)
*Bowen Xiang,Jon S. Heiselman,Michael I. Miga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为SurgStreamer的系统，用于手术团队在视频监护和事件检测中的助手功能，系统通过分离特定手术感知和通用推理模块，在不同类型和新手术中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决外科手术团队在复杂手术过程中需要实时监控特定事件和回答关于手术进程的问题的需求，提高手术效率和安全性。

Method: 该方法将手术过程分为特定手术感知和通用推理模块，使用视频语言模型OneStreamer-4B进行感知，通过规则或小控制器进行推理，同时对比测试了不同模型配置下的性能。

Result: SurgStreamer在12例腹腔镜胆囊切除手术中的宏事件F1值达到0.598，75秒窗口内达到了0.677。与一个小型控制器相比，OneStreamer-4B的多种配置仅达到0.540 (Holm调整后的 p <= 0.012)。在三个外部中心重新训练感知模块，性能从0.239提高到0.470。在新的垂体手术中，控制器权重训练后的性能接近于域内控制器性能，但未达到预先注册的非劣效性阈值，除非调整警报阈值。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，将感知与推理分离是可行的，并且主要限制在于感知模块在不同分布中的表现。

Abstract: Surgical teams could benefit from assistants that watch the video for requested events, such as "tell me when the clip applier appears during Calot triangle dissection", and answer questions about what has already happened. We formalise this task as request-driven event detection with exact semantics and causal alert decisions, and present SurgStreamer. It separates procedure-specific perception, which maps the 1-FPS stream to a structured state over tools, actions and phases, from procedure-agnostic reasoning, which evaluates a formal monitoring condition over this state and an event memory, either with exact rules or with a small controller that addresses entities through pointers rather than names. We evaluate this monitoring core with gold conditions. On a pre-registered, sealed test set of 12 laparoscopic cholecystectomies, SurgStreamer reached a macro event-F1 of 0.598 over full procedures. On identical 75-s windows with only the window available to every method, it scored 0.677. The tested configurations of the streaming vision-language model OneStreamer-4B, including ones fine-tuned on SurgStreamer's state or on video with an adapted vision encoder, scored at most 0.540 (Holm-adjusted p <= 0.012). At three external centres, re-training only perception raised performance on held-out procedures from 0.239 to 0.470. On a new procedure, pituitary surgery, controller weights trained on cholecystectomy matched an in-domain controller on oracle state (0.947 vs 0.938). On predicted state they missed the pre-registered non-inferiority margin (-0.024 [-0.040, -0.008]) unless the alert thresholds were re-calibrated. The results support separating perception from reasoning and point to perception under distribution shift as the main limitation.

</details>


### [79] [Seeing is Believing: Benchmarking and Mitigating the Interference of Language Priors on Visual Perception in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21902)
*Li Ma,Yan Zhuang,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViSiP-Bench 是一个新的基准测试，通过引入动态先验诱导协议和多维度评估指标来更精确地衡量 MLLMs 中语言先验的影响。研究发现，先验依存普遍存在且与模型规模无关，提出 MAPS 方法来缓解过强或过弱的先验依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Benchmark 假设模型共享相同的先验知识，并依赖准确率来评估。然而实际情况下，跨模型先验一致性较低，部分问题仅依靠文本就能回答，导致准确率过高估计了先验的抵抗力。

Method: 引入 ViSiP-Bench，它基于罕见但真实的情景，涵盖六类先验。使用模型感知的动态先验诱导协议以及三维评估指标。此外，提出 MAPS 方法，通过软化过度依赖先验，使其符合事实背景，而不影响模型的通用能力。

Result: 24 个模型在 ViSiP-Bench 的评估中显示，先验依存普遍存在且与模型规模无关，较大模型倾向于完全忽略或严格遵循先验。

Conclusion: 研究提议使用 MAPS 方法来识别并调整模型的先验依赖，以改善模型在视觉感知任务中的表现。

Abstract: Language priors in MLLMs can override visual evidence, impairing visual perception. Existing benchmarks assume shared priors across models and rely solely on accuracy. However, cross-model prior agreement falls below 80%, and over 15% of items can be answered correctly without any image, causing accuracy to overestimate prior resistance. To address these limitations, we introduce ViSiP-Bench, a benchmark grounded in rare-but-real scenarios across six prior categories, with a model-aware dynamic prior induction protocol and three metrics that more precisely and continuously measure prior resistance than accuracy alone. Evaluating 24 models reveals that prior adherence is pervasive, does not improve with scale, and larger models tend to either strongly override their priors or firmly adhere to them. We therefore propose MAPS, which identifies each model's strongest priors and fine-tunes on factually grounded knowledge to soften excessive prior reliance without disrupting general capabilities.

</details>


### [80] [BridgeAfford: Bridging Query-Target Gap for Generalizable 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21918)
*Yijie Su,Yakun Zhu,Yi Bin,Pengpeng Zeng,Liang Chen,Guoqing Wang,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BridgeAfford 提出了一种框架从两个互补的角度减少查询和目标区域之间的语义差距，以提高对未见物体的语义导向能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在未见物体上的泛化能力较差，主要原因是查询特征与目标区域特征之间存在语义差异。

Method: BridgeAfford框架包括两个主要模块：交互区域对齐（IRA）和视觉证据聚合（VEA）。IRA促进几何相似交互区域之间的跨对象对齐；VAE利用2D语义模型精炼注意力分布，引导查询指向正确的区域。

Result: 在LASO和PIAD数据集上的实验表明，BridgeAfford在未见物体上的表现优于现有方法。

Conclusion: BridgeAfford通过减少查询和目标区域之间的语义差距，显著提高了对未见物体的语义导向能力。

Abstract: Language-guided 3D affordance grounding aims to localize functional regions on 3D objects according to interaction intents. However, existing methods exhibit poor generalization to unseen objects. One challenge is the query-target gap: a semantic discrepancy between query features encoding interaction intents and target-region features, leading to inaccurate grounding. End-to-end training favors query representations tailored to seen objects, limiting their compatibility with shifted target-region features on unseen objects. To address this issue, we propose BridgeAfford, a framework that reduces the query-target semantic gap from two complementary perspectives. First, Interaction Region Alignment (IRA) promotes cross-object alignment among geometrically similar interaction regions during training. This enables structures on unseen objects that resemble previously observed ones to produce representations similar to those learned during training, facilitating target-region recognition. Second, Visual Evidence Aggregation (VEA) leverages a 2D affordance model to refine the attention distribution and guide the query toward the correct region, yielding more target-aware queries. Extensive experiments on the LASO and PIAD datasets demonstrate that BridgeAfford outperforms existing methods, particularly on unseen objects, highlighting its robust generalization ability.

</details>


### [81] [Flow-CLIP: Image-Conditioned Residual Scoring for CompositionalImage-Text Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21937)
*Minghong Wei,Enguang Zhu,Pu Cao,Lu Yang,Qing Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为Flow-CLIP的方法，通过改进全局图像-文本对齐，增强了句子级别的跨模态相似性度量，在属性-对象绑定基准测试中提高了匹配准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言预训练技术在图像文本检索和零样本识别中取得了显著进展。然而，这些技术在处理组成匹配时，句子级别对齐不足，导致描述相同对象和属性的句子具有不同的组合方式。

Method: Flow-CLIP方法包括三个关键模块：全局兼容性校准（GCC）通过学习句子级别的相似性度量；图像条件证据精炼（ICER）将视觉块上下文融入到标记查询中，并使用局部匹配证据的变化调整全局得分；跨图像边距排名（CIMR）在匹配与不匹配的图像之间比较同一个正负标题对，增强了支持正确描述的图像的支持。

Result: 实验结果表明，Flow-CLIP相比预训练的CLIP模型和通过局部证据精炼学习全局对齐，在组成匹配和属性-对象绑定基准测试中提高了匹配准确度，并通过跨图像排名监督减少了对语言先验的依赖。

Conclusion: Flow-CLIP方法通过增强全局图像-文本对齐和引入跨图像排名监督，提高了组成匹配和属性-对象绑定的准确度，表明了该方法对图像文本理解任务的有效性。

Abstract: Vision--language pre-training has enabled substantial progress in image--text retrieval and zero-shot recognition by learning transferable cross-modal representations. However, sentence-level alignment remains insufficient for compositional matching, where captions can describe the same objects and attributes while differing in how these elements are bound. To address this limitation, we propose Flow-CLIP, which refines global image-text alignment through image-conditioned changes in token--patch evidence. Global Compatibility Calibration (GCC) learns a sentence-level similarity metric, while Image-Conditioned Evidence Refinement (ICER) incorporates visual patch context into token queries and uses the resulting change in local matching evidence to adjust the global score. We further introduce Cross-Image Margin Ranking (CIMR), which compares the same positive--negative caption pair across matched and mismatched images, encouraging a larger ranking margin when the image supports the correct description. Experiments on compositional matching and attribute--object binding benchmarks show that Flow-CLIP improves binding accuracy over pretrained CLIP and learned global alignment through local evidence refinement, while cross-image ranking supervision mitigates reliance on language priors. Code will be released at link.

</details>


### [82] [VesperMUSE: Multi-Modal Fusion Vision Transformer for Image Semantic Segmentation Under Diverse Light Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21990)
*Raul Balmez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VesperMUSE是一个多模态框架，通过物理启发的低光照适应算法、热引导和多模态融合方法，解决低光照条件下的语义分割问题，提高了极端环境下的视觉感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语义分割技术在低光照环境下表现不佳，因为存在噪音、伪影、色彩失真和结构细节丢失等问题。VesperMUSE通过克服这些挑战，旨在实现跨多种光照条件下的鲁棒图像语义分割，提高机器在极端和不规则环境中的感知效果。

Method: VesperMUSE框架结合了一种新的基于物理的低光增强算法、热导向方法和多模态融合 approaches 来提高低光照条件下的语义分割性能。具体而言，框架通过自注意机制融合彩红外图和表面法线图，突出几何不连续性和材料边界的特点，提取全局上下文和精细局部特征，实现多模态表征的统一投影，增强网络对跨模态相关线索的注意力。

Result: 在广泛的评估中，VesperMUSE成功恢复了丢失的视觉线索，为极端和不规则环境下的机器感知提供了灵活的最新解决方案。

Conclusion: 综上所述，VesperMUSE通过多模态融合方法显著提升了低光照条件下的语义分割性能，展示了其在不受规则环境感知方面的潜力。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is critical for the reliable navigation and scene understanding of autonomous systems, yet its performance degrades severely in low-light environments due to noise, artifacts, colour distortion, and the loss of structural details. To address this challenge, this work introduces VesperMUSE, a unified multi-modal framework designed to achieve robust image semantic segmentation across diverse illumination conditions. The architecture addresses the light deficiency problem on multiple levels: proposing a novel physics-informed synthetic low-light adaptation algorithm, approaching Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) through a thermal-guided paradigm, and finally aiming to recover the intrinsic scene details for semantic segmentation through a multi-modal fusion approach. Following enhancement, the framework dynamically integrates the primary RGB features with auxiliary thermal representations and surface normal maps highlighting geometric discontinuities and material boundaries. At the core of this integration is a highly scalable, modality-agnostic self-attention mechanism that extracts both global context and fine local features from an arbitrary number of auxiliary inputs. By combining and projecting these hierarchical multi-modal representations into a unified component, the attention module allows the network to selectively attend to relevant cross-modal cues without requiring any architectural modifications when different sensor configurations are used. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VesperMUSE successfully recovers missing visual cues, providing a flexible, state-of-the-art (SOTA) solution for machine perception in extreme and unstructured real-world environments.

</details>


### [83] [Data-Efficient ConvNeXt Pretraining for High-Resolution Sentinel-1 GRD SAR Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21993)
*Yuki Natsume,Sang-Ho Yun,Chai Kiat Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparK-SAR是一种省算力的自监督预训练框架，通过提出ConvNeXt-RS编码器、轻量级的ConvNeXt-512解码器和对FP16混合精度预训练不稳定性的研究，提高S1GRD数据上基于遥感的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前S1GRD数据稀少，阻碍了监督深度学习在陆表面遥感中的应用；同时，现有的自监督预训练方法依赖大量数据和计算。因此，需要一种在有限资源条件下有效利用卷积归纳偏差的自监督预训练方法。

Method: SparK-SAR框架采用了适应性卷积编码器ConvNeXt-RS、轻量级解码器ConvNeXt-512以及混合精度预训练策略，同时进行了数据规模对效率的影响研究。

Result: 在四个Copernicus-Bench S1GRD任务上，SparK-SAR框架的调优ConvNeXt-RS编码器相比于无预训练基线分别提高了4.7%，5.0%，5.8%和1.4%的性能，并且在三个任务上表现优于139M参数的冻结Copernicus-FM ViT，参数量减少约5倍。数据规模扩展研究表明，分类任务在10-25%的数据量下饱和，而密集预测任务持续改善至25-50%的数据量。

Conclusion: SparK-SAR框架为S1GRD数据的自监督预训练提供了一种有效方法，提高了端到端的遥感任务性能，特别是在资源受限的情况下。

Abstract: Labeled Sentinel-1 Ground Range Detected (S1GRD) datasets remain scarce, limiting supervised deep learning for land-surface remote sensing, while self-supervised pretraining for this domain has been dominated by Vision Transformer (ViT)-based masked image modeling requiring substantial data and compute. We propose SparK-SAR, a sparse convolutional masked pretraining framework built on SparK and ConvNeXt, designed to exploit convolutional inductive biases for S1GRD imagery under constrained compute. SparK-SAR makes three core technical contributions: (i) ConvNeXt-RS, a domain-adapted encoder that halves the stem stride (4x to 2x) to produce 14x14 feature maps at stride 16x, providing 4x more spatial tokens at identical parameter count; (ii) a lightweight ConvNeXt-512 reconstruction decoder with PixelShuffle upsampling that reduces pretraining VRAM by 7.5-8.5% per GPU relative to a UNet-128 baseline while matching its accuracy; and (iii) an empirical characterization of FP16 mixed-precision pretraining instability in the standard SparK UNet-768 decoder on S1GRD data, complemented by a finding that finetuning with layer-wise learning rate decay (LLRD) improves downstream performance of pretrained encoders. On four Copernicus-Bench S1GRD tasks, our finetuned 27.8M-parameter ConvNeXt-RS encoder improves over a non-pretrained baseline by +4.7, +5.0, +5.8, and +1.4 pp on EuroSAT-S1, BigEarthNet-S1, DFC2020-S1, and Flood-S1 respectively, and outperforms the Copernicus-FM ViT (139M parameters, frozen) on three of four tasks at roughly 5x fewer parameters. A pretraining data-scaling study reveals task-dependent data efficiency: classification saturates with as little as 10-25% of the SSL4EO-S12 v1.1 corpus, while dense prediction continues to improve significantly up to 25-50%. The study also includes a CKA representation analysis and a geographic de-collision experiment.

</details>


### [84] [Visual Sanad: Auditing Vision-Language Models for False Acceptance of Qur'anic Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22009)
*Niaz Mohaiman Abtahi,Reyana Shahid,Evan Maksud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Visual Sanad 是一个用于检验视觉语言模型（VLMs）在不同视觉呈现方式下是否能准确验证宗教引用的真实性的基准测试。研究发现，尽管一些模型在这一任务上表现良好，但大多数模型在这一方面表现较弱，尤其是开放版本的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视觉语言模型被用于解释宗教、教育和文化敏感的视觉内容，其引用验证的可靠性仍需更深入的研究。Visual Sanad 的创建旨在揭示视觉呈现方式对这些模型准确验证宗教引用能力的影响。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个包含 2,400 张渲染图像的基准测试，这些图像源自 100 部英语《古兰经》经文，每部经文有四种事实状态和六种视觉条件，所有引用均经过验证和人工审核。通过 24 次完整的模型提示运行来评估模型表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，专有 API 服务的模型在引用验证上表现出不同程度的准确性，而最新的开放模型则在大多数情况下表现较弱，表明它们缺乏强大的引用验证能力。去偏见的提示可以减少错误接受，但这通常通过改变决策阈值而不是产生明确的引用区分能力来实现。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，视觉语言模型在验证宗教引用的真实性方面存在文化根基的多模态事实验证失败。拥有流畅性但缺乏强大引用验证能力的模型可能会将显示的宗教引用视为自证合法的。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret religious, educational, and culturally sensitive visual content, yet their reliability on citation verification remains underexplored. We introduce Visual Sanad, a controlled benchmark for testing whether VLMs falsely accept incorrect Qur'anic verse attributions when the same quote-reference pair is rendered under different visual framings. The benchmark contains 2,400 rendered images built from 100 English Qur'an verses, four factual states per verse, and six visual conditions ranging from plain text to decorative and authority-framed layouts. All quote-reference pairs were source-checked and human-audited before rendering. Across 24 full model-prompt runs, we observe a sharp capability split. Proprietary API-served VLMs range from near-perfect citation verification to moderate false acceptance, whereas recent open-weight VLMs often show weak discrimination and default to VALID on most images. De-biased prompting can reduce false acceptance, but often by shifting the decision threshold toward INVALID rather than by producing strong citation discrimination. Visual Sanad therefore exposes a culturally grounded multimodal factuality failure: fluent VLMs may treat displayed religious citations as self-authenticating unless they have strong citation-verification ability.

</details>


### [85] [When Models Misread Ink: Auditing VLMs for Arabic Calligraphy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22018)
*Niaz Mohaiman Abtahi,Evan Maksud,Reyana Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了12种视觉语言模型在阿拉伯书法识别任务上的表现，发现模型普遍存在标签合并问题，特别是Thuluth风格占主导。提示方法效果不一，视觉线索有助于提升准确性但也有负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在填补对阿拉伯书法，一种高价值的文化视觉领域，VLM评估的空白，尤其是在其细粒度书法传统方面。

Method: 研究人员使用一个包含1195张图片的六分类基准数据集，测试了12种VLM在阿拉伯书法识别任务上的表现。

Result: 在闭合集中，最佳的模型准确率为：API模型60.9%，开放权重模型47.2%。主要问题是标签合并，尤其是Thuluth风格，GPT-4o将52.9%的图片识别为Thuluth，而实际上只有18.1%的图片为Thuluth风格。

Conclusion: 研究认为阿拉伯书法识别是对VLM稳健性的一次压缩测试，并揭示了哪些视觉传统可被自动系统识别。

Abstract: Arabic calligraphy is a high-stakes cultural visual domain for Muslim communities, yet VLM evaluations rarely test its fine-grained script traditions. We audit 12 VLMs on a cleaned 1,195-image six-way benchmark using numeric, visual-cue, and Diwani-first prompts. In this closed-set setting, the best numeric-prompt accuracy is 60.9% for closed/API models and 47.2% for open-weight models. The main failure is default-label collapse: every numeric run is dominated by a single label taking 33-63% of predictions, against an 18.8% majority-class baseline. For 8 of 12 models the default is Thuluth; GPT-4o labels 52.9% of all images as Thuluth although it is only 18.1% of the set. Prompting helps unevenly: visual cues move accuracy by more than 10 points for some models but hurt others, and foregrounding Diwani often relocates the default rather than removing it. Arabic calligraphy recognition is therefore a compact, culturally grounded stress test for VLM robustness and for measuring which visual traditions become legible to automated systems.

</details>


### [86] [FeaTok: Feature-Loss Tokenization for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22060)
*Zhang Xiaofeng,Samuel Lavoie,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Felix Friedrich,Jakob Verbeek,Aaron Courville,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero-Soriano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为FeaTok的新方法，通过重建预训练特征而不是像素来训练离散标记，从而提高生成性能。在条件生成任务中，FeaTok在计算效率和生成质量之间提供了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图像标记化方法主要关注像素级别的重建，这可能破坏生成过程中有用的特征。为了改善这种情况，研究者提出了FeaTok，通过重建预训练的特征来优化离散标记化的训练过程。

Method: FeaTok方法利用了特征预测的自我监督学习方法，将标记化过程与像素重建分离，转而训练模型根据预训练的特征进行重建。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用FeaTok训练的生成模型在多项任务上都表现出更优秀的生成性能。特别是在条件生成任务中，FeaTok相比其他基准模型，不仅提升了生成质量和减少了计算成本，还能够在没有任何奖励训练的情况下获得更高的奖励模型评分。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过利用预训练特征进行离散标记化训练，可以在确保生成性能的同时降低计算资源的消耗，为图像生成领域提供了一种新的解决方案。

Abstract: Discrete image generators learn the distribution of tokens produced by an image tokenizer. These token representations should be easy for generators to model, not merely enable faithful image reconstruction. Yet these tokenizers are commonly trained to reconstruct pixels, which prioritizes low-level fidelity and may disrupt representation structure useful for generation. Motivated by feature-predictive self-supervised learning, we introduce feature-loss tokenization (FeaTok), which trains discrete tokens by reconstructing pretrained features rather than pixels. Ablations across SigLIP, SigLIP2, DINOv2, and DINOv3 show better generative performance with FeaTok. In class-conditional generation, FeaTok advances the quality-compute Pareto frontier, reducing gFDr6 at 256x256 by up to 29 percent over the strongest discrete baselines and outperforming the strongest unguided continuous baseline (RAEv2) with 38 times less sampling compute. FeaTok also achieves higher human-preference reward-model scores than prior art without reward-based training and surpasses continuous and discrete text-conditional baselines on three benchmarks with only 500 fine-tuning updates after pre-training.

</details>


### [87] [Memory as Weights: Internalizing Long-Term History for Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22070)
*Zhuoming Liu,Jinhong Lin,Lin Zhang,Yiwu Zhong,Yin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaLoW 提出了一种新的流式记忆框架，通过递归 HyperNetwork 动态合并视频观察，减少历史信息和当前观察的竞争，提供优越的性能和较低的延迟，同时在行动成功率达到提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的流视频理解模型在处理历史信息和当前观察时存在竞争，导致性能不佳。MaLoW 旨在解决这一问题，以提高流视频理解的性能。

Method: MaLoW 使用递归 HyperNetwork 将视频观察逐步整合进固定尺寸的 LoRA 权重，将历史信息作为权重而不是重新处理完整视频流。此外，还构建了一个大规模的流视频数据集 MaLoW-Instruct，以支持训练。

Result: 在 OVO-Bench、StreamingBench 和 StreamArena 上，MaLoW 表现出了优越的性能，特别是在 OVO-Bench 的后向分割上，相比之前的记忆方法，性能提高了 3.9% 到 11.8%，同时仅带来轻微的延迟增加。此外，MaLoW 还增强了视觉语言行动模型的表现。

Conclusion: 研究证明，MaLoW 的有效性不仅在流视频理解，还在其他视觉语言模型中表现出有效的改进。

Abstract: Streaming vision-language models (VLMs) must continuously integrate information from the past while remaining responsive to current observations and user queries. Existing memory mechanisms typically compress, retrieve, or cache historical observations and reintroduce them into the input context, forcing historical memory and current observations to compete for limited context. We propose MaLoW, a streaming memory framework that instead internalizes historical context into evolving lightweight parameter updates. MaLoW uses a recurrent HyperNetwork to incrementally consolidate incoming video observations into fixed-size LoRA weights, allowing historical information to be carried forward without revisiting the full video stream. In doing so, MaLoW represents history as weights, while keeping the current observation as input. To support training, we further construct MaLoW-Instruct, a large-scale streaming video dataset containing 381K question-answer pairs over 56K videos. We conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate that MaLoW achieves strong performance on OVO-Bench, StreamingBench, and StreamArena. On the backward split of OVO-Bench, MaLoW outperforms prior memory approaches by 3.9% to 11.8% across different video backbones while incurring only modest latency overhead. Beyond streaming video understanding, MaLoW also generalizes to vision-language-action models, improving the action success rate on Simpler WidowX by 9.3%.

</details>


### [88] [Retrofitting Recurrent Vision-Language Models from Pretrained Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22075)
*Madhav Iyengar,Andreas Geiger,Hilde Kühne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将深度递归策略应用到预训练的视觉语言模型中，通过精简语言主干层并循环使用剩余层，提出了一种三阶段恢复训练方法来恢复性能，从而减小参数量并提升模型在推理基准测试中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的工作表明，深度递归可以被整合到预训练语言模型中，以减少模型的参数数量。本研究探索了将这一策略应用于预训练视觉语言模型，提出了一种新的优化方法。

Method: 研究者通过从视觉语言模型的语言主干中删除某些层，并使用循环机制来重新处理剩余的层。在此过程中，他们使用线性适配器将块的输出与前面层的token表示进行融合，并将融合表示投影回块中。为了缓解因剪枝对模型性能的影响，研究者提出了一个由语言仅预训练、视觉语言投影对齐和全面微调三个阶段组成的恢复训练过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，经过递归回路调整的模型在不使用额外的视觉语言数据的情况下，显著减少了23.3%的语言参数量，并且在推理基准测试中优于未修剪的基本模型。同时，该方法在将递归语言模型转换为视觉语言模型的任务中也表现出色。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的方法在处理某些推理基准测试方面表现出显著优势，且在基本视觉理解基准测试中的性能与基线模型相当，表明该方法在特定任务中的有效性和适用性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that depth recurrence can be retrofitted into pretrained language models. In this work, we adapt this paradigm for visual understanding, using depth recurrence to reduce the parameter footprint of a pretrained vision-language model (VLM). To this end, we prune layers from the language backbone of the VLM and loop over a subset of the remaining layers. At each step of the loop, a linear adapter merges the block's output with the token representations from the preceding layers and projects the fused representation back into the block. As the pruning impacts the original model performance, we propose a three-stage recovery training: first, we leverage a language-only pretraining to recover the performance of the language backbone, followed by an alignment of the vision-language projector, and a full fine-tuning of the model via visual instruction tuning. In the first and the last stage, we distill the outputs of the pretrained non-recurrent model into the recurrent model, which further improves the recovery. We evaluate the proposed approach on the TinyLLaVA-1.4B backbone without introducing any new vision-language data during retrofitting. Our experiments demonstrate that the retrofitted recurrent model outperforms the unpruned baseline trained with the same recovery data while storing 23.3% fewer language parameters. We further show that the proposed retrofitting recurrence outperforms the setup of converting a recurrent LM into a VLM on the same data. Additionally, our ablations show how design choices, such as the adapter between recurrences and a larger recurrent block, lead to the improved performance of our approach. Finally, we find that our proposed model gains on reasoning-oriented benchmarks, whereas its performance on basic visual understanding benchmarks remains on par with the baseline.

</details>


### [89] [Pathology foundation models for training-free case retrieval in salivary gland tumors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22087)
*Maximilian J. Roesel,Niklas Kiermeyer,Philipp Keyl,Frederick Klauschen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该方法利用内容相关检索，通过病理基础模型查询数字化档案，以识别相似病例，从而辅助罕见癌症诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理诊断依赖于丰富的经验和培训，但识别罕见癌症亚型仍具有挑战性。研究旨在探索是否可以通过使用当前的病理基础模型在不需要特定任务训练的情况下提高罕见癌症诊断的准确性。

Method: 研究利用栅门注意力机制的多实例学习模型自动或手动选择感兴趣区域（ROI），通过检索形态学相似的病例及其ICD-O代码和相应的全视野图像（WSI），来实现内容相关检索。评价方法使用不平衡数据集进行10折交叉验证。

Result: 该方法在137例唾液腺肿瘤病例中（涵盖12个形态学类别）进行了测试，结果显示Atlas2模型在最顶部一、三、五个检索结果中的正确率为62.5%、76.7%和81.3%；四个模型在五个具备至少十个病例的类别上的宏平均F1分数分别为69.0%至76.6%。Atlas2模型在前五个搜索结果中分离正确匹配与错误匹配的性能最佳。

Conclusion: 该方法不需要额外的训练或手动标注，利用病理基础模型可使部门档案可搜索，为基于病例的鉴别诊断提供了低门槛的途径，并扩大了个人框架视野至整个部门的经验范围。

Abstract: In pathology, pattern recognition relies on a frame of reference built through extensive training and experience, which may still fall short when encountering rare cancer subtypes. Archives of previously diagnosed tumors often contain relevant precedents, yet identifying the most relevant cases remains challenging. Here we explore whether content-based retrieval with current pathology foundation models facilitates rare cancer diagnostics without any task-specific training. Pathologists can query a digitized archive using a region of interest (ROI) from a whole slide image (WSI) and retrieve morphologically similar cases, along with their ICD-O codes and corresponding WSIs. The ROI can be selected manually or automatically using a gated attention-based multiple instance learning model. We evaluate our approach by leave-one-case-out on an imbalanced dataset of 137 salivary gland tumor cases spanning 12 ICD-O morphology categories, comparing four pathology foundation models (Atlas2, Atlas2-B, Atlas2-S, and UNI2h). Top-1/3/5 retrieval was 62.5%/76.7%/81.3% for Atlas2, 50.2/66.6/72.9% for UNI2h, 52.3%/71.5%/77.6% for Atlas2-B and 54.4/65.2/73.7% for Atlas2-S. Macro-averaged F1 ranged from 69.0% for the smallest model Atlas2-S to 76.6% for the largest model Atlas2 across the five classes with at least ten cases. Our modified Chamfer similarity separated correct from incorrect matches among the Top-5 more reliably for Atlas2 (AUC 0.754) than for Atlas2-B (0.679, p = 0.020) and Atlas2-S (0.653, p = 0.003), but not UNI2h (0.697, p = 0.196). Requiring no additional training or manual annotation, this approach makes departmental archives searchable with pathology foundation models, providing a low-barrier route to case-based differential diagnosis and extending the individual frame of reference to the collective experience of the department.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond the Dominant Label: Recovering Coexisting Lesion Evidence with Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22091)
*Lubin Gan,Jing Zhang,Xinming Zhang,Chengpeng Xu,Siying Wu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为DomSet-MIL的新框架，旨在更好地处理包括共存病变预测在内的复杂多实例学习问题。该模型通过建模最严重病变而不会忽略未报告的病变，保留次要发现的支持，从而实现了更有效的临床诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多实例学习方法通常难以处理复杂的真实世界场景，如共存病变，特别是当监督信息不完整时，仅仅关注最严重病变而忽略次要病变。因此，提出一种处理这些挑战的新框架是必要的。

Method: DomSet-MIL框架包括Dominant-Label Censored Likelihood 和Severity-Ordered Residual Routing。前者建模报告的诊断而不假定未报告病变的缺失，后者从高到低排序局部证据并连接到整体预测。

Result: 实验表明，与现有方法相比，DomSet-MIL在共存病变预测中实现了最先进的性能，能够更好恢复未报告共存病变的证据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，建模主导标签机制可以帮助提高诊断预测能力，同时保留临床相关的次要发现，从而完善复杂实例学习的应用。

Abstract: Whole-slide images may contain coexisting lesions, yet weak supervision typically provides only the most severe diagnosis. We formulate this setting as dominant-label multiple instance learning, where the observed label is the maximum-severity element of a latent lesion set. Standard multiclass MIL treats it as a complete one-hot description and therefore suppresses lower-severity evidence whenever it co-occurs with a dominant lesion. We propose DomSet-MIL, a label-aware framework that aligns the annotation mechanism across bag and instance levels. Dominant-Label Censored Likelihood models the reported diagnosis without declaring unreported lesions absent. Severity-Ordered Residual Routing assigns patch evidence from high to low severity and preserves residual support for secondary findings, while top-$q$ pooling links local routing scores to bag prediction. Experiments across natural and controlled settings show that DomSet-MIL achieves state-of-the-art performance in dominant-label prediction while more effectively recovering latent coexisting-lesion evidence. These findings demonstrate that modeling the dominant-label mechanism can improve diagnostic prediction without sacrificing clinically meaningful secondary findings.

</details>


### [91] [SCCL-DT: Semantic-Conflict-Aware Contrastive Learning with Dual-Rule Teacher Distillation for Zero-Shot Chest X-Ray Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22094)
*Ting Liu,Haoran Lai,HaobinChen,Ming Liu,S Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新颖的对比学习方法SCCL，用于医学影像的零样本诊断，通过检测并规避语义冲突来改进诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在处理胸部X光片时存在语义冲突问题，导致误分类和不良的监督，研究旨在解决这一问题。

Method: SCCL方法通过跟踪模态特异性相似性分布，并使用指数移动平均（EMA），结合双向跨模态排名，通过标准蒸馏确定一个单一同等有效的学生SCCL-DT。

Result: 实验结果表明，SCCL-DT方法在五个公开的胸部X光数据集上实现了平均零样本AUC为0.850，优于最强基线方法0.837。

Conclusion: SCCL方法通过解决语义冲突，提高了零样本胸部X光诊断的准确性。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language pre-training supports zero-shot chest X-ray diagnosis, but standard objectives treat every off-diagonal image-report pair in a batch as a negative. In radiology, recurring findings across patients mean that instance mismatch need not imply semantic incompatibility, creating false-negative supervision that we term a semantic conflict. A training-trace audit shows that pair-observations selected by the online mask used during training are enriched for report-derived finding agreement, although this proxy analysis does not establish clinical equivalence. Fixed thresholds can become miscalibrated as similarity distributions evolve, while soft reweighting leaves residual repulsive gradients. We therefore propose Semantic-Conflict-Aware Contrastive Learning (SCCL), which tracks modality-specific similarity distributions with exponential-moving-average (EMA) statistics, derives adaptive thresholds, and hard-masks flagged candidates before softmax normalization. Inclusive (OR) and consensus (AND) rules define two SCCL teachers, whose bidirectional cross-modal rankings are consolidated through standard distillation into a single deployable student, SCCL-DT. Across five public chest X-ray benchmarks, SCCL-DT achieves a mean zero-shot AUC of $0.850$, compared with $0.837$ for the strongest evaluated baseline in our comparison. Code will be made publicly available soon.

</details>


### [92] [FALCON: FPGA-Accelerated Latency-Certifiable Occupancy Networks for Camera-Based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22143)
*Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究表明，通过开发FALCON系统，实现了在最坏情况下的帧延迟保证，即使在包含冗余任务的情况下也能满足100ms的截止时间要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对感知系统需要在每个帧上都要在最坏情况下的截止时间内做出决策，传统加速器并不能满足这种严格的延迟要求。

Method: 研究团队基于AMD Kria KR260嵌入式FPGA构建了FALCON系统。通过分析可编程逻辑的周期性计算与测量推理的边界值，并采用看门狗机制保证输出及时性。

Result: 系统能够在三个各200,000帧的强制考核期间，实现了所有代理帧在100ms加上最多10.2ms内输出。在完整的六摄像机FlashOcc管道上，每帧仅花费1.3秒，虽然尚未达到10Hz的运行速度。通过FALCON系统，推理库在全Occ3D-nuScenes验证集上达到了29.88的mIoU，比DPU无法运行的FP32基线低92.8%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，FALCON系统可以在保持低延迟的同时，实现复杂的3D占用网格生成，并在实际部署中展示出强大的性能和可靠性。

Abstract: A perception system that must brake for a pedestrian does not need a low average latency. It must deliver an answer before the deadline on every frame, the worst case included. Commodity edge accelerators optimize the mean through dynamic scheduling, OS jitter, thermal throttling, and contention, so their per-frame latency can be measured but not bounded. We build FALCON, a camera-based 3D occupancy front-end on the AMD Kria KR260, an embedded FPGA drawing 4.3 W idle and up to 6.6 W under inference, and make the latency guarantee layered and explicit: programmable-logic (PL) compute is cycle-bounded analytically; inference is a measured envelope over 100k-frame traces; and a watchdog enforces output delivery, publishing the previous output flagged stale when a frame is late. The deadline runs use a fixed-work proxy of six ResNet-50 DPU inferences per frame, not the full occupancy network: across three 100k-frame enforced runs, all 300k proxy frames delivered an output within the 100 ms deadline plus at most 10.2 ms, the worst of the three runs. The full six-camera FlashOcc pipeline composes to about 1.3 s per frame on the board and is not yet a 10 Hz system. The two operations the DPU cannot run, a view-transform gather and a bilinear resize, become fixed-trip-count PL IPs numerically matched to their software references. On the full Occ3D-nuScenes validation set the board reaches mIoU 29.88, 92.8% of an FP32 baseline the DPU cannot run. On a ResNet-50 probe over 100k frames the KR260 tail stays within 1.5 times its median (2.0 times for the six-inference proxy), where Jetson Orin reaches 4.4 times and the worst datacenter GPU run (NVIDIA L4) 23.6 times. The deployment recipe, HLS sources, and raw timing traces are released.

</details>


### [93] [Motion- and Gravity-Aware Feedforward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22177)
*Shiyao Qian,Yuan Ren,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习使用辅助信息（LUPI） paradigm的运动和重力感知框架，通过在训练过程中提供残差光流和重力方向作为辅助信号，以指导几何表示的学习；在推理过程中，模型仅使用标准视觉输入。这些先验知识被整合到轻量级的transformer骨干网络中，保持了前馈效率。实验结果表明，在包含动态对象、模糊结构和挑战性摄像机运动的场景下，该方法在重建准确性和鲁棒性方面均表现出了一致的提升。进一步的分析表明，对于不同的骨干网络，最佳的先验注入位置可能不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于feed-forward transformer的多视图3D重建方法虽表现出色，但仍然主要依赖于基于外观的注意力机制，在动态场景下由于缺乏几何线索而表现不佳。因此，本文提出了一种运动和重力感知框架，通过在训练过程中提供额外的辅助信息来解决这些问题。

Method: 本文提出了一个结合了运动和重力信息的transformer模型。在训练阶段，将残差光流和重力方向作为辅助信息（privileged signals）提供给模型，以指导几何表示的学习。而在推理阶段，模型仅使用标准的视觉输入。通过简单的架构调整，将这些先验信息整合到transformer模型中，保持了模型的前馈效率。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法在标准数据集上的重建准确性与鲁棒性得到了一致的提升，特别是在动态物体、模糊结构和具有挑战性的摄像机运动的场景中表现尤为明显。进一步分析表明，不同的transformer模型最优的先验注入位置有所不同，如对于VGGT骨干网络，最适合在中间层进行集成；而对于π3模型，则应在较晚阶段进行集成。

Conclusion: 本文介绍的基于LUPI paradigm的运动和重力感知框架为在feed-forward 3D重建框架中注入运动和物理先验提供了一种有效的解决方案，而不会增加测试时间的复杂性。

Abstract: Feed-forward transformer models have recently achieved strong performance in multi-view 3D reconstruction, but they still rely primarily on appearance-based attention and often struggle in dynamic scenes with weak geometric cues. In this work, we introduce a motion- and gravity-aware framework under the Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI) paradigm. During training, residual optical flow and gravity direction are provided as privileged signals to guide geometric representation learning; during inference, the model uses standard visual inputs only.
  We integrate these priors into transformer backbones with lightweight architectural changes, preserving feed-forward efficiency. Experiments on standard benchmarks show consistent gains in reconstruction accuracy and robustness, especially in scenes with dynamic objects, ambiguous structure, and challenging camera motion. Our analysis further shows that the optimal injection stage is backbone-dependent: middle-layer integration is generally preferable for VGGT, while late-stage integration is more effective for π3. These results suggest that LUPI offers a practical way to inject motion and physical priors into feed-forward 3D reconstruction without increasing test-time complexity.

</details>


### [94] [LAMP: Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors for Training-Free Few-Shot Vision-Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22182)
*Zhipeng Ye,Feng Jiang,Qiufeng Wang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMP 提出了一个无需训练的框架，整合多层编码器的全局和局部特征，尤其适用于少量样本的适应任务。LAMP-F 进一步微调文本锚定的类原型，提升分类表现，尤其是在 ViT 和 ResNet 基础架构下的 4, 8 和 16 个样本的场景中。实验表明，多层特征融合是性能提升的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的少样本适应方法大多依赖于编码器的全局特征，而未充分利用中间层的补充信息。LAMP 和 LAMP-F 设计旨在克服此限制，通过结合多层特征增强少样本分类任务的效果。

Method: LAMP 使用了一种无需训练的框架，能够在单个前向传播过程中提供所有描述符。它独立地拟合了全局和支持校准的局部核预测器，并利用标记验证数据在类评分空间中选择它们的混合比例。LAMP-F 则进一步适应文本锚定点的类原型，并通过缓存响应精确更新固定内核残余预测。

Result: LAMP 在 ResNet-50 的 4、8 和 16 个样本场景中达到了最佳的平均准确率，分别达到 78.73%。此外，LAMP 相较于仅使用全局特征的基础线提高了 1.14 和 1.59 个百分点。LAMP-F 增加了这些优势，达到了 1.96 和 2.54 个百分点。具体而言，它在 ViT-B/16 基础架构下的最高中 16 个样本准确率为 83.57%，在 ResNet-50 下为 79.10%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，融合特定层的预测可以利用冻结编码器中的互补特征，从而改善少样本分类。未来的工作将探索如何更有效地整合这些预测，以进一步提升模型在少样本分类任务中的性能。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation often relies on an encoder's final global representation, leaving complementary information in intermediate layers underused. Combining these features is challenging because their dimensions can differ and their usefulness varies across tasks. We propose LAMP, Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors, a training-free framework that combines global and regional information from multiple encoder layers. A single forward pass per view supplies all descriptors. Global and support-calibrated regional kernel predictors independently fit the same label residuals. Labeled validation data select their mixture in class-score space, preserving native feature dimensions without learned projections. LAMP-F further adapts text-anchored class prototypes, using a cached response to update the fixed-kernel residual predictions exactly. Experiments cover eleven datasets and ViT and ResNet backbones. LAMP achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy among the compared published training-free ResNet-50 methods at 4, 8, and 16 shots, reaching 78.73% at 16 shots. Averaged across five support budgets, LAMP improves over a paired global-feature-only baseline by 1.14 and 1.59 percentage points on ViT-B/16 and ResNet-50; LAMP-F raises these gains to 1.96 and 2.54 points. LAMP-F reaches 16-shot accuracies of 83.57% and 79.10%, respectively. Controlled experiments identify multi-layer integration as the main source of improvement and examine pooling, calibration, validation labels, and computational cost. These findings show that combining layer-specific predictions can exploit complementary features from frozen encoders to improve few-shot classification.

</details>


### [95] [EVA-Cap: Optimizing Audiovisual Video Captioning via Event-Centric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22186)
*Jiapeng Shi,Qiuxia Hou,Jiaqi Leng,Junke Wang,Yanhao Zhang,Haonan Lu,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVA-Cap 提出了一种新的框架以事件为中心对音频视频事件进行分解和对齐，该方法在事件中心的评估指标上优于现有方法，同时在通用的音频视频字幕基准上也表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音频视频字幕方法未能利用视频固有的语义结构，导致音频视频对齐不佳。EVA-Cap 的目的是通过将字幕分解为基本的音频视频事件，并将非结构化的文本监督转化为事件中心的对齐，来弥合这一差距。

Method: EVA-Cap 通过 Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) 和 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 训练，使用事件为中心的数据集 EVA-Data 自行构建。在 GRPO 阶段，模型针对全局事件完整性、事件内归因和事件间同步三种事件中心对齐目标进行明确优化。

Result: EVA-Cap 在事件中心评估指标上显着优于现有方法（例如，在 ChronusAV 上平均增益为 9.0%），同时在通用的音频视频字幕基准上也取得了竞争性的表现（例如，在 WorldSense 上增益为 3.3%）。

Conclusion: EVA-Cap 是一种改进的音频视频字幕系统的框架，它通过事件的分解和对齐取得了显著的研究成果，证明了其在特定任务和通用基准上的优越性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Omni Language Models (OLMs) provide a unified solution for audiovisual video captioning. Nevertheless, existing approaches predominantly treat captioning as an unstructured text generation process, neglecting the inherent semantic structure of videos and leading to suboptimal fine-grained audiovisual alignment. To bridge this gap, we propose EVA-Cap, a novel framework that decomposes captions into atomic audiovisual events and transforms unstructured text supervision into event-centric alignment. We train EVA-Cap via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on a self-curated dataset, EVA-Data, which is built with event-centric quality control. During the GRPO phase, the model is explicitly optimized against three event-centric alignment targets: global event completeness, intra-event attribution, and inter-event synchronization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA-Cap significantly outperforms existing methods in event-centric evaluation (e.g., +9.0% average gain on ChronusAV), while achieving competitive performance on general audiovisual captioning benchmarks (e.g., +3.3% on WorldSense).

</details>


### [96] [OpenGCUA: Toward a Fully Open Stack for Generalist Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22200)
*Hanzhang Zhou,Long Li,Quyu Kong,Lei Wang,Panrong Tong,Long Chen,Tianyu Xia,Gongjie Zhang,Xu Zhang,Chen Liu,Jianan Zhang,Chenglin Cai,Zhengwen Zeng,Steven Hoi,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个开源的通用计算机使用代理OpenGCUA，该代理能够在跨视觉和编程接口的环境中完成任务，并且展示了与GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus 5等系统相比，其在任务执行上的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算机使用从GUI交互转向视觉和编程界面交互，研究人员定义了通用计算机使用代理（GCUA），并计划通过OpenGCUA项目开放这一领域，以便进行系统性研究。研究团队的目标是提高模型在真实设备上执行任务的效率，同时提供了一种编程接口，以便于不同设备之间的共通编程。

Method: 通过引入OpenGCUA，以及开源其实现、提示、模型评估轨迹、设备化载体和跨设备软件开发工具包，研究团队致力于在广泛的实际应用场景中测试和应用GCUA。

Result: 在OSWorld-v2的环境下，OpenGCUA的全配置能够显著提高所有八种评估模型的任务评分，平均提高了19.6分，并将每任务步骤减少了约53.0%。与批量GUI执行相比，这种改进体现在OpenGCUA代理能够动态执行中间结果，并允许模型直接应用其编程能力进行交互。

Conclusion: 这项研究表明了OpenGCUA在提高模型执行任务效率方面的潜力，并为未来的相关研究提供了基础和框架。

Abstract: Computer use has reached a turning point: the focus is expanding from GUI-only interaction to systems that complete tasks on users' computers through visual and programmatic interfaces. We define a generalist computer-use agent (GCUA) as a system in which the model chooses how to observe, act, and organize its work across visual and programmatic interfaces. Yet frontier implementations, such as GPT-6 Astra's computer-use system, remain largely closed, limiting systematic study of their design. We introduce Open Generalist CUA (OpenGCUA), an ongoing project to develop and open the full stack for GCUAs with the community. This report presents the first release of OpenGCUA. Code execution is a key feature of our agent design: it lets intermediate results determine subsequent operations and enables models to apply their existing programming capabilities directly to computer interaction. We also propose harness-to-model decision transfer, moving selected observations and task-state decisions from fixed harness rules to the model. On OSWorld-v2, our full configuration improves task scores across all eight evaluated models, including GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5, raising mean partial score by +19.6 points and reducing reported steps per task by an average of 53.0% relative to batched GUI-only execution. We release our implementation and prompts, evaluation trajectories from all eight models, and preliminary versions of a real-device harness and a cross-device SDK. The harness addresses real-device challenges not fully captured by benchmarks, while the SDK provides a common programming interface across computers, mobiles and browsers. Across four real macOS tasks under our real-device harness, selected Qwen3.8-Max runs use 19.4 times as many reported reasoning tokens as GPT-6 Astra, highlighting substantial differences between models in real-device execution efficiency.

</details>


### [97] [TRACE: Topology-Aware Reconnection via Prediction-Adaptive Connectivity Enforcement for Thin-Structure Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22210)
*Junho Moon,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE是一个拓扑感知、预测自适应的目标，它利用目标骨架定义所需的局部连接。对于每个连接，TRACE识别当前预测下的最容易的重新连接，并与相应的骨架点联合监督瓶颈。这项监督随着预测的演变而适应，而不是固定在中心线。此外，还引入了一种拓扑感知的骨架载体，能够同时保护前景和类特定的拓扑。广泛的实验表明，TRACE在维护高分割准确性的同时提高了连接性和拓扑，且训练开销较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于骨架的目标通常监督固定的中心线位置，不考虑可能恢复连通性的替代局部路径。TRACE旨在解决这一问题，通过定义所需的局部连接来提高连通性和拓扑。

Method: _TRACE通过识别当前预测下最容易的重新连接来适应性地监督局部连接的瓶颈。同时，还引入了一种能够保护前景和类特定拓扑的拓扑感知骨架载体。

Result: 在六个2D和3D基准测试上进行的实验表明，TRACE提高了连接性和拓扑，同时保持了强大的分割准确性，并且训练开销较低。

Conclusion: 总之，TRACE提供了一种有效的方法来改善模型的连通性和拓扑，同时保持预测性能。

Abstract: Thin, elongated structures are particularly sensitive to connectivity errors, as even a few local segmentation mistakes can disrupt otherwise continuous paths. Existing skeleton-based objectives typically supervise fixed centerline locations and do not account for alternative local routes that may restore connectivity. We propose TRACE, a topology-aware, prediction-adaptive objective that uses a target skeleton to define required local connections. For each connection, TRACE identifies the easiest valid reconnection under the current prediction and jointly supervises its bottleneck with the corresponding skeleton point. The supervision thus adapts to the evolving prediction rather than remaining fixed to the centerline. We further introduce a topology-aware skeleton carrier that preserves both foreground and class-specific topology, enabling reliable definition of these connections. Extensive experiments across six 2D and 3D benchmarks show that TRACE improves connectivity and topology while maintaining strong segmentation accuracy with low training overhead. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [98] [FutureLift: Lifting Trajectory Proposals into Bidirectional Future Graphs for Motion Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22215)
*Daniel Grimm,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FutureLift 提出了一种通用的未来预测校正方法，能够显著提高静态和联合预测的精度，无需额外的激光雷达数据或模型集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当使用现有的预测模型进行自动驾驶中的运动预测时，它们可能会留下一些误差，即使是很强大的预测模型也是如此。传统的校正方法要么压缩完整的预测方案，要么按顺序更新各个部分，这限制了它们提供一个包含所有预测结果的整体上下文来进行准确的校正。

Method: 未来提升 (FutureLift) 提出了一种与骨干网络无关的方法，这种方法将每个多模态提案表示为一个异构未来图，该图中的时间节点与代理历史、地图元素、替代模式以及在联合预测时其他代理的未来轨迹相连。双向时间边允许后一级据证据一次性修正早期段落。更改节点可以预测残余坐标和置信度修正。

Result: 在 Argoverse2 和 Waymo Open Motion 数据集上，FutureLift 始终改进了冻结提案，并在边缘预测和联合预测中都优于原生和专门的校正器。在 WOMD 运动预测排行榜上，相比其他标准输入方法，FutureLift 在 minADE (0.5049m) 和 minFDE (0.9942m) 上排名第一，而无需使用激光雷达或集成。

Conclusion: FutureLift 提供了一种通用方法来改进复杂的运动预测，而不需要依赖复杂的模型或额外的数据，这为自动驾驶技术提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Accurate motion prediction is critical for safe autonomous driving, yet even strong forecasters leave residual errors in their multimodal proposals. Existing refiners compress complete proposals or update their segments sequentially, preventing the full proposed future from serving as explicit relational context for correction. We introduce FutureLift, a backbone-agnostic refiner that represents each multimodal proposal as a heterogeneous future graph, with spatially grounded temporal nodes connected to agent histories, map elements, alternative modes, and, for joint prediction, other agents future trajectories. Bidirectional temporal edges allow later evidence to revise earlier segments in a single pass, and the updated nodes predict residual coordinate and confidence corrections. Requiring only proposals, scores, and scene context, FutureLift provides a common refinement interface for structurally different forecasting backbones. On Argoverse2 and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, it consistently improves frozen proposals and outperforms native and specialized refiners across marginal and joint forecasting. On the WOMD motion-prediction leaderboard, FutureLift ranks first in minADE (0.5049m) and minFDE (0.9942m) among the compared standard-input methods, without LiDAR or ensembling, while achieving a competitive 0.4874 Soft mAP.

</details>


### [99] [Skill-Decoupled On-Policy Expert Distillation for Unified Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22217)
*Yuqi Xiong,Chunyi Peng,Zhipeng Xu,Qing Yang,Zhenghao Liu,Zulong Chen,Yu Gu,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPED提出了一种技能解耦的在策略专家蒸馏方法，通过技能引导的监督促进了统一学生模型内部化程序知识，而无需在推理时提供显式的技能。这种方法在八个跨模态基准测试中优于基础模型和联合能力优化的RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的整合多模态任务特性的强化学习方法可能会引入跨能力梯度冲突。直接奖励优化可能会使特化知识与奖励作弊偏见交织在一起。ScoPeD旨在通过训练针对特定能力的专家，提取通过奖励引导的反思得来的具体技能，来解决这些问题。

Method: SCOPED方法首先训练特定能力的专家，然后通过奖励引导的反思提取明确的技能。在使用这些技能进行在线策略蒸馏时，这些技能引导专家监督，而这些技能对统一的学生模型不可用，以鼓励其在推理时不使用显式技能内部化程序知识。

Result: 在八个跨模态基准测试中，ScoPeD方法显著优于基础模型和联合能力优化的强化学习方法，展示了技能解耦的在线策略专家蒸馏对于整合独立获得的能力的有效性。

Conclusion: ScoPeD通过技能解耦的在线策略专家蒸馏方法成功解决了联合优化多模态任务特性带来的问题，改进了强化学习模型在不损失特化能力的情况下，能够有效学会程序知识。相关代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can elicit specialized visual capabilities in vision-language models (VLMs), yet jointly optimizing heterogeneous objectives often introduces cross-capability gradient conflicts. Training experts independently mitigates this interference, but direct reward optimization may entangle specialized knowledge with reward-hacking biases. This paper proposes Skill-Decoupled On-Policy Expert Distillation (SCOPED), which trains capability-specific experts and extracts explicit skills through reward-guided reflection. During on-policy distillation, these skills guide expert supervision while remaining unavailable to the unified student, encouraging it to internalize procedural knowledge without explicit skills at inference time. Across eight multimodal benchmarks, SCOPED consistently outperforms the backbone and mixed-capability RL, demonstrating the effectiveness of skill-decoupled on-policy expert distillation for integrating independently acquired capabilities. Further analyses reveal interference during joint capability optimization and suggest that skill augmentation promotes more consistent expert trajectories, greater attention to task-relevant visual evidence, and better preservation of specialized expertise in the unified student. All code and data are available at https://github.com/NEUIR/SCOPED.

</details>


### [100] [MagiC: Evaluating Multimodal Cognition Toward Grounded Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07297)
*Chengfei Wu,Ronald Seoh,Bingxuan Li,Liqiang Zhang,Fengrong Han,Dan Goldwasser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个名为MagiC的综合基准，旨在评估模型的接地多模态认知能力，涵盖超过5500个弱监督问答样例和900个人工标注样例，通过四个维度评估模型表现，并引入新的评估指标如MagiScore和StepSense，揭示了当前基于视觉推理的方法的关键局限和机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大规模视觉-语言模型虽然在视觉问答和多模态推理方面取得了显著性能，但这些模型是否真正地依赖于视觉证据进行合理推理仍然存在疑问。为了更好地理解模型的推理过程和能力，研究者提出了MagiC基准，以评估模型的多方面性能。

Method: 研究者设计并实现了MagiC基准，包括弱监督的问答样本和精细标注的人工样例。基准通过四个维度（最终答案的正确性、推理的有效性、着地点的准确性以及自我纠正能力）评估15种不同规模的视觉-语言模型。此外，还引入了一些新的诊断设置和评估指标，如MagiScore和StepSense。

Result: 实验结果显示，大多数模型在MagiC基准下的表现不尽如人意，揭示了当前方法中的一些重要局限。新的评估指标帮助研究者更好地了解并量化了模型在不同方面的表现。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，当前的视觉-语言模型在多模态推理方面仍然存在显著的局限性。研究者还提出了一些新的评估方法和指标，这对进一步改进视觉-语言推理模型具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models have led to impressive performance in visual question answering and multimodal reasoning. However, it remains unclear whether these models genuinely perform grounded visual reasoning or rely on superficial patterns and dataset biases. In this work, we introduce MagiC, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate grounded multimodal cognition, assessing not only answer accuracy but also the quality of step-by-step reasoning and its alignment with relevant visual evidence. Our benchmark includes approximately 5,500 weakly supervised QA examples generated from strong model outputs and 900 human-curated examples with fine-grained annotations, including answers, rationales, and bounding box groundings. We evaluate 15 vision-language models ranging from 7B to 70B parameters across four dimensions: final answer correctness, reasoning validity, grounding fidelity, and self-correction ability. MagiC further includes diagnostic settings to probe model robustness under adversarial visual cues and assess their capacity for introspective error correction. We introduce new metrics such as MagiScore and StepSense, and provide comprehensive analyses that reveal key limitations and opportunities in current approaches to grounded visual reasoning.

</details>


### [101] [How Much Physics Can Vision Learn from Audio? Probing and Distilling Damping Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22221)
*Zainab Afolabi,Vadim Selyutin,Zhao Gao,Babs Khalidson,Mohamed Ghoneim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究表明，音频嵌入能够更有效地排序材料的品质因子$Q$值，而视觉信息仅能提供有限的帮助。通过音频到视觉模型的迁移学习，可以在一定程度上提高视觉信息的排序能力，特别是在区分不同材料时显示出显著的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在探索如何利用物理属性的信息更好地进行决策，特别是在处理材料特性等需要物理理解的任务时。通过对比音频和视觉信息在排序材料品质因子方面的表现，以及通过迁移学习提高视觉信息的性能，实现跨模态的信息增强。

Method: 研究首先通过音频特征直接获取材料的品质因子$Q$值，然后利用音频嵌入的质量评估方法（如Spearman等级相关系数$ρ$）进行排序。接着，通过压缩音频模型的特征到视觉模型中，实现了跨模态的信息迁移和融合。最后，在保留测试材料的情况下，测试了不同输入帧数量对排序效果的影响。

Result: 实验中，音频嵌入方法在排序材料的品质因子方面表现出较高的效率，得到的$ρ$值达到0.73，而视频帧单帧信息的支持仅能实现$ρ$值0.28。通过将压缩的音频教师模型的特征嵌入到视觉模型中，实现了视觉模型性能的提升。在实验设置中采用“不使用保留材料”协议，发现即使在剔除了特定材料信息的情景下，压缩后四帧输入的$ρ$值提高了0.053（从0.234提升到0.338），这种改进是任何基于类别的策略所无法达到的。

Conclusion: 研究表明，音频信息在排序材料的品质因子方面具有显著优势，而视觉信息相对较弱。通过迁移学习实现了视觉信息性能的显著提升，特别是在具体区分不同材料时表现出更强的能力。

Abstract: Physical understanding is crucial for decision making, yet vision models struggle to infer properties such as damping that govern how materials behave. Audio encodes such properties directly, because an object's acoustic response to impact reveals its damping through measurable decay. We probe frozen encoders for the quality factor $Q$, a dimensionless measure of how many oscillations an object completes before its ring dies away, and report how well each embedding orders impacts by $Q$ using the Spearman rank correlation $ρ$, for which $ρ=1$ is a perfect ordering and $ρ=0$ an unrelated one. Audio embeddings reach $ρ=0.73$ and retain most of that advantage on materials withheld from training, while a single video frame taken at the moment of impact, encoded by CLIP, supports only $ρ=0.28$. Having established where the signal lives, we ask how much of that gap vision can recover. We distill a frozen audio teacher into a frozen CLIP backbone through a trained projection head and find that this recovers a useful fraction of the audio advantage. Because pooled correlations on this task are dominated in practice by category identity, we also evaluate under a leave-one-material-out protocol in which the held-out material is withheld from the projection head as well as the probe. Distillation with a four-frame input improves the correlation there from 0.234 to 0.338 (+0.053 over the matched four-frame baseline, 14 of 17 materials), a gain that no category-level strategy can produce. Widening the visual input to four frames spanning the impact supplies half of that improvement at no acoustic cost, which indicates that part of what a single frame misses is visible dynamics.

</details>


### [102] [WaveLLM: Wavelet-Domain LLM Entropy Modeling for Lossless Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22232)
*Yue Yu,Bowei Kang,Changsheng Gao,Dong Liu,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaveLLM通过将图像分解为多级小波子带，将其复杂的预测问题转化为更具结构化和统计同质性的子问题集合，从而提高熵建模效果，并使用专门为数值编码符号设计的文本标记接口和任务适应性提示策略，展示了在无损图像压缩中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无损图像压缩方法主要依赖于大型语言模型（LLM）的建模能力，但未能有效解决输入表示的可预测性问题，从而导致不同信号模式交织在一起。

Method: WaveLLM通过在LLAMA-3.2-3B的基础上进行LoRA适应，并结合小波变换和任务适应性的提示策略，设计了一种用于无损图像压缩的基于LLM的熵模型。

Result: WaveLLM在Kodak、CLIC.pro和DIV2K数据集上的bpsp值分别为2.51、2.24和2.27，分别优于基于PixelCNN的编解码器0.43、0.36和0.46 bpsp，并在所有三个基准上都优于最近的LLM基无损压缩方法。

Conclusion: 与传统的无损图像压缩方法相比，WaveLLM在提高熵建模效果和压缩性能方面表现出更高的潜力。

Abstract: Lossless image compression fundamentally relies on accurate entropy modeling, driving a recent shift toward large language model (LLM)-based methods. However, existing approaches mainly emphasize the modeling capacity of LLMs while directly serializing images into pixel or residual sequences, leaving heterogeneous signal patterns entangled within a single prediction stream. In this paper, we argue that effective entropy modeling depends not only on model capacity, but also on the predictability of the input representation. Motivated by this insight, we propose WaveLLM, a wavelet-domain LLM-based entropy model for lossless image compression. By decomposing an image into multi-level wavelet subbands, WaveLLM converts a heterogeneous prediction problem into a set of more structured and statistically homogeneous subproblems that are more amenable to autoregressive probability estimation. To bridge numerical coding symbols with the LLM's native text token space, we introduce a dedicated numerical interface featuring value embeddings and a coefficient prediction head. Furthermore, a task-adaptive prompting strategy is designed to enable a single shared model to adapt to diverse subbands within a unified framework. Built on Llama-3.2-3B with LoRA adaptation, WaveLLM achieves 2.51, 2.24, and 2.27 bpsp on Kodak, CLIC.pro, and DIV2K, respectively, outperforming the PixelCNN-based codec using the same wavelet transform by 0.43, 0.36, and 0.46 bpsp, and surpassing recent LLM-based lossless compressors on all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [103] [MMSearch-OPD: Easy Made Hard, Hard Made Learnable for Long-Horizon Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22240)
*Huanyao Zhang,Jiepeng Zhou,Jiaye Lin,Ontai Tang,Jingtian Hao,Mugeng Liu,Ru Peng,Lang Mei,Xiaohan Yu,Liyan Liu,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为MMSearch-OPD的新方法，结合了可控数据精炼与连贯的强化学习及在线政策蒸馏，以提升长期的跨模态深度搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的多模态深度搜索主要是基于短查询，训练难度高，缺乏有效的反馈机制。因此，提出MMSearch-OPD以克服这些限制。

Method: MMSearch-OPD采用了一种全新的方法，包括数据精炼流水线和联合强化学习与在线政策蒸馏。数据精炼模块通过参数化转换来扩展推理链，控制搜索深度并重用现有的图像-问题对。在训练过程中，结合答案奖励和行为轻量化惩罚引导强化学习，同时调整奖励差距来渐进地引导序列级指令。

Result: 实验结果表明，数据精炼方法能够带来性能提升，并且在自我训练（SFT）后进行联合训练能够进一步增强长期多模态深度搜索的能力。

Conclusion: MMSearch-OPD通过改进的数据处理方法及更高效的训练策略，提升了长期跨模态信息获取和推理的能力，有望在实际应用中实现更灵活和智能的搜索。

Abstract: Multimodal deep search is moving from short queries to long-horizon investigations that continually acquire evidence. However, training such agents remains constrained by limited challenging training questions and insufficient process feedback.
  We introduce MMSearch-OPD, combining controllable data refinement with joint reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). Our Refine pipeline performs targeted expansion of reasoning chains derived from existing simple multimodal questions. Parameterized transformations control search depth and cross-modal evidence requirements while reusing existing image--question pairs. For training, answer rewards and lightweight behavior penalties guide RL, while the reward gap modulates token-level teacher guidance on imperfect student trajectories. An exponentially decaying distillation coefficient reduces teacher dependence as training proceeds.
  Experiments show gains from data refinement and further improvements from joint training after SFT, indicating stronger long-horizon multimodal deep search capabilities.

</details>


### [104] [FPG-Seg: Annotation-free Retinal Vessel Topology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22259)
*James Willoughby,Irina Voiculescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FPG-Seg实现了无需标注的视网膜血管分割，其性能接近完全监督下的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要大量人工标注，耗时且依赖临床专家。FPG-Seg旨在提供一种无需标注的视网膜血管分割方法，以解决这一问题。

Method: FPG-Seg结合了经典的血管性筛选（vesselness filtering）、贝叶斯深度学习和拓扑意识图重建技术，实现无需手动注标的视网膜血管拓扑分割。

Result: FPG-Seg在四个常用视网膜血管分割基准数据集上的性能达到了0.74-0.78的clDice值，接近于无标注训练和超参数调优的情况下，等效完全监督模型的性能。

Conclusion: FPG-Seg为视网膜血管分割提供了一种新的无标注方法，该方法在多个基准数据集上表现出接近监督学习方法的性能，为减少标注需求提供了新的途径。

Abstract: State-of-the art approaches to retinal vessel segmentation typically require extensive annotations, a highly time-consuming process, reliant on clinical expertise. We present FPG-Seg, an annotation-free pipeline for retinal vessel segmentation combining classical vesselness filtering, Bayesian deep learning and topology-aware graph reconstruction to segment retinal vessel topology without manual labels. Tested across four widely used benchmark datasets for retinal vessel segmentation, FPG-Seg attains a clDice of $0.74{-}0.78$, representing $86{-}98\%$ of the performance of an equivalent fully-supervised baseline, despite using no manual annotations for training or hyperparameter tuning. As part of this work, we also provide, to our knowledge, the first performance benchmark of an annotation-free vessel segmentation method on FIVES.

</details>


### [105] [SCOPE: Semantic Bounded Credit Optimization for Prompt Engineering in Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22264)
*Yufei Jiao,Jinfa Huang,Zeyue Xue,Tianyu Xie,Yuexiao Ma,Rongrong Ji,Xiawu Zheng,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE 提出了一种基于 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 的方法，用于提升文本到图像生成任务，通过考虑语义结构优化策略更新权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法通常为图像生成器提供单个信号，但物体、数量、属性和关系构成有意义的语义跨度。SCOPE 旨在通过一种新的目标函数来解决这一问题，该函数能够更好地捕捉文本与图像间的语义联系。

Method: SCOPE 使用了基于 GRPO 的方法，通过联合生成增强的提示及其语义分割来优化策略。该方法通过在每个跨度内聚合策略概率变化来为每个跨度的令牌损失计算一个共享系数，同时为每个完成保留在单一的优势值。这种方法不需要额外的语义跨度奖励或将图像进行多次渲染。

Result: 实验结果表明，在 Qwen-Image、SD3 和 FLUX.1-dev 上，SCOPE 相比于传统的 GRPO 在 GeneVal 和 T2I-CompBench 上分别平均获得了 9.48% 和 7.62% 的相对增长。

Conclusion: 通过实验证明，语义边界和在跨越内进行聚合对于提升性能都是关键因素。这支持将语义结构作为强化学习提示工程的实用先验选择。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables language models to improve text-to-image (T2I) generation by rewriting prompts for a frozen image generator. Existing methods typically apply a single signal derived from the image to individual subword tokens, although objects, counts, attributes, and relations form coherent semantic spans. We propose SCOPE, an objective based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that aligns update weighting with this structure. The rewriter jointly generates an enhanced prompt and its semantic partition. SCOPE pools shifts in policy log probabilities within each span to derive a shared coefficient for its token losses while retaining a single advantage for each completion. It requires neither separate span rewards nor additional image renders. Across Qwen-Image, SD3, and FLUX.1-dev, SCOPE achieves average relative gains over vanilla GRPO of 9.48% on GeneVal and 7.62% on T2I-CompBench. Ablation studies show that both semantic boundaries and pooling within spans contribute to these gains, supporting semantic structure as a practical inductive bias for prompt engineering with RL.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Pairwise Repulsion: Collective Diversity via Determinant Maximization in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22267)
*Kwanyoung Lee,SeungJu Cha,Yebin Ahn,Sungho Koh,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DetNO的方法，通过最大化初始噪声上的RKHS体积来实现样本多样性，从而在文本到图像的任务中提供更广泛的模式覆盖。这种方法在多种模型和评估指标上优于现有的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像扩散模型在生成样本时倾向于集中在高密度区域，从而导致模式多样性不足，尤其是在合成数据增强任务中。作者为了提高合成数据的多样性，提出了DetNO以平衡多样性和质量。

Method: DetNO通过最大化初始噪声上的核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）体积来实现多样性的生成，最大化RBF核格拉姆矩阵的对数行列式。通过拉格朗日松弛，引入了一个带有单一超参数λ的双项目标函数，该参数控制着多样性和质量的权衡，同时保持优化后的噪声接近可能的区域。

Result: 在三种不同的模型（SD1.5、SDXL、SD3）上，DetNO在VendiScore和Mean Self-Similarity等指标上表现出领先的一致多样性。下游少量样本分类实验表明，广泛的模式覆盖可以为合成数据增强在少量样本情况下带来统计显著性的准确率提升。研究还表明，基于RBF的聚合器比InfoNCE表现更好，且随着批次大小增加，对数行列式的目标函数表现出更好的下游准确率。

Conclusion: DetNO通过最大化初始噪声的RKHS体积最大化，提供了多样性的生成，这在文本到图像的任务中产生了更强的模式覆盖，并在多个关键评估指标上优于现有技术。此方法无需对管道进行改动，仅通过批处理目标函数优化即可实现多样化生成。

Abstract: Strong guidance in text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models concentrates sampling toward high-density modes of the conditional distribution, collapsing independently sampled noise vectors onto nearly identical visual prototypes. This limits utility for tasks that demand distributional breadth, most critically synthetic data augmentation. Diversity, however, is a collective property of the whole batch: a set can preserve every pairwise distance yet still collapse onto a low-dimensional subspace. Existing sum-of-pairwise noise-optimization and repulsion objectives respond only weakly to this higher-order failure. We propose DetNO (Determinant Noise Optimization), which formulates diverse generation as RKHS volume maximization on the initial noise, maximizing the log-determinant of an RBF Gram matrix over one-step Tweedie predictions. This set-level functional equals the squared batch volume; its repulsion is strongest when the batch is most collapsed and self-attenuates as diversity is achieved. An anchor-based fidelity term keeps the optimized noises near plausible regions, and Lagrangian relaxation gives a two-term objective with a single hyperparameter $λ$ governing the diversity-quality trade-off, without training or sampler modification. Across three backbones (SD1.5, SDXL, SD3), DetNO achieves leading intra-batch diversity in VendiScore and Mean Self-Similarity, and a downstream few-shot classification experiment confirms that broader mode coverage brings statistically significant accuracy gains for synthetic data augmentation in the low-shot regime. Fixing the pipeline and varying only the batch objective, both RBF-based aggregators outperform InfoNCE, and the log-determinant attains the best downstream accuracy on both benchmarks. Its diversity advantage over InfoNCE widens as the batch grows. Code is available at https://github.com/mobled37/DetNO.

</details>


### [107] [Probing Negation Understanding in Vision-Language Models With Object Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20852)
*Beth Pearson,Michael Wray,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视-语言模型处理否定的能力，通过分析不同模型的注意力模式，并将模型与人类的眼动进行比较。研究发现，大多数模型在处理否定时并不遵循类似人类的注意力模式，表明它们依赖于注意力之外的机制。研究表明，某些模型在处理否定时存在空间偏向，这在下游任务中导致了性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言理解成为AI研究的核心，正确理解否定在视觉和语言之间的作用变得至关重要。现有VLMs在处理否定时存在不足，本文旨在揭示这些模型在处理否定过程中的机制差异，以期改进模型性能。

Method: 通过分析注意力模式和比较模型与人类的眼动，本文研究了VLMs在处理否定时的行为。对PaliGemma2-3B和LLaVA-1.5-7B模型进行了具体的层分析和头分析。

Result: 研究发现，大多数VLMs在处理否定时并未遵循类似人类的注意力模式，并且存在诸如空间偏向等问题。PaliGemma2-3B模型在处理否定时表现出了将注意力转移到另一种对象的倾向，而这一现象可能并非其处理否定的机制。LLaVA-1.5-7B模型则表现出对左侧对象的空间偏好。

Conclusion: 本文的研究发现，尽管部分模型在处理否定时表现出特定的注意力行为，但整体而言VLMs在处理否定时的表现仍有改进的空间。未来的研究需要进一步探究这些模型处理否定的机制，以期提高模型性能。

Abstract: Negation is a fundamental aspect of language understanding and it is important vision-language models (VLMs) are able to process it effectively. We investigate how VLMs process negation by analysing attention patterns of different models. We draw comparisons between human gaze and attention in models when processing affirmative vs negated statements. We compare object level model attention to human eye-fixations in both binary and multary settings, evaluating with both synthetic and realistic datasets. We find models generally do not follow human-like attention patterns when resolving negation, suggesting they rely on mechanisms outside of attention. While layerwise analysis shows that PaliGemma2-3B shifts object attention to the alternative under negation, our headwise ablations suggest this is a byproduct rather than a negation processing mechanism. Additionally, we find that LLaVA-1.5-7B displays a spatial bias towards left-side objects. Our evaluation of models on downstream tasks shows that all models have a discrepancy between affirmative and negated questions highlighting the need to better understand the underlying processing of negation to close this performance gap. Code available at https://github.com/otmive/negation_attention

</details>


### [108] [Unsupervised Clustering of Public Transport Vehicles Using Deep Morphological Features and Color Histograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22270)
*Juan Irving Vasquez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种结合形态学特征和手动设计的HSV颜色直方图的无监督聚类方法，用于公共交通车辆的分类。该方法在墨西哥城一段主干道路上的摄像机录制的1,460小时视频中进行了测试，最终生成了14个聚类，能够区分不同的公交线路和车辆类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市道路上的监控摄像头产生大量未标注的视频数据，可用于公共交通的监控，但缺乏有效的无监督分类方法。特别对于公共汽车，不同线路的车辆虽然车身结构相同，但颜色方案差异显著，传统的自监督方法难以有效区分类别。

Method: 本研究提出了一种结合形态学特征（通过冻结的DINOv2 Vision Transformer提取）和手工设计的HSV颜色直方图的无监督聚类方法。通过加权早期融合，然后利用UMAP投影和K-Means聚类完成分类。整个过程包括特征提取、特征融合、降维和聚类四个步骤。

Result: 该方法在墨西哥城一段主干道的7,972张巴士图像上得到了验证，结果显示该方法能够有效地区分不同的公交线路和车辆类型，且与仅使用形态学特征相比提高了目标簇的精确度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，颜色特征可以补充自监督特征，用于公交车辆的路线级聚类，但需要注意颜色特征不要主导特征表示。

Abstract: Surveillance cameras along urban roads produce large volumes of unlabeled video that could support the monitoring of public transport, but grouping the observed vehicles into meaningful categories without manual annotation remains challenging, since buses from different routes often share the same body structure and differ mainly in their color schemes. This study presents an unsupervised clustering methodology for public transport vehicles that combines morphological features extracted with a frozen DINOv2 Vision Transformer and handcrafted HSV color histograms through weighted early fusion, followed by UMAP projection and K-Means clustering. The method was evaluated on 7,972 bus images extracted from about 1,460 hours of video of an arterial road in Mexico City, with the goal of isolating the buses of the city's public bus network (RTP) without supervision. With a fusion of 65\% morphological and 35\% color features, the pipeline produced 14 clusters that separate distinct routes and vehicle types, and it increased the precision of the target cluster with respect to morphological features alone. These results show that color complements self-supervised features for route-level clustering, provided that it does not dominate the representation.

</details>


### [109] [MJev-Spatial: System One Spatial Intelligence via Native Attention Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22276)
*Luozheng Qin,Yuhan Zhu,Changhai Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Yuhua Zhou,Harry Zhang,Xiangyu Zeng,Yuxuan Liu,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MJev-Spatial 是一个无参数增益的统一分类框架，通过教师强迫和联合监督高效训练，显著提高了多种空间理解任务的表现并加速了推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 VLMs 通过自由形式文本生成答案，需要进行自回归解码和答案解析，尽管答案空间紧凑。MJev-Spatial 直接通过分类框架处理空间理解任务，避免了这一过程，以提高效率。

Method: MJev-Spatial 使用预训练的查询和键投影处理每种候选方案，并在粗到细分类中根据具体情况选择值区间或图像区域。通过单一前向传递监督所有阶段，结合长度感知全局批处理和跨 GPU 负载平衡策略进行训练。
