Result: MJev-Spatial 在各种空间理解任务中表现出色，达到最高整体分数（65.64%），在 12 个基准中的 7 个上排名第一，并且在所有 12 个基准上均超越其未修改的自回归基线模型，推理速度分别比最快速的自回归基线快 2.5 倍，以及原始基线的 5.6 倍。

Conclusion: MJev-Spatial 提供了一种高效处理空间智能任务的新方法，展示了在空间理解任务中的优越性能和高效推理能力。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence lets an agent perceive and act in the physical world, and many of its judgments must be made at interaction rates: which spatial relation holds, how far an object is, or where to place it. Vision-language models (VLMs) produce these answers by free-form text generation, paying for autoregressive decoding and answer parsing even though the output space is compact: one of a few options, a scalar, or a 2D point. In light of this, we introduce MJev-Spatial, a System 1 multimodal decision model that tackles all three types of spatial understanding tasks within a unified classification framework. Specifically, MJev-Spatial treats each candidate as a class and scores it using the backbone's pretrained query and key projections, without adding parameters. The candidates are the given options for choice tasks, or value bins and image regions refined through coarse-to-fine classification for numerical and point prediction, respectively. At inference, each classification decision takes a single forward pass and generates no answer tokens. To train on heterogeneous spatial data efficiently, we supervise all coarse-to-fine stages jointly with teacher forcing in one forward pass, and combine this with length-aware global batching and cross-GPU load balancing, raising training throughput by 30.8% over the original batching strategy. Built on Qwen3.5-4B and trained on 12.3M question-answer pairs converted from existing spatial datasets, MJev-Spatial achieves the highest overall score (65.64%) among eight tested models, ranks first on seven of twelve benchmarks, and outperforms its unmodified autoregressive base model on all twelve, while running 2.5 times faster than the fastest autoregressive baseline and 5.6 times faster than the base model.

</details>


### [110] [Routing Policy Optimization for Online Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22282)
*Qiuhong Shen,Yiding Yang,Yuxin Zhang,Xinwei Huang,Minxuan Lin,Bo Liu,Hao Kang,Haotian Yang,Yizhi Wang,Chongyang Ma,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RPO通过在生成器内部引入随机探索来提升视觉生成器的性能，同时保持其原始采样器的确定性，从而显著提高训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引入随机探索以改进目标对齐，同时维护视觉生成器的原始采样器确定性，提高训练效率。

Method: RPO使用注意力路由作为随机探索动作，在特定去噪阶段，每个查询块保持自身块并按生成器自身的注意力质量比例采样进一步的关键块，或者保持密集注意力。被采样的路径改变该过渡的速度，一个高斯概率围绕密集速度评估扰动，使用带有评分函数目标的受控双卷出估计器。

Result: 在文本到图像和文本到视频生成任务中，RPO提高了生成质量并实现了比Flow-GRPO样式方法高4倍的训练效率。

Conclusion: RPO提出了一种有效的策略，能够在保留原始采样器的同时，通过引入随机探索来增强生成器性能，并显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can align visual generators with reward signals beyond their pretraining objectives, but existing score-function methods typically introduce stochasticity by perturbing the reverse denoising trajectory, leading to costly rollout-based optimization. We instead ask whether stochastic exploration can be introduced inside the generator while preserving its original sampler. Routing Policy Optimization (RPO) answers this question by turning attention routing into a stochastic exploration action. At a few selected denoising levels, each query block keeps its own block and samples further key blocks in proportion to the generator's native attention mass, or keeps dense attention. The sampled route perturbs the velocity of that transition, and a Gaussian likelihood around the dense velocity scores the perturbation with a score-function objective. RPO further uses a controlled two-rollout estimator: two rollouts share the same prompt, initial latent, routed denoising levels, and dense anchor layers, differing only in their sampled attention routes. Their reward difference provides an advantage signal for the routed transitions. Because the likelihood differentiates the dense velocity itself, the learned updates remain effective after routing is removed. At inference, RPO restores dense attention and retains the original deterministic sampler. Experiments on text-to-image and text-to-video generation show that RPO improves generation quality while achieving nearly 4x higher training efficiency than Flow-GRPO-style methods. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/rpo for visual results.

</details>


### [111] [Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22295)
*Jay N. Paranjape,Jurijs Nazarovs,Rohan Sarkar,Yue Guo,Lipeng Ke,Sankar Venkataraman,Vishal M. Patel,Nataraj Jammalamadaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究引入了Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition (GHADAR) 任务，旨在更全面地描述视频中每个人的特点和活动，提出了一个包含开放集描述的新数据集，提出了一种改进的训练方法，并提出了一种改进的评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多人群活动识别数据集简化了问题，导致模型难以推广。研究旨在通过识别视频中每个人的特征和活动来解决这一局限性。

Method: 研究提出了一个新的数据集，该数据集来自AVA-Actions，产生了开放集描述，还提出了一种新的训练方法，通过在训练中约束跨注意力掩模，以有效利用定位信息。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在该任务上优于现有的SOTA视觉语言模型。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一个强健的框架，包括数据集、新颖的方法和评估方案，以加强未来在该领域的研究基础。

Abstract: Understanding characteristics and activities of individuals in complex multi-person environments is crucial for real-world applications. However, existing datasets often simplify this problem to grounded group activity recognition, single-person activity recognition, or close-set activity recognition, thus limiting the generalizability of models trained on these datasets. In this work, we introduce the task of Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition (GHADAR), which involves describing the characteristics and activity descriptions of \textbf{every} person in a video, provided the location of the person in the video, which is more practical for real-world applications. To facilitate this, we introduce a new dataset derived from AVA-Actions by generating open-set captions for the person description and activity. In addition, we propose a novel method to effectively utilize the information contained in grounding during training by constraining the cross-attention masks during training in VLMs to improve performance for this task. Our experiments show that our method outperforms SOTA VLMs on this task. Finally, we demonstrate the limitations of existing evaluation metrics, which are overly reliant on human-annotations and exact text-text matching. As an added video-based evaluation, we propose a holistic VLM-based evaluation schema that compares concepts {\em directly} between the video and the generated predictions. Thus, in this work, we develop a complete framework for GHADAR, including a dataset, a novel method and an evaluation schema, thereby establishing a strong foundation for future research in this domain.

</details>


### [112] [ARC-SORT: Adaptive, Robust, Compensated Multi-Object Tracking with Interacting Multiple Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22306)
*Girish Kumar Adari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARC-SORT 是一种适用于体育广播中的多目标跟踪的实时检测跟踪器，能够适应性地扩展搜索区域，并在检测到错误关联时进行矫正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在体育广播中进行多目标跟踪具有挑战性，因为运动员移动速度快且方向不固定，同时摄像头会进行快慢变焦。为了解决这些问题，研究人员提出了 ARC-SORT 方法。

Method: ARC-SORT 使用一个交互式多重模型（IMM）卡尔曼滤波器，为每个跟踪对象生成预测协方差，并将其转换为搜索区域大小变化，以适应预测的不确定性。当跟踪对象发生交叉时，使用跟踪成本自身来判断是否需要矫正。系统还包含一个离线重新识别步骤，在运动员暂时离开视域时仅通过外观特征进行关联。

Result: ARC-SORT 在 SportsMOT 测试集上达到了 81.82 的 HOTA，并且是迄今为止最少数次识别交换方法之一。在 DanceTrack 测试集上，它达到了 62.1 的 HOTA，相同的关联设置下。该方法在 RTX 3060 笔记本 GPU 上大约运行 50 帧/秒。

Conclusion: ARC-SORT 通过不断调整搜索区域大小和在必要时进行的矫正，大幅提升了多目标跟踪的性能，解决了快速变化场景下的实时处理问题。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking in sports broadcasts is hard for two reasons: athletes move fast and change direction abruptly, and the camera pans and zooms at the same time. We present ARC-SORT (Adaptive, Robust, Compensated SORT), a tracking-by-detection tracker that trains nothing and runs in real time. Its design rule: the tracker measures its own uncertainty, and that measurement decides how it acts. An Interacting Multiple Model (IMM) Kalman filter gives every track a predictive covariance, and we turn it into a per-track search region: the less reliable the motion prediction, the larger the region in which a detection may match (uncertainty-adaptive Expansion IoU, EIoU). On identical inputs, this continuous coupling is what matters: the same covariance used as a hard Mahalanobis gate hurts at every threshold we swept, and richer covariance summaries add nothing measurable. When two tracks cross, the assignment cost itself reports whether the decision was close. ARC-SORT keeps such pairs under review and applies at most one corrective identity swap once post-crossing appearance evidence shows the first decision was wrong; this adds +1.0 HOTA (higher order tracking accuracy) on both the SportsMOT and DanceTrack test sets at under a millisecond per frame. For players who leave the frame for seconds, motion extrapolation carries no information, so our offline re-identification drops the usual motion gate and links tracklets by appearance alone. ARC-SORT reaches 81.82 HOTA on the SportsMOT test set with the fewest identity switches of the 69 leaderboard entries as of August 2026 and tied-third multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA, 97.09), and 62.1 HOTA on DanceTrack test with the same association settings. On Deep-EIoU's public detections and embeddings it reaches 80.31 HOTA, +3.1 over Deep-EIoU. It runs at about 50 frames per second on a laptop RTX 3060 GPU. Code will be released.

</details>


### [113] [Velox: Spatial Memory Makes World Models Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22311)
*Qiuhong Shen,Shizun Wang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Velox 提出了一种无需训练的注意力路由方法，利用流式几何的视锥切块对应关系，在区域级别选择上下文。该方法不仅加速了注意力机制和去噪过程，还在保持模型准确性的同时，避免了随视觉翻转长度增加而增长的注意力项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的分块视频扩散模型通常采用增长的 KV 缓存来保留上下文，导致后期分块生成逐渐变慢，成为性能瓶颈。本文旨在提出一种新的注意力路由机制，来解决这一问题。

Method: Velox 通过视锥切块对应关系，在区域级别选择上下文。它将历史分块表示为具有深度区间的摄像机视锥切块，使用近期优先的多层展开方式获得当前块的深度。对于覆盖良好的区域，视锥切块重叠以软性调节每层分块的重要性；对于未覆盖的区域，保留模型驱动的路线，避免几何偏见。该方法的固定标记-块粒度与 FlashAttention 样式的分块执行方式相匹配，减少随视觉翻转长度增加的注意力项。

Result: 测试结果表明，Velox 在 chunk 48 时，加速了注意力机制 $4.36	imes$ 和去噪过程 $2.43	imes$。在完整的 158 案例 WBench 导航分割中，其表现与 dense LingBot-Fast-Cam 相差在 0.15 API 自由平均点以内。

Conclusion: Velox 的提出为交互式视频模拟提供了一种高效解决方案，同时保持了模型的准确性，并克服了视觉序列长度增加带来的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Video world models turn actions and camera motion into long visual rollouts, making video a natural interface for interactive simulation. Such interaction requires responsive generation, yet coherent rollouts must retain visual memory of what has already been generated. Chunked video diffusion models usually keep this context in growing KV caches, so later chunks become progressively slower. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Velox, a training-free attention router for region-level context selection using frustum slab correspondences from streaming geometry. Velox represents historical blocks as camera frustum slabs with depth intervals and obtains current-block depth through recency-priority multiwarp. For well-covered regions, frustum slab overlap softly modulates each layer's model-derived block importance. Uncovered regions retain the model-derived route without geometric bias. The fixed token-block granularity matches FlashAttention-style tiled execution and reduces the attention term that grows with rollout length. Frustum slab construction runs asynchronously with denoising on the same H200. At chunk 48, Velox accelerates attention by $4.36\times$ and denoising by $2.43\times$. On the full 158-case WBench navigation split, it remains within 0.15 API-free average points of dense LingBot-Fast-Cam. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/velox/ for more details.

</details>


### [114] [Not Just a Subject: Capturing Adaptive Scene-Specific Variance for Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22320)
*SeungJu Cha,Ye-Chan Kim,Kwanyoung Lee,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EOS-Story 提出了一种无需训练的框架，以实现身份一致性和场景特定适应性，改进了文本到图像生成，特别是在视觉叙述中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本转图像生成方法主要关注身份保持，但在多样场景变化面前表现出色不足，导致语义对齐问题和结构刚性。

Method: 该方法通过在文本空间中拆分叙述为身份和场景子提示，并在视觉空间中使用自适应身份保护来解决上述问题。具体包括拆分机制和交叉上下文桥接来防止信息稀释，并通过适应性身份保护来调整身份特征以匹配场景的特定比例。此外，通过扩展基准测试使其包括多种构图场景来评估表现。

Result: 该方法在视觉叙述中实现了竞争力或优越的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提出的方法为改进视觉叙述中的文本到图像生成提供了新的视角和解决方案，具体体现在身份一致性和场景适应性的处理上。

Abstract: Recent advancements in diffusion models have significantly improved text-to-image generation, enabling their application to visual storytelling, where coherent image sequences are generated from narrative prompts. However, existing methods primarily focus on identity preservation and often fail to adapt to diverse scene variations, resulting in semantic misalignment and structural inflexibility. We propose EOS-Story, a training-free framework that harmonizes identity consistency with scene-specific adaptation across text and vision spaces. In the text space, we decouple the narrative into identity and scene sub-prompts to prevent information dilution, but this leaves the scene without access to appearance cues. To bridge this gap, we introduce Cross-Contextual Bridging, which softly injects a phrase-level appearance residual into the scene's <EOS> embedding. In the vision space, we find that scale mismatch between identity and scene subjects causes a few identity tokens to be repeatedly transferred, producing structural artifacts. We address this with Adaptive Identity Preservation, which anchors identity features at the scene-specific scale (Identity Anchoring) and integrates them via Identity-Aware Self-Attention. To evaluate performance under varied spatial compositions, we extend the Consistory+ benchmark with diverse framing scenarios (Consistory+_+). Extensive experiments show that EOS-Story achieves competitive or superior performance on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [AVC-Reward: Event-Centric Audio-Video Consistency Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22321)
*Yuruo Wu,Kaihang Pan,Kai Shen,Jiacheng Li,Shuyuan Tu,Jisheng Dang,Li Tuoyu,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种事件中心的评估框架AVC-Reward，用于评估音频-视频生成模型，该框架能更好地处理复杂场景并评估全局叙事和谐、事件级时间同步和细粒度属性对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估方法更适合于简单且单向的任务，不足以评估日益复杂的声音-视频场景。因此，本文提出了事件中心的评估框架AVC-Reward，以更全面地评价生成模型。

Method: 该框架在事件级别分离音频和视频事件，独立分析每个事件，跨模态验证关系，并将判断结果归一化为从粗到细的三层次一致性度量。

Result: AVC-Reward 提供了一种通用公式用于音频-视频一致性评估的不同生成设置和跨模态关系中。通过对不同音频-视频生成模型生成的多样正面和负面样本构建的AVC-Benchmark进行评估，AVC-Reward 表现出了优于其他自动化方法的临界属性一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的AVC-Reward提供了一种进行音频-视频生成模型评估的有效框架，该框架能够帮助改进音频-视频的一致性，特别是在使用在线强化学习信号改善模型表现方面具有优势。

Abstract: As joint audio-video generation models become more capable, they can generate audio-visual scenes exhibiting three forms of complexity: multi-event composition, multi-layer audio mixture, and asymmetric cross-modal relations. However, current evaluations largely reuse metrics from earlier simple and unidirectional tasks. They typically evaluate an audio-video clip as a whole and rely on simple predefined relations, making them unsuitable for these increasingly complex scenes. We propose an event-centric evaluation framework that disentangles audio and visual events at the event level, analyzes each event independently, cross-validates relations across modalities, and aggregates the judgments into a coarse-to-fine, three-level consistency metric. The metric assesses global narrative harmony, event-level temporal synchronization, and fine-grained attribute alignment, providing a general formulation for audio-video consistency evaluation across diverse generation settings and cross-modal relations. We instantiate the metric as AVC-Reward, a trainable audio-video reward model. To evaluate the agreement between automatic judgments and human perception across generation paradigms and relation complexities, we construct AVC-Benchmark, which is primarily built from diverse positive and negative samples generated by various types of audio-video generation models. AVC-Reward achieves state-of-the-art alignment with human judgment among automatic methods, provides interpretable rationales that localize failures, and improves the audio-video consistency of a joint generation model when used as an online reinforcement-learning signal. Project page: https://tsuki0512.github.io/AVC-Reward.github.io/

</details>


### [116] [Dense4D: Pixel-Level 4D Human Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22325)
*Rıza Alp Güler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dense4D是一种新颖的端到端模型，用于从单目RGB图像中重建人体在身体、衣物和头发上的密集4D对应，无需身体模板，显著提高了跟踪精度并展示了一致的密集对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数4D人体重建方法都需要预先存在的身体模板或准确的相机姿态估计，而Dense4D通过联合处理人体中心裁剪图像来预测人体点图（HPM）和深度，从而无需这些先前提要，直接从单目视角进行4D对应。

Method: Dense4D首先对特定于人体几何结构的人像中心裁剪图像进行处理，通过已知的图像到裁剪图的变换来预测人体点图和深度。这些图通过匹配各帧之间的坐标建立无须预测相机姿态或大范围场景位移的对应关系，并将每个图像的深度用于提升匹配至3D轨迹。

Result: 在实际人的实验中，Dense4D在跟踪准确性方面表现出了显著的改进，与扫描表面具有更高的契合度，并且通过不同帧中点的组合展示了在变化和穿着下的人体几何保持一致性。

Conclusion: Dense4D为4D人体重建提供了一种新的视角，通过端到端的模型直接从单目图像进行对应预测，为未来的无人驱动4D重建技术铺平了道路。

Abstract: Reconstructing human motion in 4D requires identifying the same surface points through articulation, deformation and changes in visibility. We introduce Dense4D, to our knowledge the first feed-forward model dedicated to dense 4D correspondence across the body, clothing and hair from a set of monocular RGB images, without a body template. Trained specifically for human geometry, Dense4D jointly processes person-centred crops with known image-to-crop transforms to predict Human Point Maps (HPMs) and depth for each image. An HPM assigns each person pixel the 3D position of its observed surface point at a common reference time. These maps support amodal correspondence: points hidden in the reference image can be localised from observations in other frames. Matching HPM coordinates across crops establishes correspondence without predicting camera poses or large scene displacements; per-image depth then lifts the matches into 3D tracks. Experiments on real people demonstrate substantially higher tracking accuracy and closer agreement with scanned surfaces than the evaluated baselines. Points from different frames assemble into coherent human geometry in the reference pose, providing visible evidence of consistent dense correspondence through articulation and clothing deformation. Additional qualitative results, videos, and an interactive viewer are available at https://alpguler.com/dense4d.

</details>


### [117] [ACEdit: Stable Multi-Turn Image Editing via Asymmetric Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22328)
*Sara Dorfman,Nir Goren,Gal Barak,Daniel Cohen-Or,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种训练方案，使图像编辑在多次编辑下保持稳定，通过强制编辑器在其先前输出上操作来实现自我强化，同时引入了一种去分布偏移的策略，并通过学生-教师模型架构提供可靠的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的图像编辑模型在多次编辑下表现不佳，因为每次编辑都会引入累积的分布偏移，导致输入分布与原始训练数据不匹配。为了解决这一问题，研究提出了一种新的训练方案。

Method: 该方法的关键是使编辑器在训练过程中在其自身之前的输出上操作，引入了不对称的条件方案，其中学生模型在自己的先前输出上进行逐次编辑，而教师模型则保持与原始干净图像的联系并接收复合编辑指令。通过这种方式，该方法利用了一个教师图像编辑器来提供可靠的学习信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，在长多回合序列中显著减少了退化现象，同时保持了编辑质量和指令的遵从性。

Conclusion: 研究人员提出了一种有效的自我强化机制来应对多次编辑过程中的退化问题，同时展示了该方法在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Image editing models produce impressive results for individual edits, but their quality often deteriorates over multiple editing turns. Each editing turn introduces small distribution shifts that accumulate over successive edits, creating a gap between the input distributions encountered during the editor's original training and multi-turn inference. We introduce a training scheme that makes multi-turn image editing stable under this accumulating distribution shift. A key is to force the editor to operate on its own outputs from previous editing turns during training, bringing the principle of Self-Forcing to image editing. The main challenge is that self-generated editing trajectories deviate from ground-truth trajectories, making direct supervision unreliable. We address this challenge through a distillation objective that leverages a teacher image editor to provide a reliable training signal when the model operates on its own previous outputs. We introduce an asymmetric conditioning scheme: while the student performs edits sequentially on its own outputs, the teacher remains anchored to the original clean image and receives a compound editing instruction. Experiments show substantially reduced degradation over long multi-turn sequences while maintaining editing quality and instruction adherence.

</details>


### [118] [3DCodeVerse: Exploring 3D Worlds as Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22341)
*Yipeng Gao,Ziyao Zeng,Jinfa Huang,Yijiang Li,Haolin Xiong,Wang Qin,Xinlei Yu,Youheng Yao,Jie Zhang,Kun-Yu Lin,Rong Liu,Yinyuan Zhao,Yajie Zhao,Meiqi Guo,Jiebo Luo,Lei Shu,Yunhao Ge,Xinyu Hu,Laurent Itti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3DCodeVerse是一个大型研究生态系统，包含了一个大规模、高质量的可执行3D代码数据集，一个用于生成和优化3D内容的多智能体框架，以及对3D编程的系统性研究，展示了其显著提高开源模型的3D编码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，随着虚拟现实和增强现实技术的发展，3D内容的生成和验证变得越来越重要。然而，现有的方法通常缺乏大规模数据集、自动化验证机制和系统性指导。3DCodeVerse旨在解决这些问题，以促进更加高效和可靠的3D内容创建。

Method: 3DCodeVerse的方法包括构建大规模数据集、开发多智能体框架和进行系统性实验。首先，通过收集开源数据、转换专有表示和生成新程序，构建了一个大规模的可执行3D代码数据集。其次，开发了一个多智能体定向无环图框架，用于生成和改进3D内容，该框架通过执行检查、空间测量和视觉反馈实现自动化评估和程序修正。最后，通过使用该开源模型对数据集进行微调和组件级分析，进行系统性研究。

Result: 3DCodeVerse达到了显著提高开源模型的3D编码能力的效果。实验表明，微调后的模型在3D图形生成上表现优于开源基础上的模型。此外，3DCodeVerse框架在与Claude Design（3D对象生成器）的评比中，以90%的胜率超过后者。

Conclusion: 3DCodeVerse作为一个创新的编程平台，对于促进大规模、高质量的可执行3D内容的生成和验证具有重要意义，为未来的3D图形生成和自动化验证提供了有力支持。

Abstract: We introduce 3DCodeVerse, a research ecosystem that enables coding agents to create editable and verifiable 3D content at scale. 3DCodeVerse comprises large-scale, high-quality datasets of executable 3D code, a coding harness for spatial reasoning and automated verification, and practical guidance for 3D coding. First, we construct 3DCodeVerse-2M, a large-scale curated dataset of standalone 3D executable code. We build this corpus by collecting open-source data, converting specialized representations, and generating new programs with coding agents. Each program is packaged with the source files and associated assets required for execution in its declared environment, along with available descriptions and verification records. Second, we develop the 3DCodeVerse Harness, a multi-agent directed acyclic graph framework for generating and refining 3D content. It uses execution checks, spatial measurements, and visual feedback to automatically evaluate and revise generated programs without human feedback during generation. Third, we conduct systematic empirical studies of 3D coding, establishing baselines with fine-tuned open models and deriving practical guidance through component-level analyses of our harness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning on our dataset significantly improves the 3D coding capabilities of open-source models. Notably, our 3DCodeVerse harness achieves a 90% win rate against Claude Design (3D Object) in pairwise human evaluations, when both are utilized by Claude Opus 5.5.

</details>


### [119] [GeoState: Learning View-Decoupled Geometry States for 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22343)
*Hongchang Liu,Jian Liu,Yujuan Ding,Liang Peng,Xun Jiang,Fei Ma,Xing Xu,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoState 提出了一种视图无关的几何表示法，用于预测 3D 世界建模，通过分离场景状态与相机视角，提升了场景状态演变的预测和跨视图查询的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 3D 世界模型依赖于几何基础模型（GFMs）的特征，虽然这些特征来自丰富的几何先验，但它们与视角相关，使得场景动态与相机引起的变异交织在一起，增加了预测场景状态演变的难度。

Method: GeoState 通过一种场景标记化的方法，将多视角的 GFM 特征聚合形成一个视图无关的场景状态，然后通过几何解码器和自回归世界模型来进行场景状态的预测和未来场景状态的解码。

Result: 实验结果表明，GeoState 在 Waymo 上的表现优于先前的 3D 世界模型，支持几何上一致的跨视角查询，并且能够在 KITTI 和 nuScenes 上实现零样本泛化。

Conclusion: GeoState 提出了一种有效的方法来解决 3D 世界模型中存在的视角相关性问题，展示了其在多种场景中的优越表现和灵活性。

Abstract: Recent 3D world models increasingly use features from geometry foundation models (GFMs) as predictive world states, benefiting from their rich geometric priors. However, the view dependence of these features entangles scene dynamics with camera-induced variation, making the resulting state evolution harder to predict. We propose GeoState, a view-decoupled geometry representation for predictive 3D world modeling that separates scene-state evolution from camera-dependent observation. Specifically, a scene tokenizer aggregates multi-view GFM features into a view-decoupled scene state, which a geometry decoder queries using camera rays. An autoregressive world model then predicts future scene states, while camera motion is modeled separately. The predicted states can therefore be decoded under either predicted or externally specified cameras, while retaining a single context-derived scale throughout the rollout without per-frame scale alignment. Experiments show that GeoState outperforms prior 3D world models on Waymo, supports geometrically consistent cross-view querying, and generalizes zero-shot to KITTI and nuScenes. Project page: https://hongchang-liu.github.io/GeoState-project-page/

</details>


### [120] [Speculative Forcing for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22347)
*Hyunwook Choi,Seungryong Kim,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Speculative Forcing 提出了一种框架，将中间清理估计视为草稿，并预测何时可以安全地提前接受单个令牌。这减少了不必要的去噪步骤，从而提高速度，同时保持生成视频的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多步自回归视频生成器在每个时间步骤对所有令牌都进行最终去噪，而实际上许多令牌在早期步骤已达到接近最终输出的质量，因此进行这些步骤是不必要的。

Method: Speculative Forcing 使用一种新颖的方法，通过预测何时可以安全地接受早期的令牌来减少不必要的去噪步骤。引入了两种验证器设计：一种基于与干净内容的相似性和步骤间的稳定性；另一种通过读取冻结生成器的中间特征来估计每个令牌的剩余差距。

Result: Speculative Forcing 在短视频、长视频以及世界生成上保持了全步采样的质量，同时显著减少了计算量。在 LongLive 上，它与四步生成器的效果相当，但只需约1.7个去噪步骤，运行速度提高超过1.5倍。在 Lingbot-World 2.0 上，即使相机运动不断改变可见内容，它也能够以约2.5个去噪步骤保持质量，运行速度提高1.35倍。

Conclusion: Speculative Forcing 提供了一种有效的策略来优化自回归视频生成，在保持视频质量的同时提高了计算效率。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generators enable streaming generation, yet they run every token through the final denoising step, regardless of how much refinement it needs. We find this uniform computation is often unnecessary: many tokens already closely match their eventual full-step outputs in the initial steps, while others require refinement until the end. We introduce Speculative Forcing, a framework that treats intermediate clean estimates as drafts and predicts when individual tokens can be safely accepted early. Once accepted, a token is frozen as its final output and undergoes no further denoising, while its cached keys and values remain available as context for the remaining tokens. We explore two alternative verifier designs: a training-free one based on similarity to clean content and stability across steps, and a learned one that reads the frozen generator's intermediate features to estimate each token's remaining gap to its full-step output. Across short-video, long-video, and world generation, Speculative Forcing preserves the quality of full-step sampling while substantially reducing computation. On LongLive, it matches the four-step generator with ~1.7 denoising steps per token, running over 1.5x faster at 41.7 and 37.8 fps. On Lingbot-World 2.0, where camera motion continually changes the visible content, it maintains quality with ~2.5 denoising steps per token and runs 1.35x faster at 24.5 fps.

</details>


### [121] [SUPRA-TEX: 3D Generation with Super-Aligned Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22352)
*Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Fanpeng Meng,Zihao Huang,Jichen Pang,Guanjun Wu,Zhikuan Bao,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUPRA-Tex通过将可见材料估计、表面对齐和观测指导的3D完成分离来解决现有方法中的挑战，实现与参考图像高度对齐的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图像3D生成方法存在融合多视角预测时引入伪影或难以保留细节点细节的问题，SUPRA-Tex旨在通过将这些任务分离，使得3D生成器能够在有限的3D数据上专注于保留可见证据和完成未观察到的表面。

Method: 首先训练一个基于MLLM的图像编辑模型预测参考视角下的照明分离PBR图，然后将这些图映射到可见表面上并通过预训练的材料VAE编码器编码为特定的数据生成目标的观察拉格朗日量。交替更新未观察区域和全表面生成完整材料拉格朗日量。

Result: SUPRA-Tex在公共数据集上训练，达到了在几何条件纹理和端到端资产生成中的SOTA性能，并在野外图像上与顶级商业系统相媲美。

Conclusion: 总之，SUPRA-Tex通过独特的设计解决了以往工作的不足，实现了高质量的3D图像生成。

Abstract: Single image 3D generation aims to dress a given 3D mesh with relightable materials while faithfully preserving the visual details of a reference image. Existing methods either introduce artifacts when fusing inconsistent multi-view predictions or struggle to preserve fine details through implicit image-feature conditioning. To resolve these challenges, we introduce SUPRA-Tex, built on the principle of decoupling visible-material estimation, surface alignment, and observation-guided 3D completion. This division of labor allows the 3D generator, trained on limited 3D data, to focus its capacity on preserving visible evidence and completing unobserved surfaces. Specifically, we first train an MLLM-based image editing model to predict lighting-separated PBR maps in the reference view. We then project these maps onto the visible surface and encode them with the pretrained material VAE encoder into native observation latents that share the generation target's latent space. An interleaved flow transformer holds these latents as fixed, read-only anchors while alternating unobserved-region and full-surface updates to generate complete material latents. This design achieves super-alignment with the reference, delivering What You See Is What You Get fidelity on visible regions and coherent completion on unobserved surfaces. Trained solely on public datasets, SUPRA-Tex achieves SOTA performance in both geometry-conditioned texturing and end-to-end asset generation, matching or even exceeding leading commercial systems on in-the-wild images. Project page: https://supra-3d.github.io/supratex/

</details>


### [122] [DK-VLM: Geometry as Position for Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22356)
*Han Su,Meng Tian,Tianyu Huang,Mingyu Gao,Lennox Wei,Dongxu Li,Jianhua Han,Hang Xu,Xiaohe Wu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的视觉语言模型DK-VLM，它通过双核注意机制直接将几何信息引入注意力机制，而不是融合3D重建特征，并引入了参考帧组成监督来提高多步空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉-语言模型在理解和更新跨视角的3D空间关系方面仍然存在困难，尤其是在有限的空间监督条件下，难以将3D重建特征与预训练的语义表示对齐。

Method: 提出的DK-VLM模型使用双核注意机制，其中包含一个语义内核保留了预训练模型的固有注意和位置编码，以及一个几何内核使用估计的3D点和相机姿态直接通过点位移和相对视图变换调制注意力。一个学习的门控机制结合了它们的输出。此外引入了参考帧组成监督，提供逐步旋转和方向目标，从验证的一步注释中导出。

Result: DK-VLM-8B在六个空间基准测试中取得了最高的平均得分，超越了其他方法，并且在VSI-Bench和ReVSI-32上的总体得分分别为74.33和58.28。此外，参考帧组成监督进一步提高了多步空间推理能力，而完整的模型保持了对视频的通用理解。

Conclusion: 这种新的方法能够更有效地理解复杂的3D空间关系，并且在多种基准测试中显示出优越的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models still struggle to understand 3D spatial relations across views and update spatial judgments as the observer moves. A common approach fuses 3D reconstruction features into visual tokens, but aligning these features with pretrained semantic representations remains challenging under limited spatial supervision. We propose DK-VLM with Dual-Kernel Attention, which computes attention from both a visual token's input position and its geometric position in the scene. A semantic kernel retains the pretrained model's native attention and positional encoding, while a separate geometric kernel uses estimated 3D points and camera poses to directly modulate attention through point displacements and relative view transformations. Then, a learned gate fuses their outputs. Geometry thus enters attention as position rather than as fused reconstruction features. We further introduce Reference-Frame Composition (RC) supervision to train the model to update its reference frame across successive judgments. It provides stepwise turn and heading targets derived from verified single-step annotations. Among the compared methods, DK-VLM-8B achieves the highest average score across six spatial benchmarks and the highest overall scores on VSI-Bench (74.33) and ReVSI-32 (58.28). RC supervision further improves multi-step spatial reasoning, while the full model preserves general video understanding. Project page: https://sumuru789.github.io/DK-VLM-website/.

</details>


### [123] [Grounding Generative Verification in 3D Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22360)
*Shuanglin Li,Jiaying Lin,Shuquan Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了3DVerifierBench基准，涵盖10项任务评估决策、拒绝解释及规范和候选更改的响应。通过人类研究和解释驱动分析揭示了现有模型的局限性，开发了大规模处理管道处理多视角事实提取、实验性3D编辑和本地注释生成14,126个矛盾解释及反事实。此外，验证支持3D生成通过候选选择和递归精炼，人类指导的递归精炼在八个输入研究中满足了所有要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D生成和理解系统需要验证反馈机制，以解决任务特定的正确性问题。

Method: 本文通过设计3DVerifierBench基准，利用多视角事实提取、控制3D编辑和本地注释生成数据集，评估模型并探讨验证如何支持3D生成。

Result: 人类研究和解释驱动分析揭示了现有的局限性，开发了可扩展的处理管道，生成了大量有矛盾解释的数据。通过验证，候选选择和递归精炼显著提高了生成效果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个可靠的验证反馈基础，使模型能够反思和改进其3D内容。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and 3D generation have expanded 3D understanding and synthesis. However, beyond visual fidelity, task-specific correctness grounded in 3D evidence remains a major challenge. We argue that building reliable 3D systems requires not only stronger generation and reasoning, but also verification feedback that identifies unmet requirements and explains their violations, allowing systems to reflect on and refine their outputs. Such feedback must integrate multi-view observations and distinguish genuine inconsistencies from projection-dependent appearances. We make three contributions. First, we introduce 3DVerifierBench, a benchmark spanning 10 tasks that evaluates decisions, rejection explanations, and responses to controlled changes in specifications and candidates. A human study reveals a 21.6 percentage-point gap between humans and the strongest tested model, GPT-6 Astra, while explanation-aware analysis exposes weaknesses in rejection reliability. Second, we analyze verification across fine-grained factual categories, exposing errors in values and target binding. We develop a scalable pipeline combining multi-view fact extraction, controlled 3D editing, and native annotations to produce 14,126 training examples with contradiction explanations and linked counterfactuals. Third, we examine how verification could support 3D generation through candidate selection and recursive refinement. In human-labeled sixteen-candidate pools, oracle selection improves complete factual success from 26.7% to 90.0%. In a separate eight-input study, human-guided recursive refinement satisfies all seven requirements for every input, compared with three under parallel Oracle@8. Together, our benchmark, analysis, and data pipeline establish a foundation for models to reflect on 3D content through reliable verification feedback.

</details>


### [124] [Perceive, Predict, and Plan: a Unified World Action Model for Egocentric Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22363)
*Zirui Wu,Chen Guo,Tianjian Jiang,Zeren Jiang,Zeming Li,Lingchen Yang,Longteng Duan,Ying Xue,Handi Yin,Shenyuan Gao,Martin R. Oswald,Jun Zhang,Ping Tan,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P$^{3}$-WAM 是一种新的统一世界行动模型，它在预训练视频扩散变换器中引入了紧凑的3D行动表示，并通过 '感知'、'预测' 和 '规划' 三个互补的模式来学习3D感知、动作条件下的预测及目标导向的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前在手部动画和视频预测中的不足，提出了一种新的统一世界行动模型P$^{3}$-WAM，旨在提高3D感知、动作预测和目标导向规划的能力。

Method: 通过引入预训练视频扩散变换器和混合变换器架构，实现共享基础权重，模态特定适应，双向视觉与行动注意力的新方法进行建模。

Result: P$^{3}$-WAM 在四个数据集上优于专门的baseline，特别是在3D手部重建和动作条件下的视频预测方面表现出色。同时，生成的目标导向操作视频比视频生成Baseline具有更强的指令遵守性和行动进展。

Conclusion: 统一训练方法提高了模型在所有三个组件上的性能，增强了在多任务设置下的整体表现。

Abstract: We introduce P$^{3}$-WAM, a unified world action model for egocentric dexterous manipulation that learns 3D perception, action-conditioned prediction, and goal-directed planning through three complementary modes: Perceive, which reconstructs articulated hand motion and camera trajectories from video; Predict, which generates visual futures under prescribed actions; and Plan, which jointly generates action trajectories and their anticipated visual consequences conditioned on an initial image, a language instruction, and an optional visual goal. Our model integrates a compact, structured 3D action representation into a pretrained video diffusion transformer through a mixture-of-transformers architecture with shared base weights, modality-specific adaptation, and bidirectional visual-action attention. Randomized modality visibility lets video and 3D actions serve as either conditioning context or prediction targets across the three modes. Experiments show that unified training improves all three capabilities over single-mode training, including gains in planning quality under a vision-language model evaluation. Through unified training, our model outperforms specialized baselines on 3D hand reconstruction and action-conditioned video prediction across four datasets, while producing goal-directed manipulation videos with stronger instruction adherence and action progression than video-generation baselines. Project Page: https://p3-wam.pages.dev/

</details>


### [125] [SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22369)
*Shimon Malnick,Nadav Orenstein,Ohad Fried,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoftCache通过软缓存方法在采样过程中根据激活的头程来调整跳过节点的步数，无需训练且无需特定模型设置，在图像、视频等应用场景中显著提升了生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费缓存方法在重新利用旧激活时过于简单地进行全或无的选择，这并未充分利用信号中的细微差别，从而限制了加速潜力。SoftCache通过利用头程大小动态调整跳过的节点数目，旨在提供更精细且高效的加速效果。

Method: SoftCache采用了一种软缓存策略，根据激活函数的头程大小决定缓存步骤的执行情况。具体来说，当头程较大时，跳过更多的节点；反之，则逐渐回归到固定的步长。

Result: 在FLUX（图像）、HunyuanVideo和Wan2.1（视频）等多个基准数据集上，SoftCache在加速比方面（3.5至6.9倍）明显优于其它基线，并且在所有四类保真度指标上都取得了领先。

Conclusion: SoftCache提供了对现有训练免费缓存方法的有效改进，通过其软缓存机制能够显著提升扩散和流模型的生成效率，同时保持对不同模型及应用的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models generate a sample over iterative steps, each a full pass through a large network, and that pass dominates generation cost. Training-free caching accelerates them by reusing activations from recent evaluations instead of recomputing them. Dynamic caching methods decide when to refresh by accumulating a cheap probe of the network input into a staleness signal and spending a fresh evaluation once it crosses a threshold. They perform hard caching: the signal is a binary switch between refresh and reuse, and every step, fresh or cached, advances by the prescribed step size. We observe that the signal carries much more than this switch. How far it sits below the threshold, its headroom, predicts the error induced by reuse. We propose SoftCache, which performs soft caching: instead of a binary switch, the headroom sets how far each cached step goes. Where the headroom is large, the sampler jumps past several scheduled nodes, and as a refresh nears, its steps shrink back toward the original step. These adaptive jumps may land between the scheduled nodes, taking the sampler off its fixed grid. SoftCache requires no training and adds no per-backbone settings: on FLUX for images and on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1 for video, at speedups from $3.5\times$ to $6.9\times$, it is ahead of every baseline on all four fidelity metrics.

</details>


### [126] [Which Tokens to Merge? Diffusion Dynamics for Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22370)
*SeungJu Cha,Ye-Chan Kim,HyunGee Kim,Sungho Koh,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DaTMe 提出了一种基于扩散动力学的训练-free 框架，通过构建动态感知的重要性图来确定 token 的合并优先级，从而在加速生成同时保持图像的视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 token 合并方法依赖于文本相关性来保护前景区域，但容易忽略背景对象和未被明确提示的内容，导致结构连贯性下降。DaTMe 通过关注 token 的动态稳定性和结构一致性，改进了 token 合并决策。

Method: DaTMe 方法通过 Tweedie 去噪估计生成动态感知的重要性图，该图结合了时间信号（步长间预测干净图像的变化量）和结构信号（局部高频内容）。基于此图，DaTMe 可以在压缩率条件下为 token 分配合并职责，保持重要 token 作为锚点，安全合并那些已时间稳定且结构均匀的 token。

Result: 在 PixArt-a 和 FLUX 数据集上的实验结果显示，DaTMe 成功地在整个图像范围内维护了视觉保真度，证明了扩散动力学是 token 级别合并决策的有效性。

Conclusion: DaTMe 提供了一种新颖的 token 合并策略，通过利用扩散模型的内在动态属性，有效地减小了冗余计算，而不会显著影响生成质量。

Abstract: Token merging has emerged as an effective training-free strategy for accelerating diffusion models by reducing redundant token computation while minimizing generation degradation. A key challenge is determining which tokens can be safely merged and which should be preserved for subsequent computation. Existing method addresses this by preserving prompt-aligned foreground regions, thereby protecting semantically relevant structures. However, this text-relevance criterion overlooks background objects, local details that are not explicitly mentioned in the prompt, leading to degraded structural coherence. In this work, we propose Dynamics-aware Token Merging (DaTMe), a training-free framework that grounds token merging in the underlying diffusion dynamics. Our key insight is that token importance should reflect whether a token remains unresolved during denoising and whether it carries structural information. To this end, DaTMe constructs a dynamics- aware importance map from the Tweedie denoised estimate. The map combines a temporal signal, measuring inter-step changes in the predicted clean image, with a structural signal, capturing local high-frequency content such as boundaries and fine details. Using this map, DaTMe adaptively assigns tokens into merging roles under a given compression ratio: important tokens are preserved as anchors, while tokens that are both temporally stabilized and structurally homogeneous are selected as safe merge candidates. Experiments on PixArt-a and FLUX demonstrate that DaTMe maintains visual fidelity across the entire image, highlighting diffusion dynamics as an effective token-level merging decision in efficient generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/SeungJuCha/DaTMe.

</details>


### [127] [Gen4P: Video Generators are 4D Perceivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22371)
*Yitong Xia,Zeren Jiang,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gen4P 是一个统一框架，利用预训练的视频生成器转化为前馈 4D 接受器，无需迭代去噪。该框架通过单次编码器传递干净的视频潜在变量，并通过轻量级交叉注意力解码器预测所需 4D 属性。此外，插件深度先验增强了几何知识，提高整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了从单目视频中高效地恢复 4D 几何和运动，利用预训练的视频生成器作为前馈 4D 接受器，增强几何知识并提高 3D 点跟踪、视频深度和摄像机姿态估计任务性能。

Method: Gen4P 利用预训练的视频生成器作为前馈 4D 接受器，通过单次编码器处理清洁的视频潜在变量，并采用轻量级交叉注意力解码器预测所需 4D 属性。另外，通过插件深度先验增强了几何知识。

Result: Gen4P 在 3D 点跟踪、视频深度和摄像机姿态估计任务上达到或接近最佳性能。实验证明其能力出色，即使在野生视频中也能跟踪复杂运动，保持精细结构。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了视频生成预训练在统一 4D 感知中的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Gen4P, a unified framework for recovering 4D geometry and motion from monocular video. We show that a pretrained video generator can be repurposed into a feed-forward 4D perceiver without iterative denoising. Building on on-demand querying, Gen4P processes clean video latents in a single encoder pass and predicts the requested 4D attributes through a lightweight cross-attention decoder. The on-demand query formulation enables flexible spatial sampling, and our proposed continuous-time queries further provide temporal flexibility. To strengthen geometric knowledge, we propose a straightforward plug-in solution by incorporating a pretrained video-depth LoRA as a frozen geometry prior, improving overall performance without adding extra trainable parameters. Gen4P achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on tasks of 3D point tracking, video depth and camera pose estimation. Qualitative results on in-the-wild videos illustrate its capability of tracking complex motion while maintaining the delicate structure. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of video generative pretraining for unified 4D perception. See https://gen4p.github.io for more visual results.

</details>


### [128] [ReasonUni: Reasoning-Augmented Reconstruction Advances Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20781)
*Feize Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Yuhuan Hong,Yuwei Niu,Zongjian Li,Zheyuan Liu,Bin Lin,Jinbo Yan,Jie Qin,Xudong Mao,Limeng Qiao,Guanglu Wan,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ReasonUni的框架，它结合了推理增强和重建后训练方法，实现图像到文本和文本到图像任务之间的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重建增强方法在感知上取得了一定成果，但未能提供足够的监督以理解结构、意图和转换。为了弥补这一不足，作者提出了一种新的方法来优化统一多模态模型。

Method: 该方法结合了树结构强化学习方法（TreeGRPO）和重建后训练技术，通过在每个推理路径上采样多个推理路径和候选图像来联合优化推理模块和生成模块。

Result: 该方法在多个基准测试中的实验结果表明，无论是理解和生成能力，都有了一致的提升，展示了推理增强重建对于统一多模态模型的真正协同收益。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了推理增强在统一多模态模型中重建阶段的作用，为未来通过增强推理来增强模型的表现提供了路径。

Abstract: Image-to-text (I2T) understanding and text-to-image (T2I) generation are dual tasks in unified multimodal models (UMMs). Reconstructive post-training couples these tasks, but existing reconstruction-based methods remain perception-centric and provide limited supervision for reasoning about structure, intent, and transformation. We propose ReasonUni, a reasoning-augmented reconstructive post-training framework that extends this coupling beyond perception. Given an original image and an editing instruction, the UMM first reasons about what and how to edit and describes the target state, then generates the corresponding target image conditioned on this understanding output. To jointly optimize both modules, we introduce TreeGRPO, a tree-structured reinforcement learning method that samples multiple reasoning paths and candidate images per path, then aggregates visual rewards to update both modules under a unified objective. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in both understanding and generation, showing that reasoning-augmented reconstruction yields genuine mutual gains for UMMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Real-time Chatbot Romance Scam Detection in Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20631)
*Yi-cheng Liu,Guan-ru Lai,Yi-cheng Lai,Lyn Chao-ling Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了使用聊天机器人的方法来实时检测通过Line消息API实施的爱情诈骗，并利用社会科学中的七阶段理论，通过大规模语言模型识别诈骗阶段。此外，一旦识别出诈骗行为，聊天机器人会通过语言模型提供当前阶段的解释和预防建议。这项研究的贡献在于提供了一个在实际场景中减少爱情诈骗风险的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交媒体和人工智能技术的进步，虚假识别变得容易，爱情诈骗已经成为一个严重的问题。本文为了应对这一挑战，寻求开发了一种能够实时检测爱情诈骗的技术和应用。

Method: 通过应用Line消息 API 和大规模语言模型，基于社会科学中的七阶段理论，开发了一种聊天机器人来检测并解释爱情诈骗的各个阶段，并提供预防建议。

Result: 聊天机器人能够实时检测爱情诈骗并提供详细的阶段解释和预防建议，研究提供了实用的应用场景来应对爱情诈骗。

Conclusion: 这项研究通过开发聊天机器人应用于实际场景，为减少爱情诈骗风险提供了可行的方法和技术支持。

Abstract: Romance scam has become a serious problem via social media, and the improvement of artificial intelligence enable to fake identify easily. In the study, a chatbot has developed to detect romance scam in real-time through Line Messaging API. Using the seven stage theories in the social science field, the stages of romance scam can be detected through large language model. In addition, once a romance scam is detected, the chatbot provides explanations of the stage and prevention advices through large language model. The contribution of the work provides a practical application, and can be used in real scenario for reducing the risk of romance scam.

</details>


### [130] [AbridgeKV: Compressing Long Reasoning into the KV Cache of Learned Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20641)
*Jack Cai,Lijie Yang,Vasilis Kontonis,Dimitris Papailiopoulos,Shivam Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AbridgeKV的模型压缩技术，该技术通过生成暂停令牌并将其 KV 缓存条目包含在生成文本的块中，来压缩模型输出，从而在保持准确性和文本长度的同时减少了 KV 缓存的大小，并提高了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的长链解码过程由于生成令牌的 KV 缓存的增长而受到内存限制，即使移除缓存条目也无法解决问题，因为未经训练在具有移除条目的缓存上的模型会产生更长的输出，甚至达到最长时间限制。解决此问题的方法是训练模型压缩其自己的输出，AbridgeKV 就是这样做的。

Method: AbridgeKV 通过将每个完成的文本块压缩为几个学习到的暂停标记的 KV 缓存条目来压缩生成的文本。这些暂停标记作为输入令牌具有训练嵌入，但不产生输出。一旦这些暂停标记已编码该块，AbridgeKV 便会释放该块的原始缓存；其后生成的令牌将对应于暂停标记的 KV 缓存条目。

Result: 在 446 个 AMC 和其他数学和科学竞赛问题的数据集上，保持四分之一生成缓存的 AbridgeKV 模型在准确性和生成长度方面与完全 KV 模型表现相同；最强公开的缓存移除器在同等预算下会产生更长的输出，而最接近的压缩器训练后在相同数据集上表现下降了 10 分。应用在 vLLM 上的缓存相比完全 KV 模型的序列大小减少了 3.5 倍，并且饱和的 H100 在问题解决上比使用完整 KV 模型快 3.2 倍。

Conclusion: AbridgeKV 通过减少 KV 缓存的大小来提高推理效率，同时保持了模型的准确性和生成长度，并能够在实际应用中显著提升性能。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought decoding is memory-bound: the KV cache of the generated tokens grows linearly with the test-time compute being spent. Evicting cache entries does not fix this, because a model never trained on a cache with entries removed generates longer, often up to the length limit. We instead train the model to compress its own output. AbridgeKV compresses each completed block of generated text into the KV entries of a few learned pause tokens. These are extra input tokens with trained embeddings that produce no output. Once the pause tokens have encoded the block, AbridgeKV frees the block's original cache; subsequent tokens attend to the pause tokens' KV entries in its place. Three novel choices make this work: the pause tokens' KV entries take positions inside the block they replace, the cache budget is allocated across layers according to the measured value of each layer's entries, and compression operates on relatively large blocks. On 446 competition problems from AIME, HMMT, AMC, BRUMO, CMIMC and SMT with 32K-token generations, keeping a quarter of the generated cache matches an identically trained full-KV model in accuracy and output length; the strongest published evictor at a matched budget writes far longer, and the closest trained compressor, retrained on the same data, loses 10 points. Served in vLLM, the cache is 3.5x smaller per sequence and a saturated H100 finishes a problem 3.2x faster than with full KV.

</details>


### [131] [CoTS: Competitive Token Selection for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20642)
*Jie Yu,Yuxi Sun,Zhaoxu Li,Chunxiao Liu,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoTS是一种无需训练的框架，它通过在每个推理步骤中竞争性地选择最可能的候选者来动态平衡离散承诺和软探索，从而改进了现有的链式思维方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的链式思维方法在面对确定性和不确定性推理时分别采取了简体承诺和软探索的模式，但缺乏动态调整机制。CoTS通过在每个推理步骤中竞争性地选择最有可能的候选者来解决这一问题。

Method: CoTS采用了概率竞争机制，在每个推理步骤中选择最有可能和次可能的候选者进行动态平衡，从而在保留推理链稳定性的前提下，确保模型探索多个可能的推理路径。

Result: 在六个推理基准测试中，CoTS持续优于标准链式思维（CoT）和最先进的无需训练的潜在推理方法。

Conclusion: CoTS提供了一种无需训练的优化模型推理方法，能够更好地平衡确定性和不确定性，从而提高模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Recent latent reasoning methods move beyond discrete Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by replacing sampled tokens with continuous soft embeddings or hidden states, enabling richer intermediate representations and preserving alternative reasoning paths. However, the need for latent reasoning varies across decoding steps: confident predictions favor discrete commitment, whereas ambiguous steps benefit from preserving multiple plausible alternatives. Existing approaches typically handle this variation through either global soft activation or hard switching based on uncertainty thresholds, treating latent intervention as an all-or-nothing decision rather than continuously adapting its strength. To address this limitation, we propose Competitive Token Selection (CoTS), a training-free framework that reframes latent intervention from binary mode selection into continuous allocation at the token level. CoTS uses the probability competition between the two most likely candidates to dynamically balance discrete commitment and soft exploration at each reasoning step. The second candidate represents the strongest alternative to the model's preferred continuation, so the separation between the two directly reflects the stability of the current discrete choice. When one candidate clearly dominates, CoTS keeps the representation close to the sampled discrete token to preserve a stable reasoning trajectory; as the competition strengthens, it increases the contribution of the soft representation to retain plausible alternatives. This competition-based allocation requires neither manually tuned switching thresholds nor mixing coefficients, and introduces no additional forward passes. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CoTS consistently outperforms standard CoT and state-of-the-art training-free latent reasoning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/OkinoYu/CoTSReasoning.

</details>


### [132] [Does Personalization Break Trust? Open-Set Rejection Calibration Under Few-Shot On-Device Intent Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20652)
*Vaibhav Prajapati,Pushp Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究人员发现，利用LoRA进行增量个性化时，虽然能保持内部指令的准确性，但会导致对未涵盖意图的输入识别能力下降，具体表现在AUROC指标上。他们提出使用马哈拉诺比斯距离来评估拒绝超出范围的输入，这种方法在部分数据集上表现良好且不影响准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估虚拟助手对新命令增量学习的过程中对未定义输入的拒绝能力。

Method: 通过在五项公开的目标分类基准测试上对LoRA进行几轮微调，并利用scorer-invariant检查进行分析，以及提出使用马哈拉诺比斯距离来替代softmax置信度进行拒绝评分的方法。

Result: LoRA增量微调降低了对外界输入（OOS）的拒绝能力，使用马哈拉诺比斯距离进行拒绝评分的方法在三个数据集上恢复了大部分质量，且未影响准确性，并优于两种基线方法，该方法具有较低的部署成本且可通过广泛应用于不同样本数量、LoRA秩和第二个基础编码器。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，使用马哈拉诺比斯距离进行OOS评分可有效解决LoRA个性化带来的OOS分类性能下降问题，提升了模型对外界的适应能力。

Abstract: Always-on virtual assistants increasingly personalize on-device to new user-specific commands via few-shot fine-tuning (e.g., LoRA), but this adaptation is evaluated almost exclusively on whether it learns the new commands, not on whether the assistant can still recognize an input that matches none of its intents - the out-of-scope (OOS) rejection problem. We show that naive few-shot LoRA personalization degrades OOS-rejection AUROC (area under the ROC curve) on every one of five public intent-classification benchmarks (mean drop $0.055$--$0.258$, five seeds each), while in-scope accuracy is preserved. A scorer-invariant check shows part of this drop is an artifact of the standard confidence scorer's own miscalibration under adaptation, though a smaller, genuine effect remains under any scorer; we trace it to a measurable shrinkage of the embedding margin around known-intent regions (pooled Spearman rho=0.61$). A deployment-cheap fix is scoring rejection by Mahalanobis distance instead of softmax confidence without extra training/retraining recovers most of the lost quality on 3 of 5 datasets at no accuracy cost, beats two further OOD scoring baselines, and generalizes across shot count, LoRA rank, and a second base encoder. We report honestly where the fix falls short and release all code for reproducibility.

</details>


### [133] [Reference-Guided Turn-Taking Style Transfer for Full-Duplex Spoken Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20763)
*Yuxuan Chen,Yang Xiao,Jack Sun,Chak Sun,Brick Li,Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现了一个名为‘initiative vector’的单向残差流方向，能够控制全双工语音模型的发言比例，实现模型与人类对话者之间的类人交流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语音模型通常只有一个固定讲话比例，无法像人类对话者那样灵活地分享发言权。因此，该研究旨在找到一种方法，使得语音模型能够更好地模仿人类对话者的交流模式。

Method: 研究提取了模型内部的‘initiative vector’方向，通过调整这一方向来改变模型的发言比例。此外，研究还发现，可以通过参考录音将特定说话人的方式转移给模型。

Result: 通过调整‘initiative vector’，模型的发言比例从6-12%提升至42-61%，甚至在没有干预的情况下也能达到21-30%。在将说话人风格转移到模型时，参考录音的特定性对匹配的成功率有很大影响。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了如何通过调整‘initiative vector’来控制语音模型的发言比例，并能有效地将特定说话人的交流风格转移到模型中，使得模型的对话行为更加接近人类的自然交流。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken language models decide at every frame whether to take the floor, defining a conversational personality -- yet each set of weights encodes exactly one, which human speakers bracket rather than share. Existing controls mostly act from outside; none identifies the quantity that sets how much the model speaks. We identify it: a single late-layer residual-stream direction, the initiative vector, extracted from the model's own conversations without training or annotation. The same direction reads the decision, anticipating onsets up to 800 ms ahead, and shifts it: scaled during generation, it moves speech ratio from 6-12% to 42-61% (no steering: 21-30%), consistently across conversations, weights, and corpora. A short reference recording suffices to transfer a speaker's turn-taking style to the model, and the transfer is reference-specific: swapping in another speaker's reference worsens the match in 93-98% of cases, whereas the best uniform dose is indistinguishable from no steering. Turn-taking style is not frozen into the weights but a direction that can be read, shifted, and calibrated per person. Audio examples are available at https://elpsykongloo.github.io/initiative-vector/.

</details>


### [134] [Verdict: Encode Once, Decide Many](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20778)
*Avdhesh Singh Chouhan,Jack Perry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Verdict-MQ的开放式编码器，它一次读取案例并回答所有相关问题，通过避免问题间的相互影响以及使选项顺序无关，提高了自动化组织工作中的决策质量。实验结果表明，Verdict-MQ在基准测试数据集上表现优于现有模型，并在某些任务上超过了调优后的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的自动化组织工作依赖于逐个解决小规模问题的方式，这种做法效率低下且容易引入错误。为了改进这一流程，提出了Verdict-MQ模型来一次性处理多个相关问题，从而提高决策的准确性和效率。

Method: Verdict-MQ模型采用了结构化的注意力掩码，在输入裁剪后保持状态编码的独立性，并通过设计使问题之间不会相互影响，选项顺序也无关紧要。为了评估模型性能，作者使用了多个基准数据集进行实验，并将Verdict-MQ与标准交叉编码器及细调后的基础模型进行了对比。

Result: 在类型化决策基准测试数据集和RACE、BoolQ和MultiRC三个其他基准数据集上，Verdict-MQ模型的表现优于标准交叉编码器和细调后的基础模型，其准确度和Brier得分显著优于前者的对应指标。特别是在多段落阅读理解任务中，Verdict-MQ还超过了所有细调后的编码器。

Conclusion: Verdict-MQ模型通过一次性处理相关问题，提高了自动化组织工作的效率和准确性。虽然这种方法在某些特定任务上带来了额外的延迟，但通过合理设计，可以有效避免因问题间相互影响而导致的性能下降。

Abstract: Automating organizational work means answering many small typed questions about one piece of state (does this email need a reply, how urgent is it, who owns it) with probabilities good enough to act on without a human. The open typed-decision model we compare against, like a standard cross-encoder, answers each question with a separate pass over the input. We present Verdict-MQ, an open encoder that reads a case once and answers all of its questions: given identical retained state tokens after truncation (verified on every reported case), a structured attention mask makes the state encoding independent of the questions, keeps questions from influencing each other, and makes option order irrelevant by construction. On the typed-decisions benchmark, a 395M Verdict-MQ matches the soft gold labels significantly better than the released 421M Laya specialist on all five fresh seeds (KL 0.080 vs 0.117, Brier 0.045 vs 0.061), an advantage that survives per-system temperature scaling; its higher accuracy (0.801 vs 0.767) lies past the benchmark's own saturation point, so we read that margin as closer agreement with the benchmark's labelling teacher. On RACE, BoolQ and MultiRC, against a matched fine-tuned baseline it wins on MultiRC and loses on RACE and on BoolQ, where its training is unstable; we measure zero-shot references ourselves, including a closed general system (Jev) that beats every fine-tuned encoder. A controlled comparison measures what the shared state costs: giving each question its own question-aware copy of the state gains 0.9-3.5 points on RACE and MultiRC at 1.4-2.5x the per-case latency, and nothing measurable on typed decisions, with the invariance guarantees unchanged.

</details>


### [135] [APRiL: An Open-ended Benchmark for Chinese Social Awareness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20785)
*Tian Lan,Han Zhang,Shanshan Wang,Yahan Yu,Jiang Li,Yugo Murawaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: APRiL 是一个针对中文社会意识的综合性基准测试，包含了2,196个特定于实例的评分类别。它通过社会场景分析和社会意识响应任务来评估模型在理解社会线索、心理状态和冲突结构方面的效果，并考察这些理解能否转化为适当的响应。研究发现，高质量的场景分析能提高响应质量，而低质量的分析则可能导致响应质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试主要集中在特定的社会能力、受限的任务格式或特定的交互场景上，限制了对各种社交意识的广泛覆盖。APRiL 旨在弥补这一差距。

Method: 该基准测试包括六个类别和两个互补任务：社会场景分析和社会意识响应。研究人员对12个来自六个家族的模型进行了实验。

Result: 结果显示，理解和响应之间存在持续的差距。高质量的场景分析可以提升响应质量，低质量的分析可能导致响应质量下降。

Conclusion: APRiL 为中文背景下提升社交意识的语言模型提供了系统性的资源。数据和代码可在指定链接获取。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in social interactions, where appropriate responses require recognizing implicit cues, inferring mental states, identifying conflicts, and considering potential consequences. However, existing benchmarks often focus on specific social abilities, constrained task formats, or particular interaction settings, offering limited coverage of social awareness across diverse contexts. To address this gap, we introduce APRiL, an open-ended benchmark for Chinese social awareness with instance-specific rubrics. APRiL contains 2,196 Social Awareness Evaluation Instances across six categories and two complementary tasks: Social Scenario Analysis evaluates models' understanding of social cues, mental states, and conflict structures, while Socially Aware Response assesses whether they can translate this understanding into appropriate, natural responses. Experiments on 12 models from six families reveal a consistent understanding-to-response gap. Higher-quality scenario analyses improve responses, while weak analyses may reduce response quality. APRiL provides a systematic resource for advancing socially aware language models in Chinese contexts. Our data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/APRiL-05DE/.

</details>


### [136] [PRISM: Transfer and Limits of Activation Interventions in Tool-Calling Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20808)
*Giovana Kerche Bonás,Thales Sales Almeida,Marcelo da Silva Reis,Rodrigo Nogueira,Marcos Meideiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PRISM框架通过利用低资源环境和跨语言转移，在不进行目标语言调整的情况下，成功提高了多语言模型的安全性，减少了几个百分点的不安全行为，同时适用于大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型中文字拒绝和不安全工具调用之间的隐性缺陷，并提出PRISM框架以评估激活干预措施在精度、语言和规模之间的转移性。

Method: 引入PRISM框架来评估激活干预措施在低资源环境（量化NF4标签和跨语言转移）中的转移性，并通过比较干预估计的一致性和安全违规情况的变化来量化安全性改进。

Result: PRISM框架在NF4监督指导下，跨五种语言的干预措施成功地应用于阿拉伯语Llama-3.1-8B，减少了21.95个百分点的安全违规行为。此外，干预措施也成功转移至更大规模的模型。

Conclusion: 辅助监督来自压缩模型和多语言数据可以帮助揭示并缓解不同模型家庭中的潜在危险工具调用行为。

Abstract: Recent literature shows that unsafe tool calls can often accompany textual refusals in language models. To investigate this hidden vulnerability, we introduce PRISM, a framework for evaluating the transfer of activation interventions across precision, languages, and scales. Our findings demonstrate that leveraging lower-resource settings (specifically quantized NF4 labels and cross-lingual transfer) offers a robust pathway to expose and improve model alignment. Notably, using behavioral labels from an NF4 copy enables stable intervention estimation even with very few scenarios. Furthermore, these alignment improvements transfer effectively across languages: five-language NF4-supervised directions successfully transfer to held-out Arabic, reducing unsafe violations in Llama-3.1-8B by 21.95 percentage points without target-language tuning. By mapping paired benign activations, we also successfully transfer these safety interventions to much larger models (up to 70B parameters). Ultimately, PRISM highlights how auxiliary supervision from compressed models and multilingual data can uncover and mitigate hazardous tool-calling behaviors across different model families. We release all experimental code and supporting artifacts at https://github.com/GiKerche/prism, alongside an interactive explorer of recorded model responses and experimental results at https://gikerche.github.io/prism/.

</details>


### [137] [Wolof-Adapted Industry-Ready Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20824)
*Gwénolé Lecorvé,Delphine Charlet,Pierre Adam,Floriane Behanzin,Lionel Delphin-Poulat,Johannes Heinecke,Géraldine Damnati,Pelin Kaytmaz,Nourredine Khaous,Charbel Kindji,Meriam Maadi,Abdoulaye Mbaye,Claire Perroux,Ismaël Rousseau,Papa Séga Wadé,Gilles Xiberras,Olivier Zhang,Malek Zitouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了针对沃尔夫语（威洛语）开发的0.6B-14B参数数量的开放语言模型，并通过指令调优、偏好对齐和模型合并进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于沃尔夫语在开放语言模型中的支持较弱，以及API模型带来的成本、治理和主权问题，作者旨在创建适用于沃尔夫语的高质量语言模型。

Method: 作者通过指令调优、偏好对齐和模型合并的方法开发了沃尔夫语语言模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，该模型在沃尔夫语理解和生成上表现出良好性能，同时在其他语言和跨语言技能方面也保持有效。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，特别是8B和14B模型与大规模商业系统具有竞争力，且模型正在内部客户支持试点中进行评估，并可从Hugging Face网站公开获取。

Abstract: Wolof is spoken by 23M people, mostly in Senegal, but remains weakly supported by open-weight language models, while API models raise cost, governance, and sovereignty constraints for local deployments. In this paper, we present 0.6B-14B open-weight models adapted to Wolof through instruction tuning, preference alignment, and model merging. After presenting the pipeline and data, extensive experiments on 63~public and in-house tasks show good performance on Wolof understanding and generation while remaining effective on other languages and cross-lingual skills, with the 8B and 14B models being particularly competitive with substantially larger commercial systems. The models are currently being evaluated in internal customer-care pilots and are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/collections/Orange/wolof-lm .

</details>


### [138] [CoTranslator: Reverse to Forward Chain-of-Thought Translator for Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20828)
*Zinan Tang,Zhuoshi Pan,Kairui Chen,Ze Yan,Shaomian Zheng,Yukun Zhang,Gang Wang,Biqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoTranslator 通过将反向链式思维生成的响应翻译成正向生成的响应，提高了解题准确性，同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的链式思维生成方法效率低下，而直接逆向生成又可能导致不对齐。CoTranslator 设计目的是解决这一问题，通过翻译来对齐响应格式。

Method: CoTranslator 使用少量成对的逆向生成和拒绝采样的前向生成响应进行训练，然后应用于大规模生成和验证学生训练数据。

Result: 在数学基准测试上的六个学生模型中，CoTranslator 将平均准确率提高了至多 5.82 个百分点。对于两个 4B 学生，它保留了拒绝采样平均准确率的 95.0%，同时只用了 35.9% 的计算量。无需重新训练，相同翻译器也能应用于新数学数据集和其他科学领域问题，提高了 STEM 准确率。

Conclusion: CoTranslator 提供了一种高效且可迁移的方法，以对齐反向生成的链式思维响应，从而改善了学生模型的训练数据生成过程。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation requires chain-of-thought (CoT) data with correct answers, but obtaining such data efficiently remains challenging. Forward CoT generation (FCG) followed by rejection sampling (RS) produces verified, question-only responses, yet may require many teacher calls for difficult problems. Reverse CoT generation (RCG) is cheaper because it conditions on the known answer, but access to this answer can lead to post-hoc rationalization and a mismatch with the student's answer-free inference setting. We propose CoTranslator, which learns to translate reverse CoT responses toward the distribution of verified forward responses. Trained on a small set of paired reverse and rejection-sampled forward responses, it supports large-scale generation and verification of student training data. Across six student models evaluated on six mathematics benchmarks, CoTranslator improves average accuracy over semantic suppression by up to $5.82$ percentage points. For two 4B students, it retains $95.0\%$ of rejection sampling's average accuracy while using $35.9\%$ of its estimated compute. Without retraining, the same translator transfers to a new mathematics dataset, a different teacher, and STEM problems, improving average STEM accuracy by $4.88$ and $4.31$ percentage points for 4B and 8B students, respectively. These results show that CoTranslator offers an efficient and transferable approach to reasoning distillation.

</details>


### [139] [PEDBench: Can Speech Models Detect Phonetic Errors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20830)
*Zhonghao Shi,Zackary Rackauckas,Jasmine Yip,Savannah Halpern,Simon Campos,Lauren Brace,Emma Yang,Tiantian Feng,Xuan Shi,Shrikanth Narayanan,Teresa Satterfield,Ying Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了 PEDBench，这是首个涵盖从自然到控制音节错误的基准，旨在填补音节错误检测评估数据不足的空白。研究评估了13个最先进的语音模型，发现即使在有大量合成和操控数据的情况下，这些模型也未能有效检测出音节错误，且精度较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏高质量的音节错误评估数据，限制了语音模型对细微音节错误的可靠检测能力。为了改进这一现状，本文提出了 PEDBench，这一新的基准涵盖了自然音节错误和控制音节错误的例子。

Method: 研究采用了真人儿童、演员和基于发音条件的文本转语音合成的音节错误样本，每种样本独立标注后组合形成 PEDBench。通过对不同最先进的语音模型进行测试，评估它们对音节错误的检测能力以及在控制条件下的性能。

Result: 尽管进行了大量的合成和控制实验，但评估结果显示，当前的语音模型在检测音节错误方面表现不佳。模型的精确度较低，经常将正确发音的音节误判为错误。本文开发的经过合成数据微调的语音语言模型表现出色，但与真实数据之间的鸿沟仍然存在。

Conclusion: 研究建议通过释放代码和数据集开源 PEDBench，促进这一领域的进一步研究，最终加速准确的音节错误检测系统的开发。

Abstract: Phonetic error detection (PED) aims to identify phoneme-level substitutions, deletions, and insertions and is essential for educational and clinical speech assessment and intervention. Despite recent advances in speech models for phoneme recognition, their ability to reliably detect fine-grained phonetic errors remains unclear because of a lack of high-quality evaluation data. To address this gap, we introduce PEDBench, the first PED benchmark spanning a continuum from natural to controlled speech. It comprises real-world recordings of spoken errors made by children (N=334), scripted error productions by human actors (N=1,188), and synthetic error productions generated using phone-conditioned text-to-speech (N=1,083). All phonetic annotations were transcribed and verified by two phonetically trained annotators. Our evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art speech models shows that they fail to reliably detect phonetic errors, even under controlled conditions with acted and synthetic speech, where acoustic variability is reduced. The models also exhibit low precision, frequently misclassifying correctly pronounced phonemes as errors. Our speech-language models, fine-tuned on synthetic data, outperform commercial and open-source baselines on word-level PED, although a sim-to-real domain gap remains. We plan to publicly release our code and recipes, along with our acted and synthetic datasets, to support the development of more accurate PED systems.

</details>


### [140] [Does Morphological Correctness Matter for Tokenization? A Scrambled Control Study in Tagalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20859)
*Abdul Rehman Antall,Baidya Nath Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MorphAustro-Tok，一种认识到形态学边界重要性的分词器，比基于频率的分词器提高了塔加洛语机器翻译性能，且这种改进并非由于增加的标记数量或模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讨论了基于频率的分词方法破坏了词汇边界信息，而形态学意识分词方法旨在避免这种问题，特别是在语言如塔加洛语中，其词根与词缀非连续。

Method: 构建了MorphAustro-Tok分词器，并与基于BPE的基本模型及随机化形态学对齐模型进行比较。MorphAustro-Tok在输出词根后跟位置词缀标记，同时在两种控制水平下与打乱形态学记忆的模型进行对比。

Result: MorphAustro-Tok在33次实验中分别在三个粒度级别上显著提升了0.84, 1.49和3.40个chrF++分数，尽管标记数增加和模型容量并未解释这些提高。

Conclusion: 形态标记的正确性对塔加洛语机器翻译的影响在实验规模下不明显，分词效果的提升与形态学正确性无关。

Abstract: Typology-aware tokenizers are motivated by the claim that segmentation which respects morpheme boundaries preserves information that frequency-driven segmentation destroys. Studies routinely establish that it helps while assuming the boundaries must be linguistically correct. We separate them. Tagalog marks voice with infixes (s-um-ulat 'wrote', s-in-ulat 'was written'), so the root is not contiguous and no sequence of BPE merges can recover it. We build MorphAustro-Tok, a tokenizer that emits the root as a contiguous unit followed by a positional infix token, and compare it against a corpus-matched BPE baseline and, at two of three fertility levels, against a scrambled-morpheme control that preserves morph count, invertibility and per-type consistency while randomising the pseudo-infix. Across 33 runs, MorphAustro-Tok improves Tagalog machine translation over matched BPE by 0.84, 1.49 and 3.40 mean sentence-level chrF++ points. Token count does not explain the gain, since it grows as segmentation coarsens, and neither does capacity: at the coarsest level the scrambled arm carries more parameters and scores lower. Scrambling the analysis moves the gain by less than between-seed dispersion at both controlled levels, so we detect no contribution from morphological correctness at this scale.

</details>


### [141] [Prune the Summary, Not the History: Source-Judged Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20927)
*Guanghui Min,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，长期LLM代理在依赖上下文压缩时，总结往往融合了环境的信息和压缩器的推理。为了解决这一问题，提出了Source-Judged Compaction (SJC)方法，通过评分机制去除没有环境支持的总结项，从而提高了代理的跨运行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的长期LLM代理在压缩交互历史时，可能会融合环境和压缩器的信息，导致代理难以区分事实与推理。因此，需要一种方法来确保总结项有明确的环境依据。

Method: SJC提出了一种不需要额外训练步骤的方法，通过评分机制判别总结项是否基于环境输出或之前的总结。该方法使用Jev这样的非生成性决策模型，以概率形式回答来源问题，仅在压缩步骤后增加一次额外的判断请求。

Result: 实验结果显示，在AppWorld和OfficeBench上使用SJC方法，在不增加代理的令牌使用量的情况下，与基础压缩器相比，跨运行可靠性提高了2.1%-8.9%。

Conclusion: SJC方法通过对总结项进行概率评分并去除没有依据的项，有效提升了LLM代理在长期任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents rely on context compaction: when the interaction history outgrows the context window, a compressor replaces it with a natural-language summary. We show that these summaries contain more than a record of what happened. Compaction prompts ask for decisions and next steps, so each summary mixes facts established by the environment with reasoning written by the compressor, and the agent cannot tell the two apart afterwards. In an analysis of a widely used structured compressor, 83% of failed runs trace back to an action that carries out a specific summary entry, most of which no environment output supports. We propose Source-Judged Compaction (SJC), a training-free post-processing step that scores each summary entry for whether it has a source in the environment outputs or the previous summary, and removes the entries that do not. It changes neither the agent nor the compressor and adds one inexpensive judge request per compaction. The judge is Jev, a non-generative decision model that answers the source question with a calibrated probability instead of text. On AppWorld and OfficeBench with two agent models, SJC improves cross-run reliability over the underlying compressor by 2.1%-8.9% without increasing the agent's token usage.

</details>


### [142] [Same Context, Different Agents: A Behavioral Study of Semantic Layers, Orchestration and Compaction in Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20959)
*Zhivar Sourati,Marianne Menglin Liu,Zheng Wang,Rongguang Wang,Sai Ashish Somayajula,Tao Sheng,Kyu J. Han,Sujith Ravi,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了三种上下文工程技巧（CETs）对大型语言模型回答长期任务的效果，发现各CET单独使用或联合使用时表现出不同的优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已经验证了每个CET单独使用的效果，但未明确如何通过操作CETs来优化数据代理的行为，提高准确率。

Method: 通过使用七种大型语言模型在1514个长期任务中对三种CETs单独和联合使用的效果进行全面分析。

Result: 研究表明不同CETs单独使用时会形成三类数据代理的行为模式，每种CET虽然能带来特定益处，但只有匹配该CET行为模式的数据代理才会实际提高准确度。不同CETs联合使用时优劣较小。

Conclusion: 研究为选择合适的CETs提供了指导，该指导基于数据代理的行为特点、任务需求及准确率与成本的权衡。

Abstract: Answering questions over heterogeneous data means combining evidence from files, databases, documents and the web. Data agents automate this, but their success depends on context. Three context-engineering techniques (CETs) help by shaping it: semantic layers describe the data, orchestration splits work among agents, and compaction condenses interaction history. Existing studies validate each CET alone, leaving open how agent behavior shapes its effect. We present a comprehensive analysis of the three CETs, alone and combined, with seven large language models (LLMs) on 1,514 long-horizon tasks from five benchmarks, covering behavior, accuracy and cost. We find that data agents without CETs form three behavior groups: investigators, premature answerers that answer without inspecting data, and early stoppers that give up. Each CET delivers its known benefit, from more investigation to smaller prompts, but only the behavior group it matches turns that benefit into accuracy, such as premature answerers under orchestration. For investigators, the same CETs can hurt: the semantic layer stops them from looking, orchestration makes them overconfident, costing 5 percentage points, and compaction makes them repeat steps. Combining CETs yields smaller gains and losses than their sum. We derive guidance for choosing CETs by agent behavior, task demands and accuracy-cost trade-offs.

</details>


### [143] [One Bias Score, Two Factors: How Mitigations Affect Option Bias in Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20993)
*Hyunjong Ok,Minjae Park,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入混合选择率标准差（pooled SD）来结合选择偏差（偏好某些选项字母）和顺序敏感性（顺序变化对答案的影响），发现flip率和位置偏差分别与准确性及输出不确定性、选项符号先验相关，调整优先级主要减少位置偏差，而聚合解决顺序敏感性则主要减少翻转率，两者均缩小了正确答案字母的精度范围，但聚合有时也会锁定错误选项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在系统地分析多选题选项顺序对模型答案的影响，并提出一种新的混合选择率标准差（pooled SD）来量化这种影响，进一步探讨选择偏差和顺序敏感性如何影响模型的性能。

Method: 论文通过引入pooled SD来分析多个检查点和基准测试，测量选项选择的均匀性，并研究flip率和位置偏差如何与模型的准确性、输出不确定性以及选项符号先验相关。与此同时，论文还讨论了优先级校正和选项顺序聚合在降低选择偏差和顺序敏感性方面的效果。

Result: 研究发现，优先级校正主要减少了位置偏差，而聚合解决顺序敏感性则主要减少了翻转率；两者均缩小了正确答案字母之间的精度差异。然而，聚合还可能导致模型对错误答案的锁定。

Conclusion: 研究建议同时报告准确性和两个因素（flip率和位置偏差），以便区分稳定性与正确性，并且优先级校正与聚合应结合使用以优化模型性能。

Abstract: Reordering multiple-choice options can change a model's answer, an effect studied as selection bias (a preference for certain option letters) and as order sensitivity (changes in answers across option orderings). We connect these perspectives through the pooled selection-rate standard deviation (pooled SD), which measures how unevenly option letters are selected across questions and orderings. When every option of each question appears equally often at each position, the pooled SD is exactly the product of two label-free factors: the flip rate and the positional skew. The flip rate is the fraction of questions whose chosen option changes across orderings; the positional skew is the standard deviation of option-letter selection rates among those questions. Across 41 checkpoints and 12 benchmarks, the flip rate tracks accuracy and output uncertainty, while the positional skew tracks the estimated option-symbol prior. Prior correction for selection bias mainly reduces the positional skew. Aggregating each ordering with its reversal, a mitigation for order sensitivity, mainly reduces the flip rate. Both reduce the range of accuracy across correct-answer letters by similar amounts, but aggregation leaves fewer model-rank reversals. Applying prior correction before aggregation reduces both factors and further narrows this accuracy range. However, aggregation lowers the flip rate partly by settling questions on the same wrong option. We therefore recommend reporting both factors to distinguish mitigation effects, alongside accuracy to distinguish stability from correctness.

</details>


### [144] [Can LLMs Anticipate Their Cruxes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21006)
*David Reber,James Reber,Ari Holtzman,Victor Veitch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过降低计算成本来提取模型决策核心因素的方法。第一种方法是让模型在生成前就识别出核心因素，但效果不佳；第二种是模型生成回应之后再提取核心因素，但依然不充分；第三种方法是通过多次生成回应并比较来提取核心因素，这种方法虽然成本较高，但在识别全面的因素上更为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型决策背后的逻辑有助于提高模型性能和用户信任。在多轮对话或决策生成时，模型可能需要考虑多种可能性，但最终的决策理由可能被大量文本所掩盖。因此，需要一种方法来有效提取这种核心因素。

Method: 文章提出了三种方法来提取模型决策中的核心因素：首先，模型预测潜在的核心因素；其次，在生成响应后分析核心因素；最后，生成多个响应并比较它们的推理过程以找到核心因素。此外，通过使用廉价的重新排名器调用降低了计算成本。

Result: 三种方法的效果各不相同。模型预测的核心因素与实际重要的因素关联性不强。通过一次或多轮推理分析可以部分提取核心因素，但在重大决策中，多重推理始终是不可或缺的。

Conclusion: 虽然通过单一推理或少量多次推理可以部分提取核心因素，但在高风险决策中，需要通过多轮推理来理解和评估语言模型的决策过程。

Abstract: To use language models to help us make decisions we would like to understand the rationale behind the language model's outputs. In principle, the language model provides its rationale as part of its text output. However, the sheer volume of text makes this hard to use in practice. In this paper, we are interested in extracting the cruxes that lead a model to recommend one action over another. We take a crux to be a sub-question that a model's rollouts disagree on, where changing its value may have altered the conclusion. A crux is therefore a property of the distribution of a model's rollouts, not of any one rollout. We consider three strategies of increasing computational cost. First, we just ask a model to identify, a priori, what the cruxes of a decision will be. Second, we have the model actually go through the process of generating a response and then extract the cruxes that arose in its reasoning. Finally, we generate many responses and extract the cruxes by comparing the reasoning across the rollouts. We make this last procedure computationally tractable with cheap calls to a reranker, an estimated 86 times less compute than a judge. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation to assess whether the computationally inexpensive options are sufficient. They are not. Across domains and models, we find that a model's a priori assessment of the cruxes has little relation to the factors that are actually identified as important a posteriori. Using a single rollout helps, but is still inadequate for identifying the full set of cruxes. Accordingly, for high-stakes decisions (and to understand the space of language model decision making) the many rollout procedure is unavoidable.

</details>


### [145] [Learning to Read Out: Unembedding Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21025)
*Matteo He,William F. Shen,Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanovic,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种直接追踪模型读出部分的方法，并发现模型的隐藏态编码任务信息的时间早于其输出显示的时间，即使是在读出部分仍在学习的早期阶段，而改变读出部分的学习速率可以影响这一过程的发展速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
