Motivation: 该研究旨在评估大型语言模型在复杂病例中的诊断能力，并通过一种新基准测试，考察合成的多学科意见、结构化反馈对诊断准确性和临床适宜性的影响。

Method: 研究采用了一个包含300个已诊断案例的多维基准测试，这些案例来源自NEJM-MedQA。研究设计了八条单独模型的管道，生成角色特异性意见和交互式共识报告，自由格式和结构化临床推理条件下进行。由医生校准的语言模型“法官”评估了最终报告和结构化的主要角色输出。

Result: 研究表明，结构化的最终报告和全面合成意见在七个模型管道中的整体得分较高，对管理部分的得分尤为明显。与结构化的预共识主要角色意见相比，完整的结构化综合在所有共享标准上的得分更高，最大的增加体现在问题优先级、事实依据和推理一致性上。GPT-5.6-Sol 在所有评估组件中表现最佳，并且在最少的审评轮次中达成共识。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明基准测试可以区分模型能力、多学科合成的贡献、特定评估标准下的反馈结构效果、讨论动态以及推理需求，有助于评估大型语言模型在复杂疾病诊断中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate differential diagnoses for complex cases, but a useful second opinion must also be factually grounded, clinically coherent, actionable, and safe. We developed a multidimensional benchmark using 300 diagnostically resolved NEJM--MedQA cases: 110 for physician reference ratings and Judge calibration and 190 for held-out evaluation. Eight single-model pipelines generated independent role-specific opinions and interactive-consilium reports under free-form and structured clinical-reasoning conditions. A physician-calibrated LLM Judge evaluated 3,040 final reports and the corresponding structured primary-role outputs. Model identity separated report quality more strongly than response format. Structured final reports had higher overall mean scores in seven of eight model pipelines, with their clearest advantage in management (2.740 vs. 2.436). Compared with the structured pre-consilium primary-role opinion, the complete structured synthesis had higher pooled scores across all five shared criteria, with the largest increases in problem prioritization, factual grounding, and reasoning coherence. GPT-5.6-Sol led every evaluated component and reached consensus in the fewest review rounds. These findings show that the benchmark can distinguish model capability, the contribution of multidisciplinary synthesis, criterion-specific effects of response structure, deliberation dynamics, and inference requirements within one common-case evaluation.

</details>


### [222] [Knowledge That Is Linear Before Fine-Tuning Generalizes After It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22300)
*Faridoun Mehri,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，语言模型在进一步学习中是否能有效利用已记住的事实，主要取决于这些事实在预训练表示中是否容易线性读取


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分有助于支持模型泛化的活跃事实与仅可用于回忆但不支持泛化的惰性事实

Method: 通过重新定义现有关系并生成新的关系来测试模型的表现

Result: 在不同模型和关系中，预训练表示的线性可读性高度预测了新关系的泛化能力，且这种关联在控制了多种因素后仍保持一致

Conclusion: 新的关系是否能泛化取决于共享结构在预训练表示中是否容易线性读取，而不是这些事实是否能被回忆出来

Abstract: Not all facts a language model can recall support further learning equally well. We distinguish active facts, which support generalization beyond their own recall, from inert facts, which can be recalled but do not support such generalization in a given task. We study this distinction by constructing new relations from existing ones. Take the relation that maps a place to its continent (Paris -> Europe) and assign each of its answers an arbitrary new label (Europe -> crab, Asia -> zebra). The new relation then maps Paris -> crab. We fine-tune the model on the new relation for a subset of subjects, without telling it the original relation or the relabeling rule, and test whether it assigns the corresponding labels to held-out subjects that share an answer in the original relation. Having learned Paris -> crab, does it answer Munich -> crab? Across eleven language models and 70 relations, how linearly readable the original relation is in pretrained representations strongly predicts this generalization. Within each model, the two correlate across relations at Pearson r = 0.87 to 0.92. This association persists when controlling, separately and jointly, for the number of fine-tuning subjects and answers, the model's accuracy on the original relation, and how often the subjects occur in pretraining text. It is also robust to re-randomizing the label assignment and fine-tuning seed, and to replacing the animal labels with labels from thirteen other semantic categories. When we map a fine-tuned model's wrong label back to the original answer it was assigned to, Munich -> zebra becomes Munich -> Asia. Answers recovered this way are more often among the linear probe's top answers for the subject than among the pretrained model's own. Whether a new relation generalizes depends less on whether its facts can be recalled than on how readily their shared structure can be read out linearly.

</details>


### [223] [Grid-Bench: Can LLMs Predict How Discrete Worlds Change?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22312)
*Zixiang Xu,Zirui Song,Wanrong Zheng,Chiyu Zhang,Yue Huang,Yuyang Peng,Kexin Zhang,Jinkun Chen,Xiuying Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Yan Liu,Laurent Itti,Frank Shyu,Xiaoqiang Luo,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Luke Simon,Xiyang Hu,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究展示了大型语言模型（LLMs）预测连续空间操作最终状态的能力。Grid-Bench 是一个用于评估模型在长期操作历史记录中的性能基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估大型语言模型在处理连续空间操作序列时的预测能力，引入了 Grid-Bench，一种可以执行的基准测试，它涉及将操作应用于表示为二维、三维和四维网格的离散世界。

Method: Grid-Bench 被用来评估六个大型语言模型。它包含一个初始网格、明确的规则，以及以文本表达的事件历史。模型需要预测最终的完整网格。参考执行确保每个预测值都是可验证的，支持对不同空间操作、历史长度和工作量的比较。

Result: 评估结果表明，随着历史记录的增长，模型完全状态的准确性急剧下降。即使在相似的单项事件表现中，模型之间仍存在显著差异。一些模型即使在长期历史记录中也能恢复多个正确的变化，而不仅仅是复制初始状态能带来的。

Conclusion: Grid-Bench 显示了在不同空间条件下完整恢复和返回状态差异的直接度量能力。研究表明，大型语言模型在预测连续操作最终状态方面具有挑战性，需要进一步研究以改善其性能。

Abstract: Spatial instructions describe how to move objects, transform regions, or modify their surroundings. When several operations are applied in sequence, each acts on a configuration shaped by earlier changes. How accurately can large language models (LLMs) predict the resulting spatial state? We introduce Grid-Bench, an executable benchmark in which eight families of operations transform discrete worlds represented as two-, three-, and four-dimensional grids. Given an initial grid, explicit rules, and an event history expressed as text, a model predicts the complete final grid. Reference execution makes every predicted value checkable, supporting comparisons across spatial operations, history lengths, and workloads. Evaluating six LLMs shows that complete-state accuracy falls sharply as histories grow, with substantial divergence among models that perform similarly on single events. Models with similar exact-match accuracy can nevertheless differ substantially in the number of incorrect cells on shared instances. Even over long histories, some models recover many correct changes beyond what copying the initial state would yield. Grid-Bench makes both complete recovery and these differences in returned states directly measurable across a range of spatial conditions.

</details>


### [224] [MindEval2: Clinically Grounded Patients and Rubrics for Evaluating Language Models in Mental Health Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22314)
*John Mendonça,Daniel Tschernutter,José Pombal,Areti Vassilopoulos,Catarina Botelho,Ignacio Sarasua,Maya D'Eon,Nuno Guerreiro,Ricardo Rei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MindEval2是一种新的评价框架，旨在解决当前评估AI聊天机器人在心理健康支持中的表现的方法存在的问题，它引入了Mental Health Quality Rubrics (MHQR) 评分体系和MindSim心理模拟器，以提高评估的透明度和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的评估方法存在被模拟的病人表现过于坦诚、隐晦的评价尺度解释不清等问题，影响了评估的质量。为了提高评估效果，需要一种新方法来解决这些问题。

Method: 通过与博士级别心理学家合作，MindEval2开发了一种Mental Health Quality Rubrics (MHQR) 评分体系，以解决原来评估体系存在的问题。同时，MindSim心理模拟器使用精心设计的病人档案和工具，进行更复杂、更真实交互，并包含了记忆组件以模拟时间跨度的维度。

Result: MindEval2在18个模型上进行了测试，结果显示，尽管最新的模型在表面质量和边界问题上已有较大改善，但在指导校准方面的表现仍然不佳，是影响整体效果的关键因素。

Conclusion: 为了进一步提高AI聊天机器人支持心理健康的支持效果，需要关注指导校准的问题。MindEval2提供了一种新的评估框架，有助于推动这一领域的发展。

Abstract: With people increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, the need to evaluate these tools is becoming more pressing. Recent benchmarks attempt to mimic a realistic setting by simulating multi-turn interactions with synthetic patients and evaluating them with an LLM-as-a-judge. However, the usefulness of these methods is limited because simulated patients can be overly forthcoming and self-reflective, and judgments typically involve opaque Likert scales with unclear practical utility. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MindEval2, an evaluation framework that consists of two components: Mental Health Quality Rubrics (MHQR), for evaluation, and MindSim, for patient simulation. For MHQR, we collaborate with PhD-level psychologists to develop a set of rubrics that break down response quality into explicit operational criteria that are more interpretable and actionable than existing evaluation taxonomies. MindSim, in turn, is an LLM harness that combines carefully-designed patient profiles with tools, enabling more varied, challenging, and realistic interactions than existing baselines. The harness also contains a memory component that is updated between each interaction, enabling MindEval2 to model the longitudinal aspect of mental health. We evaluate 18 proprietary and open-weight models on MindEval2, finding that while frontier models have largely improved on issues related to surface level quality and boundaries, guidance calibration remains the dominant failure mode across all models, accounting for most of the quality gap.

</details>


### [225] [Training Reasoning Models to Generate Diverse Scientific Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22324)
*Manya Wadhwa,Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Hiroaki Hayashi,Yu Li,Tuhin Chakrabarty,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了文档导向的想法生成，通过集层面的强化学习目标来增强想法的多样性和高质量。实验结果表明，作者训练的模型在创意用途（结合质量和多样性）方面优于Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-6 Astra，并且新增多样性未降低执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM工作主要集中在提高单个想法的质量上，而忽略多样性的问题。本文试图通过集层面的RL目标，来同时提升多样性与高质量的想法生成。

Method: 本文提出了一个新的训练框架，即将LLM在一个生成过程中写出K个基于源文档的结构化想法卡片，并且这些想法作为一个整体被得分，每个想法的质量由它与其他先前想法的差异程度加权得到。

Result: 作者训练的8B模型在无污染基准测试中，在创意用途方面优于Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-6 Astra。人类专家的初步研究也显示出正面成果，尽管新想法包含的不完全相同，但独特想法的数量相近。此外，编码算法的实验也验证了模型的多样性提升并未影响执行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法显示了在想法生成中同时优化多样性和高质量的实际应用潜力，是一种创新的训练方式。

Abstract: One of the first steps of a research pipeline is generating ideas. Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate this step, but most of this work optimizes the quality of individual ideas, while overlooking diversity. In our work, we study document-grounded ideation, focusing on increasing the diversity of LLM generated ideas and ensuring they are high quality. Given prior papers, a model must propose a set of ideas that are each high-quality and clearly distinct from one another, while also being grounded in the input documents. To optimize for this, we propose training with a set-level reinforcement learning objective. In our setup, in a single generation, the model writes K structured idea cards grounded in the source papers, and the set is scored jointly, where each idea's quality is weighted by its distance from the ideas written before it. This teaches the model to steer each new idea away from its predecessors. We show that our trained 8B model achieves higher creative utility (a measure combining quality and diversity) than Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-6 Astra on our contamination-free benchmark. In a pilot human study with four experts, our sets are judged as promising as Claude Opus 4.8's and contain marginally more unique ideas. When a coding agent runs proof-of-concept experiments on the ideas, our model's success@k is comparable to Claude Opus 4.8's, so we see that the added diversity does not cost any executability.

</details>


### [226] [OmniVerifier-C1: Decomposed Graded Verifier for Rule-Governed Generative World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22355)
*Bowei Liu,Xinchen Zhang,Xuhuan Li,Tianlin Zhang,Kaian Jiang,Jingwei Liu,Congyang Zhao,Yifei Qian,Junhong Liu,Zhiheng Li,Yujiu Yang,Yang Cai,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一种能够自动分解复杂指令并提供连续评分的生成式视觉验证器，通过结合子问题级二元判断和全局对齐评分的Decoupled RL策略进行训练，该验证器在多重模态批评任务中的泛化能力更强。理论分析及实验结果表明，相比于二元验证器，点级评分验证器在传统图文配对任务以及基于规则的生成模型中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高视觉验证器在复杂多模态场景中的鲁棒性能，该研究打破了传统二元验证器只能做出真/假决定的局面，设计了一种新的验证器可以将复杂指令分解为细粒度部分并给出连续评分，这对提高多模态场景中对生成式视觉任务的综合评价具有重要意义。

Method: 研究提出了一种结合子问题级二元判断和全局对齐评分的Decoupled RL训练策略，并在传统的图文配对任务和基于规则的生成模型中进行了验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，新的验证器在传统图文配对任务中的表现优于传统的二元验证器，并在基于规则的世界建模任务中也表现出较高的准确性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了分段评分验证模型在复杂多模态场景中的有效性，并对其在其他任务中的可行性进行了初步探索。

Abstract: Generative visual verifiers play a crucial role in advancing multimodal intelligence. To enhance the robustness of visual verifiers in complex multimodal scenarios involving compositional requirements, we move beyond conventional binary verifiers that produce only True/False discrete judgments and introduce a verifier that automatically decomposes complex prompts or environmental rules into fine-grained components and assigns pointwise scores to provide a continuous and more informative critique signal. Our experiments further demonstrate that these structured and interpretable outputs improve the verifier's generalization to out-of-domain multimodal critic tasks. To train this verifier, we introduce a Decoupled RL training strategy that jointly optimizes sub-question-level binary judgment and global alignment scoring, using precise local feedback to develop fine-grained verification capabilities that provide a foundation for reliable continuous scoring at the global level. Under a general reinforcement learning framework, we first empirically demonstrate that the proposed pointwise graded verifier significantly outperforms binary verifiers in traditional text-to-image scenarios. We then extend our investigation to rule-governed generative world modeling where generative models are leveraged for world simulation and world reasoning under explicit rules. From a theoretical standpoint, we further show that a pairwise verification paradigm yields more accurate critiques than the pointwise formulation when ground-truth images are available, with empirical results on diverse structured world-modeling tasks such as Maze and Sudoku further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Overall, our findings suggest a key insight: the transition from generative models to structured world-modeling agents critically hinges on the availability of accurate pairwise graded visual verifiers.

</details>


### [227] [MeddeX: A Retrieval-Augmented LLaMA-3 System with Gemini Vision for Evidence-Backed Medical Report Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20632)
*Rajdeep Pandhare,Rohit Kundaikar,Yakub Kojar,Shreyansh Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeddeX 提出了一种综合了 QLoRA 微调的 LLaMA-3 模型、检索增强生成（RAG）和多模态 Gemini Vision 层的医学报告分析器，能够处理复杂的医学报告并提供准确的摘要，同时提高了处理和比较报告的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医疗保健中生成的复杂医疗报告挑战着临床专业人员的时间和解释负担，手动审查过程既慢又容易出错，难以扩展。

Method: MeddeX 使用 QLoRA 微调的 LLaMA-3 模型结合 Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) 和多模态 Gemini Vision 层，通过智能混合提取管道处理带垃圾文本检测机制的文本文档、扫描图像基于的 PDF 以及直接放射学图像。同时，它还引入了报告范围内的检索、自动化纵向报告比较引擎和自我修复的回填管道。

Result: 与基础的 LLaMA3-Med42-8B 模型相比，MeddeX 在 ROUGE-1 (+9.2%)、ROUGE-2 (+10.0%)、ROUGE-L (+11.8%)、BLEU (+71.4%) 和 BERTScore (+0.8%) 上表现出一致的改进，验证了领域特定微调的好处。

Conclusion: MeddeX 代表了 AI 协助临床决策支持的一个实用且稳健的步骤。

Abstract: Modern healthcare generates increasingly complex medical reports that challenge clinical professionals in terms of time and interpretive burden. Manual review processes are slow, error-prone, and difficult to scale. This paper presents MeddeX, a Medical Report Analyzer that combines a QLoRA fine-tuned LLaMA-3 (8B) model with Retrieval-Augmented Gen eration (RAG) and a multimodal Gemini Vision layer to deliver accurate, concise, and evidence-backed summaries of medical documents. The system handles text-native PDFs, scanned image based PDFs, and direct radiological images through an intelligent hybrid extraction pipeline that includes a novel garbage-text detection mechanism for corrupted and UTF-16-encoded doc uments. Beyond single-report Q&A, MeddeX introduces report scoped retrieval for precise contextual grounding, an automated longitudinal report comparison engine, and a self-healing backfill pipeline that lazily processes legacy documents on demand. A FastAPI backend, Supabase vector store, and Next.js fron tend complete the end-to-end architecture. Evaluation against the base LLaMA3-Med42-8B model demonstrates consistent improvements across ROUGE-1 (+9.2%), ROUGE-2 (+10.0%), ROUGE-L (+11.8%), BLEU (+71.4%), and BERTScore (+0.8%), validating the benefit of domain-specific fine-tuning. MeddeX represents a practical and robust step toward AI-assisted clinical decision support.

</details>


### [228] [KULTURE Bench: A Benchmark for Assessing Language Model in Korean Cultural Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07251)
*Xiaonan Wang,Jinyoung Yeo,Joon-Ho Lim,Hansaem Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KULTURE Bench是一个专为评估韩国文化理解与推理解能力的新评价框架，用于测试不同语言语料库训练的语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的多语言基准测试常使用翻译自英文的版本，其中可能含有西方文化偏见，不能准确评估其他语言和文化。因此，为解决这一研究空白，我们提出了KULTURE Bench框架。

Method: 该框架包含文化新闻、成语和诗歌等数据集，用于从词、句和段落三个层面对语言模型的文化理解与推理解能力进行评估。

Result: 研究表明，模型在理解与韩国文化深层方面相关文本的理解上仍有较大的提升空间。

Conclusion: KULTURE Bench框架的成功开发为更全面评估不同语言模型的跨文化理解能力提供了新的途径，突显了跨文化偏见在多语言基准测试中的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models have exhibited significant enhancements in performance across various tasks. However, the complexity of their evaluation increases as these models generate more fluent and coherent content. Current multilingual benchmarks often use translated English versions, which may incorporate Western cultural biases that do not accurately assess other languages and cultures. To address this research gap, we introduce KULTURE Bench, an evaluation framework specifically designed for Korean culture that features datasets of cultural news, idioms, and poetry. It is designed to assess language models' cultural comprehension and reasoning capabilities at the word, sentence, and paragraph levels. Using the KULTURE Bench, we assessed the capabilities of models trained with different language corpora and analyzed the results comprehensively. The results show that there is still significant room for improvement in the models' understanding of texts related to the deeper aspects of Korean culture.

</details>


### [229] [Bridging the Gap: Converting Read Text to Conversational Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18001)
*Parshav Singla,Agnik Banerjee,Aaditya Arora,Shruti Aggarwal,Anil Kumar Verma,Vikram C M,Raj Prakash Gohil,Gopal Kumar Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PACC的方法，通过高级神经网络调整读取语音的语调、重音和节奏等特征，使其更符合自然对话，显著提高了模型的准确性和自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术无法满足自然对话所需的语调变化，特别是语音助手、客户服务和语言学习工具的应用场景。

Method: 该方法利用了高保真生成对抗网络（HiFi-GAN）对读取语音的语调、重音和节奏等特征进行分析和修改。

Result: 研究表明，使用PACC方法的语音转换模型在自然度和准确度方面有显著提升，并且已建立新的基准和评估模型的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为语音转换任务带来了新的突破，并展示了此方法可以成功应用于其他语音转换领域。

Abstract: In recent advancements within speech processing, converting read speech to conversational speech has gained significant attention. The primary challenge in this domain is maintaining naturalness and intelligibility while minimizing computational overhead for real-time applications. Traditional read speech often lacks the nuanced prosodic variation essential for natural conversational interactions, posing challenges for applications in virtual assistants, customer service, and language learning tools. This paper introduces a novel approach, Prosodic Adjustment with Conversational Context (PACC), aimed at converting read speech into natural conversational speech used in various modern applications. PACC utilizes advanced deep neural networks to analyze and modify prosodic features such as intonation, stress, and rhythm. Unlike conventional methods, our approach uses High-Fidelity Generative Adversarial Networks (HiFi-GAN) for speech synthesis. Our experimental results demonstrate significant improvements in speech conversion, enhancing naturalness and achieving better model accuracy with additional training on speech datasets. This research establishes new benchmarks in speech conversion tasks and Mean Opinion Score (MOS) evaluation for testing model accuracy, and we show that our approach can be successfully extended to other speech conversion applications.

</details>


### [230] [Truth for Believable AI: Expressed Doubt, Provenance, and Belief Revision as an Engineerable Stance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26035)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文测试了一种行为层能否在固定语言模型上实现表达不确定性、基于证明来源的断言以及显式的信念修正，虽然部分评估达标，但整体效果有限，特别是在处理事实集中不确定性的集中化时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探讨基于证据的断言及其表达和信念修正的行为实现，提高对话代理在表达自信程度和提供信息来源方面的透明度。

Method: 通过在合成模型和Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct基础上构建评估基准，并使用构造的方法对表现层进行评估，展示其在保持现有性能的同时增强对话的可信度。

Result: 在合成模型上实现了全部的评估标准，但在真实模型上，部分标准未能达成预期，例如概率门控配置的准确性低于随机水平，而一致性门控配置则表现出较高的区分能力。此外，不同配置下的评估结果依赖于选择方法，且当前事实集下无法验证所有结论。

Conclusion: 试验表明，通过表达不确定性和基于证据的断言及显性的信念修正可以增强对话代理的行为透明度，对于基准和模型特定的表现层实现效果在某些领域里是有效的。但在面对大规模事实集时需要完善的机制，以确保评估的真实性。

Abstract: Conversational agents often express answers in a uniformly confident register. We test whether expressed uncertainty, provenance-aware assertion, and explicit belief revision can be implemented as a behavior layer over a fixed language model; we do not test believability or trust. The layer combines three epistemic states, per-claim confidence and typed provenance, a provenance-gated expression rule, and a persistent revision store with auditable acknowledgments and partial resistance to false corrections. We evaluate it on a constructed, mechanically scored multi-session benchmark using a synthetic model and Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. The synthetic instrument passes all five checks. On the real model, acknowledgment soundness, a by-construction guarantee, holds in 100% of cases, and true corrections are accepted more often than false ones (0.44 vs. 0.15 on held beliefs; 0.875 vs. 0.420 including rule-accepted corrections of unheld facts), but the pre-specified expression-fidelity, contradiction-separation, and provenance margins fail. A disclosed post hoc analysis shows that expression gated on mean answer-token probability ranks correctness below chance end to end (AUC 0.41, conversation-clustered), whereas gating on sampling consistency discriminates (AUC 0.66). A consistency-gated configuration selected from this finding and evaluated under a separately committed protocol meets the conversation-level manipulation and capability-equivalence criteria and replicates on a redrawn conversation set. The manipulation result is selection-dependent, and both criteria remain unresolved when uncertainty is clustered over the 60 facts. The supported conclusions are limited to the by-construction audit guarantee, store-dependent partial correction discrimination, and a benchmark- and model-specific failure of token-probability gating; scaling the fact base is required before human evaluation.

</details>


### [231] [Exploring the Design Space of Permutation Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20635)
*Zhe Xu,Mingfu Liang,Kavosh Asadi,Jean-Pascal Billaud,Tianyi Chen,Yanhui Guo,Christina Huang,Baekjin Kim,Jimmy Kim,Yufei Li,Daqi Sun,Jiachen Sun,Dian Yu,Zaiwei Zhang,Kaushik Rangadurai,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Xi Liu,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过三种不同的参数化方法研究了使用语言模型进行排列生成的问题，对比了硬分配、软分配和直接排序得分预测的不同效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前使用语言模型进行排列生成需要文本条件排序和全局一对一候选索引到输出位置的映射，该研究旨在探索不同参数化方法对排列生成的影响。

Method: 研究采用了三种不同的参数化方法：硬分配预测、软分配预测和直接排序得分预测。研究人员在三个Amazon域进行了推荐重排任务的评估，观察了候选集大小为10和20的情况。

Result: 硬分配和软分配方法在Recall@1上的表现与受限掩码扩散基线相当。直接排序得分预测不依赖于匈牙利分配或Sinkhorn规范化，能够保证排列的有效性。在后续的评估中，排列生成质量提升不明显，且效果很大程度上取决于状态表示和训练目标。

Conclusion: 该研究得出结论，对于使用语言模型去噪器的排列扩散，噪声污染、预测参数化、目标和解码器的设计应联合进行。

Abstract: Permutation generation with language models requires both text-conditioned ranking and a global one-to-one assignment between candidates and output positions. We study this problem through three diffusion-LLM parameterizations: hard-assignment prediction over shuffled candidate-index sequences, soft-assignment prediction over doubly stochastic matrices, and direct ordering-score prediction followed by sorting. The three families place permutation constraints at different stages of prediction and decoding and therefore support different training objectives and sampling procedures. We evaluate these designs on recommendation reranking across three Amazon domains with candidate sets of size 10 and 20. At one function evaluation, the hard- and soft-assignment variants achieve Recall@1 comparable to a constrained masked-diffusion baseline, whereas direct ordering-score prediction guarantees valid permutations without Hungarian assignment or Sinkhorn normalization. Additional function evaluations do not consistently improve ranking quality, and their effect depends strongly on the state representation and training objective. These results show that corruption, prediction parameterization, objective, and decoder should be designed jointly for permutation diffusion with language-model denoisers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Independent Data-Integrity Verification for LLM-Driven Functional Coverage Closure in Hardware Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21570)
*Venkata Krishna Kumar Vedantam*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种小规模的覆盖关闭代理，该代理应用于LLM循环过程中。它通过从仿真报告中读取覆盖数据，而非模型本身的报告，确保了数据完整性，并通过分离的计分板进行验证。代理在三个架构不同的RTL设计中实现了100%的覆盖度，并发现了一个时钟域穿越错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上仅依靠覆盖率来验证是否达到某个状态，但无法检查状态转移过程中的数据是否有效。因此，该研究旨在提出一种新方法来验证在LLM生成测试中的数据完整性。

Method: 该研究的方法是设计一个代理，该代理从仿真报告中提取覆盖率数据，而不是依赖模型自身的覆盖率报告。数据完整性则通过与模型分离的计分板进行验证。研究还在三个不同的RTL设计上进行了验证。

Result: 实验结果显示，该代理在三个不同架构的RTL设计中，能够在预算内将覆盖率提升至100%，并发现了一个时钟域穿越错误。尽管这些结果是初步的，研究者也承认需要更多的实验证据来支持其有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法是有潜力提高数据完整性的，但在大规模应用之前需要更多实验证据支持，包括不同的设计的测试、不同阶段的基线比较和更广泛的评估。

Abstract: LLM agents can write directed tests that close functional coverage, but coverage only shows that a state was reached, not that the data on the way there was correct. This is a long-known point in verification and we do not claim it as new. We describe a small coverage-closure agent that applies it to an LLM loop: coverage is read from the report that the simulation itself exports (cocotb-coverage YAML) and never taken from the model's account, and data integrity is checked by scoreboards that are separate from the model. On three architecturally distinct RTL designs (an asynchronous FIFO, a UART transmitter and an AHB2APB bridge) the agent raised coverage from 91.7%, 88.9% and 67.4% to 100% within its 8-iteration budget in every logged run, using one design-agnostic agent. A reactive scoreboard monitor attached to the FIFO's base testbench also found a clock-domain-crossing bug in the author's own stimulus code. The monitor is not yet attached to the LLM-generated tests, so their data integrity is not independently checked; one manual check on a single FIFO test is reported as preliminary. We list the weaknesses of this evidence: three designs, no baseline, no ablation, and logged runs that come from several agent checkpoints.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Top-Down Microarchitecture Analysis Without Causal Modelling Is Dangerous!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21387)
*Chenxi Wang,Lei Wang,Wanling Gao,Guoxin Kang,Fanda Fan,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: CTMA通过引入对象因果模型，重新审视并捕获工作负载、指令、CPU和系统间的三种因果路径，提出了新的认知，并据此提出了动态推测限流机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的方法，如TMA，在性能分析时存在因果盲点，导致建筑师可能进行无效的硬件优化。CTMA旨在通过引入对象因果模型来解决这些问题。

Method: CTMA的方法是利用对象因果模型来明确地建模工作负载、指令、CPU和系统的三种因果路径，并通过实验来验证这些发现。

Result: 实验结果揭示了几个反常识的观点，包括CPI自陷、前端受限情况下的过度推测、ILP窗口崩溃、Op缓存SMT重叠以及幻影状态等，这促使提出了新的机制来优化性能。

Conclusion: CTMA通过提供更全面的性能分析视角，支持架构师做出更加有效的硬件和软件优化决策。

Abstract: Top-Down Microarchitecture Analysis (TMA) is the standard for identifying CPU bottlenecks. However, TMA implicitly models performance via a single causal path: Workload(dataset, algorithm) => CPU(CPI) => System(Performance) (microarchitectural efficiency path). It overlooks two hidden causal paths: Workload(dataset, algorithm) => Instructions(Retired) => System(Performance) (algorithmic efficiency path) and Workload(dataset, algorithm) => Instructions(Retired) => CPU(CPI) => System(Performance) (microarchitecture-algorithm efficiency path). This causal blindness often misleads architects into futile hardware optimizations. To address this, we propose Causal Top-Down Microarchitecture Analysis (CTMA). Utilizing the object causal model, CTMA explicitly models these three causal paths among the workload, instructions, CPU, and system.
  We validate CTMA across Intel, AMD, and ARM platforms. Our experiments reveal counterintuitive insights: (1) CPI Profiling Blindness: Algorithmic instruction bloat can double elapsed time while the CPI profile remains frozen. (2) Over-Speculation Congestion: Throttling the Frontend paradoxically improves performance in pointer-chasing algorithms. (3) ILP Window Collapse: Extreme data topologies shift bottlenecks from Backend ROB capacity to Frontend fill-rate starvation. (4) Op-Cache SMT Overlap: Ultra-small footprints yield SMT speedups on Intel but cause severe thrashing on AMD. (5) "Ghost State" Artifact: Stale pipeline mitigations on Intel Xeon Gold 5120T artificially boost performance by 22.75% on SPEC CPU2017 and 42.2% on SPEC CPU2026. Based on this, we propose a dynamic speculation throttling mechanism.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [The World Won't Wait: Evaluating and Teaching Agents Under a Live Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20665)
*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yeqing Shen,Ning Ding,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究引入了RealtimeBench基准测试，并提出了Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement (TORSI) 方法，旨在使大型语言模型（LLM）代理能够在持续变化的环境中保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在处理复杂交互任务时存在推理和工具调用耗时，尤其是在动态环境中，动作的延迟可能导致错误。因此，研究者们希望开发一种能评估连续运行游戏期间代理性能的方法，以及实现一个方法来提高代理在动态环境中的实时性能。

Method: 研究者开发了RealtimeBench基准测试框架，包括180个任务和六种不同类型的游戏。他们进一步提出了一种Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement (TORSI) 方法，结合了慢速教师和快速学生。慢速教师在暂停的游戏之间学习失败并测试修正措施，然后撰写可重用的指令交给快速学生执行。最后，通过验证分数来决定哪些更新被接受。

Result: TORSI方法在暂停和不断运行游戏的两组评估中均显示出优于其他模型的表现，尤其在持续运行的游戏中，Jev模型的平均测试评分从13.51提高到了24.19。此外，研究者发现撰写出来的指令还可以增强其他三个模型的平均得分。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，TORSI方法是一种有效的解决策略，可以提高大型语言模型代理在实时环境中执行任务的能力。这为未来构建能够有效应对持续变化的现实世界的LLM提供了一条途径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can solve complex interactive tasks, yet reasoning and tool calls take time. In continuously changing environments, an otherwise correct action may arrive too late. We introduce RealtimeBench, with 180 tasks across six game types, to evaluate agents while games continue running during inference. Paired evaluations show that GPT-6.1-sol outperforms the faster Jev when games are paused, but falls behind when they keep running. We propose Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement (TORSI): a slow teacher studies failures and tests corrections in paused games between attempts, then writes reusable instructions for a fast student. The student acts without teacher calls during gameplay, and validation scores determine which updates are adopted. Two rounds raise Jev's average test score from 13.51 to 24.19 on a 0--100 progress scale, improving all six game types. The instructions also improve average scores for three other models without retuning. These findings point toward real-time intelligence: enabling powerful LLM agents to remain effective in a continuously changing world by organizing deliberation, learning, and action across timescales.

</details>


### [235] [Evaluating Model Capabilities After Benchmark Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20667)
*Morio Hamada,Jehyeok Yeon,Matteo Migliarini,Justin Olive,Magda Dubois*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了即使基准评估分数达到饱和，模型解决方案的详细记录（transcripts）是否仍能用于评估模型能力。通过历史基准和模型版本，模拟了分数饱和情况，发现饱和transcripts仍保留了能力信息，并且双盲评估方法能显著减少预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术的发展，基准评估分数达到饱和，不能再区分高技术水平的模型。为了探索这些详细解决方案是否还能提供有用的信息以评估模型能力。

Method: 通过模拟基准分数饱和的情境，使用几种方法评估模型解决方案的详细记录（transcripts），这些方法包括两两比较，单次解码评分，基于解码特征或嵌入的预测。

Result: 研究发现，即使分数饱和，模型解决方案的详细记录仍保留了部分能力信息。双盲评估方法表现最佳，能将预测误差减少5.3倍。该信息分布在transcripts的各个部分，但在某些渠道（如推理和工具调用）上更为集中。

Conclusion: 尽管存在分布变化（如早期引入的推理模型评估误差偏低）等问题，饱和基准的详细记录仍保留了有用的预测能力信号，需开发新的方法以稳健地提取这些信号。

Abstract: Rapid advances in AI capabilities are causing benchmarks to saturate. Once several models approach perfect scores, a benchmark loses its power to differentiate between them, and with it its utility for informing AI safety cases and frontier policies. Yet benchmark items produce far more information than the scores we typically extract from them: a full transcript of how each model solved the task. We investigate whether these transcripts can be used to assess model capabilities even after benchmark scores have saturated. We simulate saturation retrospectively using historical benchmark and model releases, and test whether protocols that access only correctly solved transcripts from saturated benchmarks can predict capabilities measured on held-out, unsaturated items. We evaluate representative contemporary approaches for extracting information from model transcripts: pairwise judging, single-transcript absolute scoring, and predictions from deterministic transcript features or embeddings. We find that saturated transcripts retain capability information beyond benchmark accuracy. Amongst our evaluated protocols, pairwise judging performs best, reducing prediction error by 5.3 times compared to a baseline using only saturated benchmark scores. This capability-relevant information is distributed across transcript sections, but concentrated in specific channels of information such as reasoning and tool-calls. However, the predictive power of protocols does not always generalise across distribution shifts -- for example by underestimating the capabilities of the first reasoning models introduced in early 2025. Saturated benchmarks thus retain capability-predicting signal in their task transcripts, but extending the lifetime of existing benchmarks requires methods designed to extract these signals robustly.

</details>


### [236] [Value-Informed Schedule Trimming for Accelerated Sequential Monte Carlo Guidance in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20669)
*Jaehyeon Kim,Ziseok Lee,Siyoung Choi,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VISTA提出了一种无需训练的方法，它能够自动在固定预算下选择稀疏指导的时间步长，显著提高了文本、生物序列和图像生成任务的效果，减少了生成过程的时间延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的稀疏指导方法使用固定启发式策略选择指导时间步长，效果在不同任务上差异较大，VISTA通过优化近似理想全程指导的概率路径，实现自动选择更有效的稀疏时间步长。

Method: VISTA方法分为三个部分：1) 将稀疏指导近似为理想全程指导的概率路径；2) 利用少量全程指导生成的值统计信息估计路径偏差的上界；3) 使用动态规划优化经验的近似目标。

Result: 在多项任务中，VISTA展示了任务适应性的稀疏指导策略，并在大多数测试场景中优于手工设计的稀疏策略，部分任务达到接近全程指导的性能。例如，在有毒文本生成中，VISTA实现了0.99的独特毒性比，而全程指导为1.00，同时将每代生成的时间延迟从204.23秒降低到38.16秒，提高了5.35倍。

Conclusion: VISTA通过自动选择优化的时间步长显著降低了稀疏指导方法的计算成本，实现了高质量生成的高效生成过程。

Abstract: Reward-guided discrete diffusion enables test-time alignment with downstream objectives, but applying Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) guidance at every denoising step is computationally expensive. Active guidance steps require additional reward evaluations and, for gradient-based proposals, value-gradient computations. Sparse guidance reduces this cost by guiding only a subset of timesteps, but commonly used schedules are fixed heuristics whose effectiveness can vary substantially across tasks. We propose VISTA, a training-free method that automatically selects guided timesteps under a fixed budget. VISTA formulates sparse guidance as approximation of the ideal full-guidance probability path and derives an estimable upper bound on \(\ell_1\) path deviation. Using value statistics collected from a small number of full-guidance warmup generations, VISTA optimizes the resulting empirical surrogate exactly via dynamic programming. The warmup generations can be retained as model outputs, and after schedule selection VISTA has the same per-generation guidance complexity as other sparse schedules with the same budget. Across text, biological sequence, and image generation tasks, VISTA discovers task-dependent schedules and outperforms hand-designed sparse schedules in most tested settings, while approaching or exceeding full-guidance performance on several task metrics. In toxic-text generation, for example, VISTA attains a 0.99 unique-toxic ratio compared with 1.00 for full guidance while reducing post-selection per-generation latency from 204.23 s to 38.16 s, a 5.35$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [237] [AlphaDiana: A Measurement System for Agents in Verifiable Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20675)
*Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Weikai Huang,Xuan Li,Chentao Cao,Xiao Feng,Xiangyu Lu,Jinbo Hu,Yi Xie,Nico Pelleriti,Shiyang Liu,Menghan Lu,Max Zimmer,Brando Miranda,Jiangchao Yao,Bo Liu,Sanmi Koyejo,Sebastian Pokutta,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了AlphaDiana测量系统，用于评估代理模型和工具的性能，并研究了多种代理模型在不同条件下的表现。实验发现，代理性能的提升并不一致，且工具和技能的干预措施对提高准确性帮助不大。研究通过比较代理的响应正确性和执行状态，关联了资源使用情况，辅助诊断性能问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解代理性能需要将执行条件与结果、行为和资源使用联系起来，而当前方法没有很好地实现这一点。

Method: 研究设计了AlphaDiana测量系统，该系统具有模块化的数据集、代理、环境和评分器接口，并支持宏观和微观级别的代理性能分析。

Result: 研究发现，代理和工具的综合性能并不总是提升，某些情况下代理自己就能解决问题而引入工具反而会降低准确率。技能指导和记忆保留对提高准确性没有显著帮助。资源使用情况与准确性的关联也揭示了一些有趣的现象，如低令牌熵可能伴随错误的答案。

Conclusion: AlphaDiana提供了一种新的测量代理模型和工具性能的方法，揭示了在不同执行条件下的性能差异，并通过资源记录和性能指标比较了不同代理的表现。

Abstract: Agents pair reasoning models with harnesses that organize model calls, execute tools, load skill instructions, and retain history in memory. Understanding agent performance requires linking execution conditions to outcomes, behavior, and resource use. We introduce AlphaDiana, a measurement system with modular interfaces for datasets, agents, environments, and scorers. It preserves harness-native control loops while controlling file reuse and memory retention separately. AlphaDiana supports macro analyses of complete agents and micro interventions on agent components such as tools, skills, and memory under matched conditions. We study three open-source models and three open-source harnesses on five answer-based benchmarks, with two models also tested on two interactive benchmarks. We find that harnesses do not consistently improve the accuracy of reasoning: gains on questions unsolved by the model alone can be outweighed by losses on questions it answers correctly without tools. This inconsistency extends to component interventions: neither skill guidance nor retained history reliably improves accuracy. Behavior and output-token use must be assessed with correctness: low token entropy can accompany wrong answers, and less output per execution can mean more output tokens per correct answer when accuracy falls. AlphaDiana links answer correctness and execution status to traces and resource records for comparing agents and diagnosing failures under shared task and resource policies.

</details>


### [238] [When Correctness Feedback Erases Evidence: Phase-Separated Completeness Auditing in Verus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20676)
*Adarsh Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Verus/Rust任务中，无反馈发现比纠错反馈提高了已认证证据的产出，尤其是在不同的模型族中，而经过不同修正阶段的执行与已有的未经强化的合同相比，具有更强的规范修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现正确性反馈实际上可能抑制规范完整性审计的目标，因为它倾向于隐瞒那些实现了书面合同但违反了预期行为的程序，这与审计希望揭露证据的目标相矛盾。

Method: 通过对比基准验证器（IntentShield）中的纠错反馈回路和独立发现的无反馈情况的效果，分别使用12个Verus/Rust任务跨越四种模型族进行对照实验，还对比了经过不同修正阶段的执行情况。

Result: 无反馈发现对通过验证的程序有超过两倍的覆盖率，纠正反馈回路模式在所有模型族中均表现落后，且在修复断点后也无明显改善。独立执行无需重用反馈，并匹配所有覆盖率。修正后的规范表现出更强大的实施成功，无反馈发现的证据比削弱合同的情况明显更有效。

Conclusion: 规范完整性的审计和正确性的修复之间存在直接的对立目标。审计应该保留并认证已知的不完整性，然后再去修复它。

Abstract: Correctness feedback suppresses the very evidence that specification-completeness auditing is designed to uncover: programs that satisfy a written contract but violate intended behavior. We demonstrate this objective reversal on 12 Verus/Rust tasks across four model families under matched call budgets. Independent no-feedback discovery more than doubles certified-witness yield relative to IntentShield's corrective-feedback loop (59.0% versus 27.1%). A format-matched iterative control places the larger share of this gap in feedback content, not independent restarts. The advantage holds in every model family and survives parser repair. A separate phase-separated execution matches the aggregate yield without reusing responses; most certified witnesses also fail held-out cases. Preserving these witnesses delivers stronger specification repair: Phase-Revised contracts achieve 81.3% implementation success, versus 62.5% for IS-Revised, with Direct-Revised close behind. Both no-feedback witness sources substantially outperform weakened contracts. The effect does not reproduce in the available Dafny and Lean evaluations, establishing an empirical boundary around the studied Verus/Rust workflow. Completeness auditing and correctness repair have opposing immediate objectives. Preserve and certify the admitted failure before repairing it.

</details>


### [239] [Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields: Evidence-Grounded Selective Safety for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20679)
*Tianshi Wang,Jingsong Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CNRF 提出了一种基于证据的框架，用于选择性的 VLM 安全性。该框架通过构造上下文匹配的有害- benign 对偶，使用它们的表示差异作为与能力相关的风险方向，并分别估计邻域支持和风险，从而实现选择性干预而不是无区别拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于表示的安全防御方法仅学会了将有害输入与良性输入区分开来，但没有解释风险评分的含义或确定何时可以信任这些评分。CNRF 强调了在这些防御模型中融入更多基于证据的支持，以实现更加准确和具有针对性的安全干预。

Method: CNRF 通过构造上下文匹配的有害- benign 对偶，使用它们之间的表示差异来定义与能力相关的风险方向。风险领域支持和风险被分别估计，以避免将不熟悉性与有害性混淆。此外，CNRF 提出了一个针对受害者的自适应界面，使得在不同 VLM 结构下调整风险观测，并能将选定的请求传递给同受害者的响应审查。

Result: 在三个 VLM 和五个跨模态破解家庭中，CNRF 达到了 86.06-89.30% 的宏防御成功率，同时保留了大量良性任务的实用性。受控实验还表明，在低误报率区域中，查询匹配的参考和保留在上下文方向上的对应关系显著提高了风险评估的效率。

Conclusion: CNRF 改变了基于表示的 VLM 安全性方法，从学习有害性区域转向构建为选择性风险决策提供依据的有效证据。

Abstract: Representation-based safety defenses for vision-language models (VLMs) often learn to separate harmful from benign inputs, but separation alone does not establish what a risk score means or when it should be trusted. We introduce Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields (CNRF), an evidence-grounded framework for selective VLM safety. CNRF constructs context-matched benign-harmful counterfactual pairs, uses their representation differences as capability-related risk directions, and estimates neighborhood support separately from risk. This separation prevents unfamiliarity from being conflated with harmfulness and enables selective intervention rather than blanket rejection. A victim-adaptive interface accommodates different safety observations across VLM architectures and routes selected requests for same-victim response review, which either releases acceptable candidates or produces constrained alternatives with reduced harmful capability. Across three VLMs and five multimodal jailbreak families, CNRF achieves 86.06-89.30% macro defense success rate while retaining substantial benign-task utility. Controlled experiments further show that query-matched references and preserved context-direction correspondence materially improve risk ranking in the low-false-positive regime. CNRF thus reframes representation-based VLM safety from learning harmfulness boundaries to constructing applicable evidence for selective risk decisions.

</details>


### [240] [IneqAgent: Agentic Inequality Proving in Lean with Symbolic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20681)
*Gaolei He,Jinhui Li,Ruobing Zuo,Xia Zeng,Zhengfeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IneqAgent 是一个结合了大语言模型推理和符号计算的框架，用于在 Lean4 中证明不等式。该框架通过引理级蒙特卡洛树搜索协调引理构造、目标重构和符号后端选择，利用不同的后台生成精确证书，并与 LLM 生成的步骤集成，通过 Lean 反馈构建完整的证明。IneqAgent 在四个基准测试中解决了175个中的186个问题（94.09%），在低大语言模型推理成本阈值下，其成功率高于 Direct Agent，并且具有较低的平均推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对正形不等式证明所需的高度数学推理和精确计算，需要一种结合大语言模型推理和符号计算的框架来提高证明效率和准确性。

Method: IneqAgent 通过引理级蒙特卡洛树搜索协调引理构造、目标重构和符号后端选择，利用不同的后台生成精确证书，并与 LLM 生成的步骤集成，通过 Lean 反馈构建完整的证明。

Result: IneqAgent 在四个基准测试中解决了175个中的186个问题（94.09%），在低大语言模型推理成本阈值下，其成功率高于 Direct Agent，并且具有较低的平均推理成本。

Conclusion: IneqAgent 是一种有效的自动化不等式证明方法，能够在低成本下提高证明的成功率，并且已经验证了某些数学猜想。

Abstract: Formal inequality proving requires mathematical reasoning and exact computation, often connected through intermediate lemmas and goal reformulations. We introduce IneqAgent, an agentic framework combining LLM reasoning with symbolic computation for inequality proving in Lean4. Lemma-level Monte Carlo Tree Search coordinates lemma construction, goal reformulation, and symbolic backend selection. Complementary sum-of-squares, Bernstein, and successive difference-substitution backends produce exact certificates for local Lean proof generation. The agent integrates these proofs with LLM-generated steps and uses Lean feedback to construct complete proofs checked against the original theorem statements. On four benchmarks, IneqAgent with DeepSeek-V4-Flash solves 175 of 186 problems (94.09%), achieving the highest overall success rate among the evaluated methods. Across four backbones, it achieves higher success rates at low LLM inference cost thresholds than Direct Agent using the same backbone, with lower mean inference costs over each method's successful runs. We also obtain Lean-verified strengthenings of Dittert's conjecture in dimensions four and five, and certify the Holens--Djokovic $(4,4)$ inequality, implying Merris's conjecture in dimension four.

</details>


### [241] [L2G-SR: Learning to Guide LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20685)
*Letian Huang,Xiangyu Wang,Haowei Lin,Di He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: L2G-SR 提出了一种基于大模型（LLM）的符号回归方法，通过学习结构验证器来增强搜索过程，使用树编辑距离（TED）作为监督信号，实现了最先进的数值拟合和公式准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的符号回归方法往往依赖数值误差来指导搜索，但可能会偏向于更准确的代理模型而不是具有正确结构的候选模型。L2G-SR 提出了一种新的框架，利用学习到的结构验证器来改善这一过程。

Method: L2G-SR 使用树编辑距离 (TED) 建立结构性监督信号，调整了通项运算和替代表达形式。验证器首先将数值观测值与公式对齐，然后学习如何评分候选公式与目标公式的结构性相似度。在搜索过程中，目标公式被隐藏，并且验证器在父选择中补充数值误差。分析师利用验证器反馈和诊断工具提出修订，再由生成器将其转化成改进的表达式。

Result: L2G-SR 在 LLM-SRBench 数据集上展示了其优越性，实现了最先进的数值拟合、方程式级别准确性和树编辑距离。

Conclusion: L2G-SR 表明了在基于大模型的符号回归中，学习到的结构指导是有价值的。

Abstract: Symbolic regression searches for an equation that explains observed data. Numerical error is commonly used to guide the search, but it alone can favor an accurate surrogate over a candidate with the right structure. We propose L2G-SR, a framework for LLM-based symbolic regression that guides search with a learned structural verifier. We construct structural supervision signal using tree edit distance (TED), accounting for commutative operators and alternative expression forms. A verifier first aligns numerical observations with the formulas, then learns to score a candidate's structural similarity to the target formula. At search time, the target formula is hidden and the verifier complements numerical error in parent selection. An analyst then uses verifier feedback and diagnostic tools to propose revisions, which a generator converts into refined expressions. On LLM-SRBench, L2G-SR achieves state-of-the-art numerical fit, equation level accuracy and TED across the evaluated LLM backbones and domains. L2G-SR demonstrates the value of learned structural guidance for LLM-based symbolic regression.

</details>


### [242] [When Reality Outpaces Cognition: A New Scaling Principle for Reliable Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20764)
*Ahsan Adeel,Talha Bin Riaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新理论，通过有效连贯认知速度（$S_C^{	ext{eff}}$）来衡量AI在不断扩大的观察-现实差距中可靠执行动作的能力。实验中，$Co^4$架构在提升环境变化速度高达50倍的情况下，仍能保持较高可靠性，优于其他基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文挑战了现有AI范式及新兴世界模型的理解能力，提出通过有效连贯认知速度（$S_C^{	ext{eff}}$）来评估智能系统在不同环境变化速度下可靠感知、推理和执行任务的能力。

Method: 通过计算有效连贯认知速度（$S_C^{	ext{eff}}$），定义为系统在增加的现实变化速度下能持续满足预设行为可靠性的最高测试速度。研究中，基线模型包括Transformer和门控MLP，而$Co^4$架构借鉴了与清醒思考和意识处理相关的细胞机制。

Result: 在不增加计算资源的情况下，提升环境变化速度达到50倍时，$Co^4$架构的表现优于基线模型，有效连贯认知速度（$S_C^{	ext{eff}}$）显著更高，证明了$Co^4$在应对快速变化环境方面的能力。

Conclusion: 研究提出有效连贯认知速度（$S_C^{	ext{eff}}$）作为衡量AI在变化环境中的可靠行为标准的新基准，并强调提升连贯认知的规模作为构建可靠AI的原则。

Abstract: This paper challenges the understanding capabilities of existing AI paradigms and emerging world models by asking a fundamental question: How quickly can reality change before an intelligent system can no longer reliably perceive, reason, and act? We formulate this as a theory- and model-agnostic challenge in which systems must infer reliable actions for increasingly distant future states across a growing observation--reality gap. We operationalize this challenge through the effective Speed of Coherent Cognition (\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\)), defined as the highest tested Speed of Reality (\(S_R\)) through which a system continuously satisfies a predefined behavioural reliability criterion from baseline. As a proof of concept, reinforcement-learning agents are trained at the baseline \(S_R\) and evaluated, without retraining, as \(S_R\) is increased by up to \(50\times\) while computational resources remain fixed. We compare Transformer and gated-MLP baselines with \(Co^4\), an architecture inspired by cellular mechanisms implicated in conscious processing and awake thought. Across the tested scenarios, \(Co^4\) achieves substantially higher \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) than the baselines, motivating \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) as a new benchmark and the scaling of coherent cognition as a principle for reliable AI.

</details>


### [243] [ARIA: Runtime Inspection and Amendment for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20780)
*Bolin Shen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为ARIA的框架，用于学习在多智能体执行过程中何时及如何进行修复，通过收集并构建了一个包含修正对的大型数据集，训练模型以识别何时需要干预及如何进行修正，实验验证了ARIA模型在多个环境中具有良好的修复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于大模型的多智能体系统（MAS）中，局部错误可能导致任务失败，现有通过事后诊断或重复运行来寻求纠正的方法无法提供运行时干预策略，为此需要一个能够学习并实时进行修复的框架。

Method: 研究人员首先构建了一个包含超过24,000个成功和失败轨迹的数据集ARIA，每个修正对描述了错误、介入位置及修正后的执行策略。接着，开发了一个称为ARIA的框架来实现运行时的修复，在此框架指导下，使用验证的修复作为训练示例，训练了一个紧凑模型来识别何时及如何进行干预。

Result: ARIA模型展示了在多种场景下的良好修复性能，并能在新配置中进行泛化。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来的多智能体系统提供了有效的运行时修复方法，并且该方法具有一定的通用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.

</details>


### [244] [Open-world AI: a Tale of Three Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20782)
*Jianyu Zhang,Léon Bottou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对比了闭世界和开世界下的AI需求，提出闭世界要求AI具备专门技能并收集独立同分布的数据，而开世界则要求AI具备泛化能力和适应未知任务的能力。文中通过语言模型试验验证了需要丰富的表示、预测性解纠缠、一致的内学习者和长上下文这些要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI在闭世界与开世界中的需求差异，以及开发适用于开世界的AI模型所需的关键特性。

Method: 通过对语言模型进行不同数据量和特定需求下的测试，验证理论预测的有效性。

Result: 满足文中提出的特性的模型在学习相关任务的数据累积时表现更好；而未满足这些特性的模型则表现较差；在尝试通过增加层和上下文长度来修补模型时没有改善效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，闭世界和开世界对AI的需求各有侧重，设计开世界AI模型需要考虑丰富的表示、预测性解纠缠、一致内学习者和长上下文等关键特性，改进模型架构也可能需要特定的调整策略。

Abstract: The closed world asks AI for specialized competence and lets it manufacture the iid data its theory assumes: a fixed criterion licenses collecting examples until the task is covered. The open world asks for versatility --- taking up tasks a machine was not built for, from few examples and with little prior from a human designer --- and offers data that is grouped, not shuffled. Its tasks are not known in advance, so a machine must do inference-time learning (1st requirement). Modeling tasks as sharing latent factors and decomposing the training objective, we derive two more: a *rich representation*, retaining more than iid generalization requires, and a predictive disentanglement that aligns inputs and outputs into matched low-dimensional groups. The analysis further predicts the need for a consistent inner learner, and the benefit of long contexts spanning related tasks. We test these claims on language models up to 8B, asking whether a model has a property (qualitative) rather than how much it wins (quantitative). Models that meet the requirements improve as data from related tasks accumulates; the rest degrade, and neither scale nor layer-type interleaving repairs this.

</details>


### [245] [EvolvingBrain: An Adaptive Skill System for Multi-modal Agents Self-Evolution in Open Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20786)
*Runxuan Liu,Jingqi Shi,Baoxin Wang,Honghong Zhao,Haoyuan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Jiafeng Liang,Tao He,Dayong Wu,Qi Gu,Shijin Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvolvingBrain利用自适应技能系统和状态感知执行策略，通过从特定模型轨迹中提取程序性模式和问题模式，实现技能自进化，形成分层技能架构，并利用结构抽象记忆来指导技能融合策略，从而提高开放世界任务的成功率并展示泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多模态代理在与开放世界的互动中虽然取得了显著进步，但仍受限于自我进化能力的不足。现有方法在模仿学习上有效但缺乏主动经验组织，在基于结果的强化学习探索上有效但中间轨迹信息利用不足。因此，需要一种能够有效利用经验和中间轨迹信息进行自我进化的方法。

Method: EvolvingBrain通过引入分层技能自进化和状态感知适应性执行两个组成部分。分层技能自进化从模型特定的轨迹中提取程序性和问题性模式，生成技能并多次与开放世界交互，形成分层技能架构，提供多粒度知识库和创新性自我进化策略。状态感知适应性执行则将长时相互作用总结为结构化抽象记忆，并根据当前状态引领技能融合策略。

Result: 在VAB-Minecraft和DiscoveryWorld实验中，EvolvingBrain提高了开放世界任务的成功率，并展示了泛化能力。

Conclusion: EvolvingBrain提供了一种新的代理自我进化方法，通过分层技能架构和状态感知执行策略显著增强了代理在复杂开放世界环境中的表现能力。

Abstract: Multi-modal agents have made significant progress in interacting with open worlds. However, their ability to self-evolve remains limited. Supervised fine-tuning enables imitation but not active experience organization, while outcome-based reinforcement learning promotes exploration yet underuses intermediate trajectory information. Therefore, we propose EvolvingBrain, which uses an adaptive skill system as the brain for agent self-evolution, consisting of two following components. Stratified Skill Self-Evolution: extracts Procedural Patterns and Problematic Patterns from model-specific trajectories, summarizes experience by generating skills, and evolves through multiple interactions with the open world, ultimately forming a Stratified Skill Architecture consisting of Domain, Plan, and Action/Trap Skills, which provides the agent with a multi-granularity knowledge repository and a novel evolution approach. State-Aware Adaptive Execution: summarizes long-horizon interactions into Structured Abstractive Memory and uses it to guide the State-Aware Skill Fusion Strategy, enabling the agent to utilize knowledge according to its current state. Experiments on VAB-Minecraft and DiscoveryWorld demonstrate that EvolvingBrain improves success rates on open-world tasks and exhibits generalization capabilities. Our code is provided in the supplementary materials and will be publicly released in the camera-ready version.

</details>


### [246] [When Reasoning Traces Conflict with Evidence: How Answer Configuration Shapes Evidence Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20805)
*Shaoang Li,Yanhang Shi,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现了推理痕迹在保持准确性和与当前证据竞争中的作用。通过分离调用和持续模型思考的配置，研究展示了这种分离调用可以更频繁地遵循当前证据。这表明受益于推理痕迹和被告之遵循它们是两个不同的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是为了深入了解推理痕迹在保持准确性与与当前证据竞争中的不同作用，以及它们带来的影响。

Method: 研究采用了三种不同的模型系列（gpt-oss，Gemma4，Qwen3），通过将验证过的证据版本与从每个生成的推理痕迹进行交叉，以两种配置方式（模型继续思考与分离回答调用）回答问题。研究对比了这两种配置方式在HotpotQA和MuSiQue数据集上的表现。

Result: 研究结果表明，分离调用配置提升了证据遵循率，相较于模型继续思考，其提升幅度为29.9到60.6个百分点。删除推理痕迹会导致准确性显著下降，但在大多数冲突情况下，分离调用会更加依赖当前证据，而不是重复过时的结论。验证式指令不同于重新进行推理，后者更倾向于遵循当前证据。此外，推理痕迹的最后一部分对其价值贡献最大，而黄金证据在写入前后的作用不同，误导信息削弱了在分离调用中的纠正效果。

Conclusion: 综上所述，推理痕迹和被告之遵循它们是两个不同的特性。为了更准确地评估模型的答案，应该同时报告这两方面特性及其对应的答案配置信息。

Abstract: A reasoning trace retained at answer time plays two roles: it can raise accuracy, and it can compete with the current evidence when the two disagree. We disentangle these roles by crossing verified evidence versions with the traces generated from each and answering from the same traces and evidence under two configurations: continuation of the model's own thinking and a separate answer call. Across three model families (gpt-oss, Gemma4, Qwen3) on HotpotQA and MuSiQue, the separate call raises evidence-following over continuation by 29.9 to 60.6 points. On the same pairs, removing the trace lowers accuracy by similar amounts under both configurations. Yet continuation repeats the stale conclusion in most conflicts, and the separate call follows the current evidence far more often. Benefiting from a trace and deferring to it are therefore distinct properties. Further analyses show that the final line of a trace carries most of its value, gold evidence helps most before the trace is written, distractors weaken correction under a separate call, and renewed reasoning, unlike a verification instruction, follows the current evidence in most conflicts. Because trace deletion alone cannot reveal which source an answer follows, evaluations should report both properties together with the answer configuration.

</details>


### [247] [A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20809)
*Yun-Shiuan Chuang,Yirou Ge,Yixi Li,Yiming Bian,Zijie Pan,Jenna L. Ballard,Uma Kona,Linsey Pang,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一种自改进代理，它通过两个嵌套循环来扩展其计算能力，无需奖励、评判者或答案键，能够生成正确且独特的操作符，在多个领域显示出显著的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自改进系统依赖于奖励、评判者或答案键来引导学习过程，但这些外部激励可能在需要精确答案的任务中不可靠。本文探索了在无奖励、无评判者、无答案的情况下，自改进代理能否成长为具有精确计算能力的系统。

Method: 通过实现一个单一的冻结模型，运行两个嵌套的循环。内层循环在两个独立编写的表达式完全一致时回答新问题。外层循环发现缺失的能力并为其编写操作符，需经独立验证且符合守恒定律而不依赖于真实数据。内层循环约增加三倍可回答的问题集，而外层循环在不相关的领域自扩展。

Result: 内层循环有效增加了可回答的问题集，外层循环独立地扩展了模型的计算能力，未经过训练的控制组无法回答这些问题。引入的验证规则在不同模型家族中均保持有效。

Conclusion: 该发现表明，代理可以通过自改进机制扩展其计算能力，并在没有外部激励的情况下生成稳定的准确结果，这具有重要的理论和实际应用价值。

Abstract: A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.

</details>


### [248] [Temporal Specification Guidance for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20822)
*Avishree Khare,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Rajeev Alur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的无需训练的时间推理框架TSG，它将时间约束转化为生成视频时的引导目标，并通过差异可微分的时间逻辑评分，指导视频生成以满足时间要求。该框架在五个模型上实现了9.5-20个百分点的性能提升，展示了时间约束和可微分满足目标在控制视频生成方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的文本到视频模型在生成视觉概念方面表现良好，但在顺序、时间和持续性需求方面缺乏准确性。本文旨在通过将时间要求表示为形式时间逻辑的规范，并引入TSG框架来解决这一问题，该框架能够实时指导视频生成，无需额外的训练步骤。

Method: 该研究将时间逻辑规范转化为生成目标，并提出了TSG框架。TSG通过差异可微分的评分机制给生成过程提供一个去噪方向，从而实现了根据时间逻辑规范的视频生成。

Result: 在包含11类时间约束的220个任务基准上，TSG框架在五个模型上实现了9.5-20个百分点的性能提升，同时保持了较低的内存消耗。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了时间规范和可微分满足目标作为控制视频生成的一种灵活接口的潜力。

Abstract: Text-to-video models often generate the visual concepts requested in natural language prompts but fail to satisfy their intended ordering, timing, and persistence requirements. We propose representing these requirements as specifications in a formal Temporal Logic, and introduce Temporal Specification Guidance (TSG), a training-free inference-time framework that turns temporal specifications into executable objectives for guiding video generation during sampling. TSG scores traces of visual concept evidence using differentiable quantitative semantics and uses the score's gradients to construct a specification-guided denoising direction. We instantiate this framework with video-text attention traces, efficient differentiable scores for Metric Temporal Logic, and gradient-derived attention biases. This instantiation requires neither an external concept classifier nor backpropagation through the video transformer. Its attention-steered forward pass yields a denoising direction that augments classifier-free guidance (CFG). On a benchmark of 220 prompts spanning 11 categories of temporal constraints, including ordered object entry and state transitions, this instantiation improves specification satisfaction accuracy over CFG by 9.5-20 percentage points across five models, with minimal memory overhead. These results demonstrate the potential of temporal specifications and differentiable satisfaction objectives as a flexible interface for controlling video generation.

</details>


### [249] [Confidence-Calibrated Inference Expansion for Evaluator-Guided Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20836)
*Weida Liang,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种计算感知的控制器，通过评估预测答案的成功和扩展的延续成功来选择局部行动，同时显式定价计算成本。实验表明，与固定策略相比，该控制器在保持或提高性能的同时，减少了约30%和16%的在线计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的评价器导向推理方法在每一步需要做出选择：信任当前答案、返回备选方案或进行更多推理。这种选择是一个局部控制问题，需要一种能够准确定价计算成本的控制器来优化决策。

Method: 该研究通过定义一个计算感知的三行动控制器，结合 rollout 基础的延续成功替代方法来评分终端行动。计算成本被明确定价。同时，进行局部行动差距分析以确定评分错误如何影响决策，并通过期望遗憾边界来考虑沿实现扩展路径的错误。最后，基于这些分析结果，学习并校准特定于行动的置信度估计，并为模棱两可的决策添加本地不确定性惩罚。

Result: 在宽度扩展和修订任务中，此控制器的观察到的实用性优于固定选择的策略，同时减少了约30%和16%的正常在线计算量。不同 Qwen3-8B 种子的计算量低于拟合Q，而实用性差异较小且不一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明，评价器导向推理可以被看作是一种资源分配下的不确定性，而不是一个更强大的推理者。同时，计算感知的控制器能够在降低计算成本的同时，保持甚至提升性能。

Abstract: Evaluator-guided reasoning presents a local control problem at every step: trust the current answer, return a fallback, or spend more inference. We formulate this choice as a compute-aware three-action controller, scoring terminal actions by predicted answer success and expansion by a rollout-based continuation-success surrogate, with compute cost priced explicitly. A local action-gap analysis characterizes when score errors can change a decision, while an expected regret bound accounts for errors along the realized expansion path. Guided by this analysis, we learn and calibrate action-specific confidence estimates, with local uncertainty penalties for ambiguous decisions. On held-out width expansion and revision tasks, the controller improves observed utility over calibration-selected fixed policies while reducing normalized online compute by about 30% and 16%, respectively. Across four Qwen3-8B seeds, compute is lower than fitted Q in both settings, while utility differences are small and mixed. The results frame evaluator-guided reasoning as resource allocation under uncertainty, rather than a uniformly stronger reasoner.

</details>


### [250] [Evolving Together, Failing Apart: The Skill-Policy Co-evolution Trap in Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20845)
*Yujie Feng,Xiaobin Wang,Jinluan Yang,Xiaonan Shi,Pengjun Xie,Philip S. Yu,Xiaoming Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实证分析，揭示了现有技能和政策共同进化的脆弱性，并提出了一种称为CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA)的部署导向评估协议，以模拟技能漂移，引入了新的评估指标，使得未来的方法可以进行审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的技能和政策共同进化评估方法在技能银行漂移时存在脆弱性，没有考虑技能的频繁更改，使得政策在运行时面临技能库变更的风险。

Method: 作者首先通过理论分析和实证研究展示了共同进化的脆弱性，并引入了名为CODA的评估协议，其中包含了模拟技能漂移下的成功率、语义保持编辑下的一致性率和对抗破坏性技能的抵抗率这三个新指标。

Result: CODA评估协议通过四个共同进化方法和五个基准（六个环境）测试发现，最坏情况的成功率比与共同进化技能银行的最佳测量值低5-13个点，这在很大程度上减少了共同进化技能银行相对于无技能基线的增益。

Conclusion: 本文提出了CODA评估协议作为共进化技能策略评估的新标准，未来的方法可以通过该协议进行审核，强调了对技能漂移的适应能力的重要性。

Abstract: Skill-policy co-evolution, in which an agent's policy model and its skill bank improve each other during training, is a promising paradigm for self-improving agents. Current evaluations score the co-evolved policy on the final bank it trained with (the delivered bank). After deployment, however, skills may be rewritten, added or removed more frequently than the policy model is retrained, leaving the policy to operate on a drifted skill bank that neither the training objective nor existing evaluation protocols address. In this paper, we first establish, through theoretical and empirical analysis, that the fragility hidden by this blind spot is not incidental. The acceptance rule these methods share (cooperative acceptance) ties a policy's competence to the bank it co-evolved with, leaving it fragile once skills drift, regardless of the implementation; empirically, sensitivity to skill drift grows as co-evolution training proceeds. We therefore introduce the CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol that simulates skill drift and, alongside success rate under worst-case drift, introduces two new metrics: a consistency rate under meaning-preserving edits and a resistance rate to corrupted skills. Across four co-evolution methods and five benchmarks (six settings), worst-case success is 5-13 points below the peak measured for each delivered pair, erasing 52-93% of the delivered bank's gain over the no-skill baseline. CODA thus reframes the target of co-evolution, from peak performance on the co-evolved bank to the ability to harness skills however they evolve, and provides a shared standard against which future methods can be audited.

</details>


### [251] [Anticipatory Commonsense with Moving Objects: An ASP-Based Framework for Predictive Reasoning with Interacting Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20867)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种通用的声明性框架，用于预测复杂动态环境中的交互式运动轨迹，通过语义导向的预测和能量优化模型实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决复杂动态环境中交互式运动轨迹预测的问题，提供一种具有可解释性和启发性的方法。

Method: 该方法利用了基于ASP的运动轨迹动力学模型、语义导向的预测模型，以及能量优化模型来评估、排序和筛选预测。

Result: 该方法在Argoverse 2基准测试中进行了评估，每个预测轨迹都包含明确且可检验的事件级假设。

Conclusion: 这种方法证明了其在复杂动态环境中的应用场景，并为低级运动计算提供了有效的指导手段。

Abstract: We present a general declarative framework for anticipatory reasoning about interactive motion in complex dynamic environments. Our focus is on (explainable) anticipatory space-time mental model formation at a semantic level to heuristically guide low-level motion computation. Our method is hybrid, modular and elaboration tolerant integrating: 1) An ASP model characterising the dynamics of interactive motion trajectories; 2) Semantically-guided prediction for deriving `branching trajectory futures'; and 3) An energy-based {optimisation} model to evaluate, rank and filter anticipations. We evaluate with the Argoverse 2 benchmark, where each anticipated trajectory carries an explicit, inspectable event-level hypothesis, unlike end-to-end stochastic prediction.

</details>


### [252] [A Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM): Proportionate Access and Capability Limits for Comprehensive Frontier AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20921)
*Chai K. Toh,Vasu Raj Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多层级AI可访问模型（MAIAM），将访问分为学习级、探索级和全尺度前沿AI三个层级，依据能力、安全性和风险程度来决定访问权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关于发布强大AI系统的选择通常被简化为只保持在API后面，或者发布权重的二元框架混淆了两个独立的决策：系统的潜在能力以及获得特定能力的条件。文章基于安全性和风险程度建议了一个多层级访问框架。

Method: 文章提出了一个新的多层级访问模型，并通过对比公开前沿级模型和安全微调后模型的成本，强调了公开模型可能会带来的安全风险。

Result: 研究成果表明，公开前沿级模型可能会失去所有在发布之后可用的安全手段。此外，基模的评估并不能反映其衍生模型的安全性。

Conclusion: 文章提出了一个新的多层级访问模型，并基于安全性和风险程度，建议采用分级访问体系和监管机构管制相结合的方法来确保AI系统的安全性。

Abstract: Most debate about releasing capable AI systems treats the choice as binary: keep the model behind an API, or publish the weights. That framing runs together two separate decisions. One is how capable a system may become. The other is who may obtain a given capability, and on what terms. This paper is about the second decision. We propose a Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM) that sorts access by purpose, safety, and capability instead of by release artifact. Tier 1, AI for Learning, is broadly accessible and deliberately capability-bounded. Tier 2, AI for Exploration, serves research and development under registration, identity, and usage controls that tighten as capability rises. Tier 3, Full-Scale Frontier AI, is available only through structured, recorded, and accountable access. The principle behind the tiers is that accessibility should be proportional to capability, safety, and risk. We show why publishing frontier-class weights forfeits every safety lever a developer has after release, and, using evidence that safety fine-tuning can be stripped from open weights for a few hundred dollars, why an evaluation of the base model says little about its derivatives. We give criteria for assigning a system to a tier along capability, autonomy, domain-consequence, and usage/safety axes, and combine MAIAM with the SAFE AI Operating Point into a two-part framework: a Capability Boundary that fixes the safety limits not to be crossed, and an Accessibility Boundary that fixes who may obtain a capability below that limit. Capability limits alone do not make AI safe; a tiered access regime administered by regulating bodies is also needed.

</details>


### [253] [Defining and Generating Compositional Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20922)
*Eric Elmoznino,Tejas Kasetty,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,Yoshua Bengio,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过算法信息论的概念，重新定义了组合结构。通过这种方法，识别并生成了具有明显层次复用和部分整体组织的结构，这表明定义能够捕捉组合结构而不仅仅是复杂性、规律性或共享信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数学描述组合结构的方法有限，本文提供了更加全面和形式化的定义，旨在更好地理解组合结构在自然界和人工系统中的重要性。

Method: 本文利用算法信息论，提出了通过信息和压缩来识别任意数据中的结构的方法，进而定义了组合结构的概念，并通过优化字符串和图像直接生成具有组合结构的对象。

Result: 通过算法信息论的应用，定义了物体中的组合结构，生成了具有明显层次复用和部分整体组织的对象，这表明所定义的概念能够更好地捕捉组合结构。

Conclusion: 本文的研究结论为组合结构的研究提供了新的视角，并为机器学习中组合结构数据课程的设计提供了原则，支持开放学习。

Abstract: Compositional structure is pervasive in nature and across scales: atoms combine into molecules, molecules into cells, and cells into organisms, while further compositional hierarchies appear within each level. More broadly, compositionality, the reuse and recombination of parts into novel structures, is a fundamental source of interesting complexity in both natural and artificial systems. Yet despite its importance, and despite our apparent ease in recognizing it, mathematical accounts capturing what we intuitively mean by compositional structure remain limited. Here, we fill this gap by formalizing a simple idea: compositional structure emerges when parts, considered together, reveal shared structure that is not captured when those parts are modeled separately. We express this idea using algorithmic information theory, the mathematics of information and compression, which provides a natural way to identify structure in arbitrary data without specifying in advance what that structure must look like. Applied across scales, our definition informs where and how compositional structure emerges within an object. Using tractable approximations, we then invert the framework to generate compositional objects: we specify the desired form of their compositional structure without prescribing their particular content, and optimize directly over strings and images. The resulting objects exhibit readily recognizable hierarchical reuse and part-whole organization, suggesting that the definition captures compositional structure rather than merely complexity, regularity, or shared information. Finally, we discuss implications for machine learning, including a new perspective on depth in neural architectures and principles for constructing compositional data curricula capable of supporting open-ended learning.

</details>


### [254] [SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20926)
*Enze Ma,Chunyu Miao,Yankai Chen,Jie Yang,Liancheng Fang,Kening Zheng,Hanrong Zhang,Muyao Xu,Xinle Yu,Chengze Li,Wei-Chieh Huang,Ziwei Ren,Yunhao Du,Zhixiang Shen,Xue Liu,Philip S. Yu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillNEO通过分别选择使用版本和需要进一步优化的版本，在多个领域中提升了模型的表现，特别是在软件工程和办公室工作中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的技能优化方法可能过于追求即时收益，而忽视了尚未展现潜力的版本。 SkillNEO通过非精英进化优化方法解决了这个问题，帮助构建更有效的代理技能。

Method: 技能NEO(SkillNEO)通过区分选取用于执行任务的版本和需要改进的版本来提高技能。这种方法评估相关版本在同一任务中的表现，以识别值得保存的操作步骤和需要修复的失败环节。此外，它可以利用之后获得的信息来回顾和修订早期版本。

Result: SkillNEO在86个SkillBench任务上，将GPT-5.4 Mini的平均奖励从49.1%提升到66.4%，这与GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6 Luna的原有技能范围相符。在41个更改技能的任务中，重用改进了Luna 10.5个百分点，并将GPT-5.5提升了10.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了技能演化能够将较弱模型的部分进展转化为更强大代理可以复用的程序性知识，从而提高它们的性能。

Abstract: Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini's average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna's original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model's partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.

</details>


### [255] [RAC: A Relational Agent Contract for Constraint-Governed Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20935)
*Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Zubin Aaron Assadian,Yuetai Li,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAC（关系约定）为工程代理的动作和反馈层引入了一个通用合同，确保精确的观察、语义操作、工具化的实现、独立验证和修复证据。与现有方法相比，RAC在多个工程任务中提高了通过率，特别是在电路板制作、车间调度和嵌入式代码任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决方案要求代理进行语义决策并推导出结果，这可能导致工程约束下的任务失败。RAC通过将代理的语义控制与工具化的机械推导分离，提高了约束下 artifact 的可靠性。

Method: RAC 设定了权威观察、语义行动、基于工具的实现、独立验证和修复证据。代理决定 artifact 应满足的关系，而专门的工具推导具体的后果。

Result: RAC 在 PCB 布局、路由和修复任务中实现了最高的无错误通过率，为所有九个评估模型的 90.9%，比最强基线（仅依赖需求）高出 16.9%。在车间调度中，直接关系行动的质量通过率高达 96.3%，而代码介导的交付只有 59.6%。在 141 个嵌入式代码任务中，RAC 证实了动作合同作为一个可操作的代理能力来源。

Conclusion: RAC 通过将语义控制与机械型输出分离提高了约束约束下 artifact 的可靠性，并且该方法显示出在其他工程任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Engineering agents must produce artifacts that satisfy exact, coupled constraints. Existing solutions often require agents to make semantic decisions and also derive consequences such as precise geometry or timing from those decisions, leading to failures in constraint-governed engineering tasks. In this paper, we introduce RAC, a harness-agnostic contract for the action and feedback layer of engineering agents. It specifies authoritative observations, semantic actions, tool-based realization, independent verification, and repair evidence. The agent decides which relations an artifact should satisfy; specialized tools derive their concrete consequences. On PCB placement, routing, and repair tasks, RAC achieves the highest clean-pass rate for all nine evaluated models, averaging 90.9%, compared with 74.0% for the strongest baseline, requirements-only delegation. In job-shop scheduling, direct relational actions achieve 96.3% quality pass versus 59.6% for code-mediated delivery with access to the same scheduling backend. Our evaluations on 141 embedded-code tasks across four microcontroller identify the action contract as a source of agent capability: preserving semantic control while delegating mechanically derivable outputs improves the reliability of constrained artifacts.

</details>


### [256] [MAI-Gym: An Open All-in-One Platform for GUI Agent Evaluation and RL Training across Mobile and Computer Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20939)
*Panrong Tong,Quyu Kong,Pengxiang Li,Tianyu Xia,Hanzhang Zhou,Lei Wang,Chenglin Cai,Long Li,Long Chen,Jianan Zhang,Zhengwen Zeng,Xu Zhang,Gongjie Zhang,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAI-Gym 是一个全面的开源平台，旨在统一移动、计算机和网络环境下的 GUI 代理评估、数据收集及在线强化学习训练。它包含了一个协变展开流水线，支持多环境实例并能自动生成各种 GUI 应用程序和具有执行验证的任务。利用 MAI-Gym，作者训练了一个达到最新成果的9B参数统一代理，并展示了其跨模拟器与实际应用的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，成功地将在线强化学习扩展到GUI代理中面临挑战，主要由于交互环境的成本高昂和可供训练的真实任务的稀缺。MAI-Gym的引入旨在简化这一过程，通过提供一个统一的平台来更高效地进行评估，数据收集和训练。

Method: MAI-Gym 通过一个共享的展开流水线确保一致的行为，支持十种标准基准，能扩展到20,000个并行环境实例。还可以自动生成各种GUI应用程序和具有执行验证的任务。

Result: 利用 MAI-Gym，作者训练了一个统一的9B代理，成功地在MobileWorld和OSWorld-Verified基准测试上分别达到了78.4%和76.2%的最新成果。另外，从模拟火车票预订应用程序训练出来的代理在现实设备上的实际任务成功率也有了显著提升，由28.8%提高至48.1%。

Conclusion: MAI-Gym 放弃了该模型检查点、合成技能、模拟应用程序以及验证任务等一系列资源，促进了可复现研究和在可扩展GUI代理训练方面的进一步进展。
